TL;DR: 本文提出了MemeBridge数据集，专门针对美国起源的表情包，从中国参与者对表情包的理解和美国参与者对跨文化误解的预期两个互补视角进行标注，揭示了文化理解的不对称性，并证明基于该数据集的微调能提升LLM的跨文化理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨文化交流，特别是通过表情包这类文化密集且模糊的格式进行交流时，本身具有巨大挑战。虽然人们期望LLM能帮助弥合文化差距，但现有基准数据集往往缺少准确解读所需的深层文化语境，因此需要构建能够体现上下文（隐性文化知识，如背景信念、规范、共享假设）的数据资源。

Method: 构建MemeBridge数据集：通过多阶段众包流程进行严格验证（包括人工一致性检查和基于GPT的分类验证）；每张表情包标注情绪、情感、文化重要性和知识类型；采用双向框架，同时收集中国参与者的解读和美国参与者对其他文化可能误解的预测。

Result: 发现美国参与者预期的误解往往不准确，凸显了文化理解的不对称性和超越自身视角的挑战。对多个LLM的探针测试表明，在不同文化背景开发的模型虽有部分跨文化理解能力，但常在精细解读方面存在困难；使用MemeBridge微调后模型性能得到提升。

Conclusion: 双向框架（同时关注表达与感知）能够实现更细粒度的跨文化理解基准测试；文化接地资源对于在全球多样化环境中训练和评估LLM具有重要价值。

Abstract: Communicating across cultures is inherently challenging, especially through culturally dense and ambiguous formats like memes. While people expect large language models (LLMs) to hold promise for bridging such gaps, existing benchmark datasets often fail to capture the cultural context necessary for accurate interpretation. To address this, we introduce MemeBridge, a curated dataset centered on U.S.-originated memes, designed to capture two complementary perspectives: (1) how Chinese participants interpret these memes, and (2) how U.S. participants anticipate how people from other cultures might misunderstand them. Here, context refers to implicit cultural knowledge, including background beliefs, norms, and shared assumptions that shape meme comprehension. The dataset was constructed via a multi-stage crowdsourcing pipeline with rigorous validation, including human agreement checks and GPT-based classification verification. Each meme is annotated with sentiment, emotion, cultural significance, and knowledge type, providing rich supervision for downstream tasks. Notably, we observe that the anticipated misunderstandings from U.S. participants are often inaccurate, highlighting the asymmetries in cultural understanding and the challenges of adopting perspectives beyond one's own. This bidirectional framing, which focuses on both expression and perception, enables more nuanced benchmarking of cross-cultural comprehension. Our probing of multiple LLMs reveals that while models developed in different cultural contexts exhibit partial cross-cultural understanding, they often struggle with sophisticated interpretations. By contrast, fine-tuning with MemeBridge improves model performance, underscoring the value of culturally grounded resources for training and evaluating LLMs in globally diverse settings.

</details>


### [106] [Human-Anchored Factuality Evaluation with Strategic Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00494)
*Yu Wang,Craig Erickson,Kevin Small*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限标注预算下的人类锚定事实性评估问题，提出基于失败空间分析（FSA）的标注策略设计流程，用于模型人类法官与人类判断不一致的样本以提升标注效率。在AutoFA和RAGTruth两个数据集上，该方法相比均匀采样和不确定性驱动基线，有效样本量分别提升40.3%和27.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的事实性评判器虽然能提供可扩展的评估信号，但其度量结果相对人类判断存在系统性偏差。现有方法主要依赖低置信度来选择标注样本，但事实性评估中评判器与人类判断的不一致并非仅由低置信度驱动，还源于证据不完整、时间错配、不可验证主张及规则错配等结构化失败模式。因此需要在有限标注预算下设计更高效的样本选择策略。

Method: 提出一个事实性特化的标注策略设计流程，核心采用失败空间分析（Failure-Space Analysis, FSA），通过对失败模式的结构化归类派生多样化的预测信号，用于建模人类评判与LLM评判器之间的不一致性。该流程将全数据集的评判器预测与少量选择性采样的人类标注结合，以获得统计有效的估计。方法在AutoFA（内部参考型事实性评估系统）和RAGTruth上进行验证。

Result: 在AutoFA和RAGTruth两个基准上，相较于均匀采样和不确定性驱动的基线方法，FSA引导的标注策略在有效样本量上分别实现了40.3%和27.1%的提升。特别是在评判器预测显著低估人类标注的事实准确率的场景下，该方法的优势更为明显。

Conclusion: 通过结构化分析评判器失败模式而非单纯依赖置信度，可以显著提升基于人类锚定的事实性评估的样本效率和统计有效性。研究展示了将领域先验（失败模式分类）融入采样策略设计对缓解评判器偏差的有效性。

Abstract: LLM-based factuality judges provide scalable evaluation signals, but their metrics are often systematically biased relative to human judgments. We study human-anchored factuality evaluation under limited annotation budgets, where judge predictions on the full dataset are combined with human labels on a small selectively sampled subset to obtain statistically valid estimates. The efficiency of this approach depends critically on which examples receive human annotation: in factuality evaluation, judge-human misalignment is not driven solely by low confidence, but also by structured failure modes such as incomplete evidence, temporal mismatch, unverifiable claims, and rubric misalignment. To exploit this structure, we introduce a factuality-specific annotation policy design pipeline that uses failure-space analysis (FSA) to derive diverse predictive signals for modeling human-judge misalignment. On an internal reference-based factuality evaluation system (AutoFA) and RAGTruth, where judge-predicted estimates substantially underestimate human-annotated factual accuracy, our FSA-guided policy improves annotation efficiency over uniform sampling and uncertainty-driven baselines, achieving effective-sample-size gains of 40.3% on AutoFA and 27.1% on RAGTruth.

</details>


### [107] [Membership Inference in Fine-tuned Diffusion Language Models via Token-level Memorization Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00873)
*Shengfang Zhai,Leo Marchyok,Yuling Shi,Huanran Chen,Yinpeng Dong,Jiaheng Zhang,Sanghyun Hong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了扩散语言模型（DLMs）的隐私风险，通过理论分析发现"token级记忆不对称"现象，并提出基于分位数加权偏度的Q-Skew指标用于微调DLM的成员推断攻击，实验证明其优于现有基线方法，还能辅助PII提取等隐私攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散语言模型作为自回归语言模型的替代范式日益受到关注，但其隐私风险研究严重不足。已有研究大多聚焦DLM的生成能力，缺乏对其记忆和隐私泄露的系统性分析。

Method: 首先通过理论分析扩散训练动力学，识别出token级记忆不对称现象；然后基于此发现，提出Q-Skew——一种基于分位数加权偏度的成员推断指标，用于检测微调后的扩散语言模型中的成员数据。

Result: 在多个微调数据集和模型上的实验表明，Q-Skew在成员推断任务上优于现有基线方法；此外，Q-Skew还能进一步辅助PII提取等其他隐私泄露攻击。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了此前未被充分探索的DLM隐私攻击面，强调了对扩散语言模型进行系统性隐私评估的必要性。

Abstract: Diffusion language models (DLMs) have recently emerged as an alternative modeling paradigm to autoregressive LMs, offering advantages such as parallel generation and bidirectional context modeling. Despite growing interest in their generative capabilities, the privacy risks of DLMs remain underexplored. We identify a phenomenon termed token-level memorization asymmetry through theoretical analysis of diffusion training dynamics. Building on this finding, we propose Q-Skew, a quantile-weighted skewness-based indicator for membership inference on finetuned DLMs. Experiments across multiple fine-tuning datasets and models show that our method outperforms existing baselines. Moreover, we show that Q-Skew can also facilitate other privacy violations, such as PII extraction. Our findings reveal a previously underexplored privacy attack surface and highlight the need for systematic privacy evaluation of DLMs.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond Token Positions: Safety Alignment Across Denoising Steps in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00495)
*Guoli Wang,Haonan Shi,Tu Ouyang,An Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散大语言模型（dLLM）的安全对齐问题，发现拒绝信号集中在去噪早期步骤和响应开头位置，并提出了一种无需训练的解码方法RAEC，通过在早期步骤提交持久的拒绝信号来降低有害提示的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型采用迭代去噪而非从左到右的解码方式生成文本，这种生成范式引入了两个影响安全对齐的维度：token在去噪过程中生成的时机以及其在响应中出现的位置。作者希望测量和理解dLLM在有害提示下的安全行为，以发现潜在的安全风险并提出改进方案。

Method: 作者通过追踪中间token分布和去噪过程中的提交决策来测量dLLM的安全行为。基于测量发现，提出Refusal-Aware Early Commitment (RAEC)，一种简单的无需训练的解码方法，在早期步骤提交持久的拒绝信号。

Result: 在LLaDA和Dream两个模型上的实验表明，RAEC能够降低攻击成功率，同时很大程度上保持实用性能（utility）。

Conclusion: 去噪步骤和拒绝token提交的持久性是理解dLLM安全性的重要因素；通过早期提交拒绝信号可以有效提升dLLM在有害提示下的安全性，且无需额外的训练。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) generate text through iterative denoising rather than left-to-right decoding. This generation paradigm introduces two axes that can influence safety alignment: when tokens are generated during denoising and where they appear in the response. In this paper, we measure dLLM safety behavior under harmful prompts by tracing intermediate token distributions and commitment decisions throughout denoising. Our analysis shows that refusal signals are concentrated in early denoising steps and leading response positions, and the tokens committed early can strongly shape the final safety outcome. Our measurements further show that the denoising step and persistence of refusal-token commitment are important for understanding dLLM safety. Based on these findings, we propose Refusal-Aware Early Commitment (RAEC), a simple training-free decoding method that commits persistent refusal signals from early steps. Experiments on LLaDA and Dream show that RAEC reduces attack success rates while largely preserving utility. The code is available at https://github.com/Glresearch1/RAEC.

</details>


### [109] [The Interlingua Hypothesis: LLMs Translate via a Latent Task-agnostic Feature Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00515)
*Jacob Brinton,Jannik Brinkmann,Mark Crovella,Aaron Mueller*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了"中间语言假说"（interlingua hypothesis），认为LLM进行机器翻译时，并非在语言对间直接映射，而是先将源语言句子编码为潜在的多语言特征空间，再从该空间生成目标语言句子。作者通过三条证据线支持该假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM展现了超越强监督基线的机器翻译性能，但对其翻译背后的机制缺乏理解。近期可解释性研究发现LLM使用大规模多语言潜在特征表示进行语言建模，这促使作者探究是否存在一个"中间语言"式的翻译机制。

Method: 提出interlingua假说，并通过三条证据线验证：（1）BLEU得分的方差在不同语言对间可主要由各语言自身能力预测，无需语言对特定的交互项；（2）许多模型组件在单语任务和翻译任务中都具有因果影响力；（3）在单语数据上进行微调能够恢复与在平行数据上微调获得的翻译改进的大部分。

Result: 三条证据线共同支持中间语言假说，表明LLM翻译确实通过共享的潜在多语言特征空间进行，而非依赖语言对特有的映射机制。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM翻译机制的收敛性证据，支持中间语言假说，并为理解和改进基于LLM的翻译任务提供了新视角和方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated improved machine translation performance over strong supervised baselines. This raises questions as to what mechanisms underlie how LLMs perform machine translation between languages. Motivated by recent interpretability findings--namely, that LLMs use massively multilingual latent feature representations to perform language modeling--we propose the interlingua hypothesis. The hypothesis holds that language models translate by reading a source sentence into a latent feature space, and generate a target sentence by reading from the latent feature space. We show three lines of evidence in support of this hypothesis: (1) variance in BLEU across language pairs is largely predictable from language-specific competences with no language pair-specific interaction terms; (2) many model components are causally influential in both monolingual tasks and translation tasks; and (3) fine-tuning on monolingual data recovers a large proportion of translation improvements relative to fine-tuning on aligned documents. Together, these provide convergent evidence in support of the interlingua hypothesis, and suggest new ways of understanding and improving how LLMs can be leveraged to perform translation tasks.

</details>


### [110] [Skill Following: Evaluating Actual Skill Use in Retrieval-Enabled LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00549)
*Seonghyeon Cho,Chanjun Park*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RAE指标，用于衡量LLM智能体在主动检索技能的任务上，技能启用与禁用之间的实际效果差异，揭示聚合检索提升可能掩盖真实性能损害的评价悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准评估方法在比较检索与未检索任务时存在严重的选择偏差，无法隔离技能使用的真实效果，导致聚合指标可能会误导对智能体工具使用能力的判断。

Method: 提出Skill Following能力并引入RAE指标，通过比较同一任务中配对的技能启用与技能禁用执行结果，且仅在智能体主动检索技能的任务上进行条件限制，消除选择偏差。在17个LLM上对编码和数学领域进行评估。

Result: 发现显著的评价悖论：多个模型在聚合检索提升上表现正面，但RAE为负。以MBPP+为例，多个看似系统层面受益的模型，实际上在其发生检索的任务上损害了自身性能。

Conclusion: 聚合平均可能造成工具使用熟练度的误导性幻觉，RAE能直接衡量从检索到答案的流水线是否真正实现了利大于弊，而非被统计假象掩盖。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on external skills, yet standard evaluations obscure whether retrieving these skills actually helps. Aggregate metrics often compare retrieved versus non-retrieved tasks, introducing severe selection bias and failing to isolate the true effect of skill use. To measure this actual-use capability-which we formalize as Skill Following (SF)-we introduce the Retrieval-Invoked Actual-Use Effect (RAE). RAE computes the same-task outcome difference between matched skill-enabled and skill-disabled executions, conditioned exclusively on tasks where the agent actively retrieved a skill. Evaluating 17 LLMs across coding and mathematical domains, we uncover a stark evaluation paradox: models frequently show positive aggregate retrieval lift but negative RAE. On MBPP+, multiple models that appear to benefit system-wide actually harm their own performance on the exact tasks where retrieval occurred. These findings demonstrate that aggregate averages can create a misleading illusion of tool-use proficiency, whereas RAE directly measures whether the retrieval-to-answer pipeline genuinely rescues more outcomes than it harms.

</details>


### [111] [Same Semantics, Different Outcome: On the Modality Robustness of Multimodal LLMs under Knowledge Conflict](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00550)
*Jungyeon Lee,Yejin Yoon,Taeuk Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在面对与参数化知识冲突的上下文证据时，其不同模态（文本、图像、或两者结合）的处理一致性。研究发现13个MLLMs在两份数据集上均远非鲁棒：模型倾向于更信任与参数知识矛盾的图像而非文本，且当矛盾文本与图像同时呈现时，偏好模态几乎任意，随输入顺序、模型和数据集而变化。这种不稳定性不仅损害多模态RAG性能，还可被对抗攻击利用。简单技术（提示、引导、DPO）大多无效，仅有监督微调（SFT）取得中等成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着MLLMs在实际应用中被广泛提供异构形式的上下文证据（文本、图像或两者兼有），理解不同表面形式是否被一致处理成为一个关键问题，尤其是在证据与模型参数化知识相冲突时，这种一致性直接影响模型的可靠性和安全性。

Method: 作者对13个MLLMs在两个数据集上进行了系统性的模态鲁棒性测试，设计实验对比文本、图像及文本-图像组合三种证据形式下的模型行为，并测试输入顺序的影响。随后评估了提示工程、引导（steering）、监督微调（SFT）和直接偏好优化（DPO）四种缓解方法的有效性。

Result: 发现：（1）与普遍认知相反，模型在图像形式下比文本形式更容易接受与参数知识矛盾的上下文；（2）当矛盾文本和图像同时呈现时，首选模态几乎任意，随输入顺序、模型和数据集变化。模态不稳定性会降低多模态RAG性能并易被对抗攻击利用。缓解措施中提示、引导和DPO基本无效，SFT仅取得中等成功。

Conclusion: MLLMs在不同模态下处理上下文证据存在根本性的不一致性，这一问题需要在整个训练流程的多个阶段引起重视和解决，不能仅依赖推理阶段的简单补救措施。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly provided with contextual evidence in heterogeneous forms: as a text passage, as a rendered image of the same passage, or as both together. However, it remains unclear how consistently these surface forms are processed, especially when the evidence conflicts with the model's parametric knowledge. We study modality robustness under knowledge conflict across 13 MLLMs and two datasets, and find them far from robust. (1) Contrary to common belief, models favor a context that contradicts parametric knowledge more readily in image form than in text form; (2) when a contradicting text and image are presented together, the preferred modality is essentially arbitrary, varying with input order, model, and dataset. We further demonstrate that this instability has practical consequences: it degrades performance in multimodal RAG and can be exploited by adversarial attacks. To alleviate this brittleness, we examine several simple techniques---prompting, steering, supervised fine-tuning (SFT), and direct preference optimization; the majority prove ineffective, whereas SFT achieves moderate success. We therefore call for greater awareness of this inconsistency and argue that it is fundamental, demanding attention at multiple training stages.

</details>


### [112] [EM^2Mem: Event-Centric Multimodal Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00551)
*Yijun Chen,Yaqi Zheng,Yanya Li,Boyi Xiao,Buqiang Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Jizhan Fang,Xinle Deng,Yunzhi Yao,Xuehai Wang,Liuxin Zhang,Hui Li,Huajun Chen,Shumin Deng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出EM^2Mem，一个以事件为中心的多模态记忆框架，通过将异构证据绑定到事件锚点来构建记忆单元，使每个记忆单元对齐多模态记录、时间上下文、图谱关联关系和语义事实，从而在长视频问答中实现紧凑的基于事件的证据读取。在三个基准上平均提升2.0-3.7个准确率点，Top-5事件级证据召回提升7.0点，推理延迟降低4.67倍，推理token减少63.66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态记忆方法检索的片段（字幕、帧、转录等）虽可搜索但并非生成就绪，语言模型必须在推理阶段重新构建跨模态和时间对齐，而此时上下文有限且归因困难，亟需一种将证据按事件组织而非按模态碎片组织的方法。

Method: 提出EM^2Mem事件中心多模态记忆框架：在记忆构建阶段将异构证据绑定到事件锚点，每个事件索引的记忆单元对齐多模态记录、时间上下文、图链接关系、语义事实及溯源信息，从而支持基于多模态事件的紧凑证据读取。

Result: 在三个长视频QA基准上，相比最强记忆基线平均准确率提升2.0/2.4/3.7个点；严格事件级Top-5证据召回提升7.0个点；每次查询延迟降低4.67倍，总推理token减少63.66%。

Conclusion: EM^2Mem通过事件级绑定解决多模态碎片化检索与生成就绪性之间的鸿沟，显著提升长视频问答准确率、证据可追溯性和推理效率，验证了事件中心记忆优于模态中心记忆的设计思路。

Abstract: Multimodal memory offers a scalable interface for long-video question answering, but existing methods often retrieve captions, frames, transcripts, summaries, or graph facts as isolated fragments. Although searchable, such fragments are not generation-ready: language models must reconstruct cross-modal and temporal alignments at inference time, when context is limited and attribution is difficult. We propose EM^2Mem, an event-centric multimodal memory framework that binds heterogeneous evidence to event anchors during memory construction. Each event-indexed memory cell aligns multimodal records, temporal context, graph-linked relations, semantic facts, and provenance, enabling compact evidence readout over grounded multimodal events rather than modality-specific fragments. Across three long-video QA benchmarks, EM^2Mem improves average accuracy over the strongest memory baseline by 2.0, 2.4, and 3.7 points, improves strict event-level Top-5 evidence recall by 7.0 points, and reduces per-query latency by 4.67 times and total inference tokens by 63.66% (The code will be integrated into https://github.com/zjunlp/LightMem).

</details>


### [113] [Enoki: Efficient Multi-Level Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00581)
*Elisei Rykov,Timur Ionov,Nikolay Ivanov,Maksim Savkin,Maksim Makarenko,Alexander Panchenko,Vasily Konovalov,Julia Belikova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Enoki，一个基于开放信息抽取（OpenIE）的多层级幻觉检测框架。Enoki通过提取文本锚定的关系事实，对其进行证据验证，并将不支持的实况映射回幻觉跨度，实现声明级验证和跨度级定位的统一，无需额外对齐。实验表明Enoki在资源消耗更少的情况下与强声明级系统竞争力相当，并在细粒度跨度级和实体级定位上表现更优，同时发布了EnokiQA双粒度数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测器通常在单一层级工作：声明级方法提供可解释的事实单元，跨度级方法定位不支持的文本。桥接这两种视角成本高昂，因为基于LLM的流程需要多次分解和验证调用，模块化系统则需要额外的声明到跨度对齐步骤。

Method: Enoki采用开放信息抽取框架，提取文本锚定的关系事实，将事实与证据进行验证，然后将不支持的事实映射回幻觉跨度。其共享表示使得声明级验证和跨度级定位无需额外对齐即可同时完成，并支持基于LLM、基于编码器和基于规则三种抽取模式，通过统一接口在准确性和推理成本之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验表明Enoki在资源消耗更少的情况下与强声明级系统保持竞争力，并在细粒度跨度级和实体级定位上取得更优性能。同时发布了EnokiQA数据集，包含对齐的声明级验证和跨度级定位标注。

Conclusion: Enoki通过统一的开放信息抽取表示有效弥合了声明级与跨度级幻觉检测之间的鸿沟，实现了多层级检测的效率和准确性兼顾，为该领域提供了新的方法论和评测资源。

Abstract: Ensuring factuality remains a critical challenge for deploying LLMs in high-stakes settings. Existing hallucination detectors usually operate at a single level: claim-level methods provide interpretable factual units, while span-level methods localize unsupported text. Bridging these views is costly, as LLM-heavy pipelines require multiple decomposition and verification calls, and modular systems need additional claim-to-span alignment. We propose Enoki, an Open Information Extraction framework for multi-level hallucination detection. Enoki extracts text-anchored relational facts, verifies them against evidence, and projects unsupported facts back to hallucinated spans. This shared representation enables claim-level verification and span-level localization without requiring separate alignment. Enoki supports LLM-based, encoder-based, and rule-based extraction regimes, balancing accuracy and inference cost through a common interface. Experiments show that Enoki remains competitive with strong claim-level systems while using fewer resources and achieves superior performance on fine-grained span- and entity-level localization. We also release EnokiQA, a dual-granularity dataset with aligned claim-level verification and span-level localization annotations.

</details>


### [114] [Quit While You're Ahead: Quit for Efficient Candidate Generation in Machine Translation Reranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00588)
*Guangyu Chen,Boxuan Lyu,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Kotaro Funakoshi,Manabu Okumura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Quit，一种面向整个"生成-重排序"流水线的提前停止策略，通过将候选生成视为不确定性下的顺序决策，在候选集质量估计稳定时提前终止生成与重排序，在MBR和QE重排序上分别实现1.47-2.66倍和3.43-4.12倍的端到端加速，同时保持翻译质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的MBR解码和QE重排序等重排序方法虽然能有效提升神经机器翻译质量，但带来了高推理延迟。已有加速方法仅针对MBR解码、只缩减重排序计算量，既未解决QE重排序的问题，也几乎未触及候选生成这一更主要的计算瓶颈。

Method: Quit将候选生成视为不确定性下的顺序决策过程，增量式地生成并重排序候选，当候选集中最高估计质量趋于稳定时即停止生成与重排序，从而实现针对完整生成-重排序流水线的早期终止策略。

Result: 在三种NMT模型和19个语言对上进行了全面实验，Quit在MBR上获得1.47-2.66倍、在QE重排序上获得3.43-4.12倍的端到端加速，同时翻译质量保持在预设等价范围之内。

Conclusion: Quit是目前首个覆盖完整生成-重排序流水线的早期停止方法，在显著加速推理的同时不影响翻译质量，为NMT重排序的高效部署提供了新思路。

Abstract: Reranking methods, such as Minimum Bayes Risk (MBR) decoding and Quality Estimation (QE) reranking, are widely used in modern neural machine translation (NMT) to select an output from a set of candidate hypotheses. However, the performance gains come at the cost of high inference latency. Existing acceleration methods target MBR decoding and reduce only reranking computation, leaving QE reranking unaddressed and candidate generation---which can be the larger computational bottleneck---largely untouched. In this work, we propose Quit (Quantifying Uncertainty for Incremental Termination), a novel early-stopping strategy for the entire generation--reranking pipeline. Viewing candidate generation as a sequential decision under uncertainty, Quit incrementally generates and reranks candidates, stopping when the highest estimated quality in the candidate set stabilizes. Comprehensive experiments on three NMT models across 19 language pairs show that Quit yields end-to-end speedups of $1.47$--$2.66\times$ for MBR and $3.43$--$4.12\times$ for QE reranking while preserving translation quality within prespecified equivalence margins.

</details>


### [115] [Investigating Assistant Bias in LLM User Simulators Using a Role Vector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00608)
*Daeheon Jeong,Yoonjoo Lee,Eugene Choi,Sinie van der Ben,Juho Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从模型激活层面分析了LLM用户模拟器中的"助手偏见"问题，通过提取用户角色向量来识别和操控用户行为方向，发现该向量可识别且能引发类似用户的行为，但过度强化会夸大用户行为并覆盖个体用户画像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM用户模拟器被广泛用于替代昂贵的人工评估，但它们存在"助手偏见"——倾向于合作和追求任务目标，很少再现真实用户的沮丧或脱离行为，这损害了评估的有效性。此前研究指出该偏见源于模型训练过程，角色扮演提示无法覆盖它，因此需要从表示层面理解这一偏见。

Method: 通过对比模型在同一对话中表征用户视角与助手视角的差异，提取"用户角色向量"（user role vector），从模型激活层面对助手偏见进行分析，并研究该向量对模拟行为的影响。

Result: 研究发现：（i）用户方向在激活中是可识别的，能引发类似用户的行为，且捕获了与助手特质不同的特征；（ii）虽然用户角色激活与模拟真实性相关且定向强化可增强它，但过度强化会夸大用户行为并覆盖个体用户画像。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了LLM用户模拟器的表示层面分析，确证了助手偏见在结构上是可识别的，且用户行为可以进行方向性分析，为改进用户模拟器提供了新的研究视角。

Abstract: LLM-based user simulators are increasingly used to evaluate autonomous agents at scale, in place of costly human evaluations. Despite this promise, these simulators exhibit "assistant bias," a tendency to cooperate and pursue task goals. They rarely reproduce the frustration or disengagement that real users exhibit, compromising evaluation validity. Prior work outlines that this bias is baked in during model training, which role-playing prompts fail to override. We analyze this bias from model activations, extracting a user role vector by contrasting how the model represents user versus assistant perspectives on the same dialogue. We observe two findings: (i) the user direction is identifiable in activations, elicits user-like behaviors, and captures characteristics distinct from assistant traits; and (ii) although user-role activation associates with simulation realism and steering strengthens it, it can exaggerate user behaviors and override individual user profiles. Together, our findings provide a representation-level analysis of LLM user simulators, confirming that assistant bias is structurally identifiable and that user behavior can be directionally analyzed.

</details>


### [116] [Trust Your Guide Only When Certain: Uncertainty-Aware Sparse Alignment at Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00624)
*Zeen Zhu,Zhuo Li,Weiyang Guo,Liye Zhao,Haibing Di,Yequan Wang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出TUSA（基于信任的不确定性稀疏对齐）框架，通过引入不确定性感知仲裁机制，仅在监督器置信且令牌语义显著时进行干预，解决了弱监督器在稠密干预下的高熵问题，实验表明相比稠密基线的安全偏好提升最高15.6%，通用偏好率提升最高12.0%，同时绕过约50%的对齐步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐范式存在结构性不匹配：弱监督器在绝大多数令牌上呈现普遍的高熵（低置信度），但稠密干预方法要求每一步解码都进行监督，导致频繁的低置信干预可能破坏基座模型的有效推理并带来效用损失。

Method: TUSA将对齐重新定义为动态仲裁过程，摒弃连续监督模式，引入不确定性感知的仲裁器，仅当两个条件同时满足时才授权干预：(1) 监督器对当前令牌输出具有高置信度；(2) 令牌具有语义显著性。该机制有效过滤由不确定性驱动的噪声和冗余监督。

Result: 跨多个模型和基准的大量实验表明，TUSA在安全对齐和通用帮助性方面均持续优于基线。绕过约50%的对齐步骤后，安全偏好最高提升15.6%，通用偏好率最高提升12.0%。

Conclusion: 选择性高精度对齐优于连续监督，证明了"少而精"的干预策略可以在推理时对齐中取得更好的效果。

Abstract: A prominent paradigm in inference-time alignment employs lightweight supervisors to steer Large Language Models (LLMs). Through empirical analysis, we identify a structural mismatch in this paradigm: weak supervisors exhibit pervasive high entropy across the vast majority of tokens, yet prevailing dense intervention approaches mandate supervision at every decoding step. This leads to frequent low-confidence interventions that can disrupt valid base-model reasoning and incur substantial utility costs. To resolve this, we propose TUSA (Trust-based Uncertainty Sparse Alignment). Moving away from continuous oversight, TUSA reframes alignment as a dynamic arbitration process, introducing an uncertainty-aware arbiter that authorizes intervention only when two conditions are met: the supervisor is confident and the token is semantically salient. This mechanism effectively filters out uncertainty-driven noise and redundant supervision. Extensive experiments across multiple models and benchmarks show that TUSA consistently improves both safety alignment and general helpfulness. By bypassing approximately 50% of alignment steps, it not only enhances safety preference by up to 15.6%, but also boosts general preference rates by up to 12.0% compared to the dense baseline, demonstrating that selective, high-precision alignment can outperform continuous supervision.

</details>


### [117] [ExpArt-KG: Artwork Image Description Generation through Iterative Exploration of Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00629)
*Yuta Kato,Shintaro Ozaki,Kazuki Hayashi,Yusuke Sakai,Hidetaka Kamigaito,Katsuhiko Hayashi,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于知识图谱检索增强生成（RAG）的框架，使大型视觉语言模型（LVLMs）能够生成更详细、更准确的图像解释，并构建了艺术品领域的知识图谱ExpArt-KG进行验证，实验表明该方法提升了说明的详细程度并降低了检索成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型在图像文本生成和视觉问答上表现良好，但难以全面准确地描述图像中对象相关的实体与概念间的事实关系。

Method: 提出一个交替执行答案生成与知识图谱检索的框架，利用正确性判断来控制搜索过程，以高效获取必要且充分的事实信息；并构建了艺术品领域的知识图谱ExpArt-KG，其中图像与实体的对应关系明确无歧义。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在提高艺术品说明详细程度的同时降低了外部知识的检索成本，且生成质量与固定迭代次数的方法相当。

Conclusion: 通过自适应检索策略结合知识图谱，可以有效提升LVLMs对图像内容的事实性描述能力，在保证生成质量的同时实现检索效率的优化。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) achieve strong performance on image-grounded text generation and visual question answering. However, it remains difficult for them to comprehensively and accurately describe the factual relations among the entities and concepts associated with the objects depicted in an image. In this work, we propose a framework that efficiently exploits factual information from a knowledge graph via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), with the goal of enabling LVLMs to generate detailed and accurate image explanations. Specifically, our method alternates between answer generation and knowledge-graph retrieval, and controls the search using a correctness judgment, thereby acquiring the necessary and sufficient factual information efficiently. We also construct a knowledge graph for the artwork domain (ExpArt-KG), in which the correspondence between images and entities is unambiguous. Applying the proposed method to this knowledge graph, we show experimentally that it improves the level of detail of artwork explanations and reduces the retrieval cost of external knowledge while maintaining generation quality comparable to that of iterating a fixed number of times.

</details>


### [118] [Creative Generation via Multi-Agent Debate: Does Debate Suppress Diversity?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00683)
*Tien Anh Nguyen,Khanh-Binh Nguyen,Van Dai Do,Svetha Venkatesh,Hung Le*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Creative-MAD，通过两项干预措施（认知透镜分配和基于嵌入的同伴选择）解决多智能体辩论（MAD）在创造性生成任务中输出多样性不足的问题，在四个创造性基准上同时提升了词汇和语义多样性且保持了输出质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体辩论（MAD）在事实性和推理任务上表现出色，但其收敛驱动的设计会抑制独立运行间的输出多样性，这与创造性生成任务（如叙事写作、科学构思）需要多样化的探索需求相矛盾。

Method: 首先从理论上证明保持每次辩论会话中智能体间的多样性是实现跨独立运行输出多样性的必要条件。基于此提出Creative-MAD，包含两项协同干预：认知透镜分配（Cognitive Lens Assignment）通过将每个智能体锚定到独特且持久的认知模式以对抗身份漂移；基于嵌入的同伴选择（Embedding-based Peer Selection）通过将每个智能体的上下文限制在最语义遥远的同伴上以对抗多数人拉拢效应。

Result: 在四个创造性基准上的实验表明，Creative-MAD显著提升了词汇和语义多样性，同时保持了与MAD相当的输出质量。

Conclusion: 保持辩论会话内智能体间的分歧是创造性任务中实现跨独立运行输出多样性的关键，该工作为将MAD范式适配到创造性生成任务提供了理论依据和有效方案。

Abstract: Creative generation tasks, such as narrative writing and scientific ideation, demand both high-quality outputs and distinct responses across independent runs to maximize exploration. Multi-Agent Debate (MAD) has shown strong quality gains on factual and reasoning tasks, making it a natural candidate for creative generation. However, we find its convergence-driven design actively suppresses output diversity across independent runs, creating an inherent trade-off with creative tasks. We theoretically show that preserving diversity among agents within each debate session is a necessary condition for achieving diverse outputs across independent runs. Building on this finding, we propose Creative-MAD, which introduces two synergistic interventions to sustain agent divergence. Specifically, Cognitive Lens Assignment counters identity drift by anchoring each agent to a distinct and persistent cognitive mode, while Embedding-based Peer Selection counters majority pull by limiting each agent's context to its most semantically distant peers. Experiments across four creative benchmarks demonstrate that Creative-MAD significantly enhances both lexical and semantic diversity while maintaining MAD's output quality.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [AgentProv: Auditing Agentic LLM API Providers via Tool-use Policy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00052)
*Xun Wang,Bihe Zhao,Michael Backes,Franziska Boenisch,Adam Dziedzic*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Provenance（AgentProv），首个基于动作（action）的智能体LLM API身份审计方法。通过指纹化模型的工具调用分布并使用MMD置换检验判定身份，解决了现有基于文本输出的审计在智能体API场景下因结构化输出和系统提示注入而失效的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 商业LLM API可能静默替换、量化或包装底层模型以降低部署成本，但现有审计方法仅依赖文本输出通道进行身份验证。在智能体API（如OpenAI、Anthropic等）中，当模型调用工具时，服务栈会丢弃文本只暴露结构化动作，且提供商注入的系统提示会扭曲文本分布，导致文本通道审计不可靠甚至误报。

Method: AgentProv利用近期智能体后训练将工具使用直接内化到权重的特性，通过模型分类的工具调用分布进行指纹识别，采用MMD（最大均值差异）置换检验来决定模型身份。

Result: 在630个评估的检查点对上，AgentProv对每个被替换模型均实现100%检出率；在系统提示注入下，假阳性率仅7%（对比MET的67%和RUT的53%）。在第三方API端点上的实验结果与独立token计数旁信道检测出的提供商注入系统提示一致。

Conclusion: AgentProv是首个针对智能体LLM API的基于动作的身份审计方法，开辟了一条新的审计通道，该通道对部署上下文具有高度不变性，有效克服了文本通道审计在智能体场景中的结构性脆弱性。

Abstract: Commercial LLM APIs advertise a specific foundation model, but the served backbone may be silently substituted, quantized, or wrapped, for example to save deployment costs. All existing audits decide backbone identity from the text-output channel, which is structurally fragile for agentic APIs because modern serving stacks (OpenAI, Anthropic, Gemini, Cloudflare Workers AI, LangGraph) discard text and expose only structured actions when the model calls a tool, and provider-injected system prompts can distort text distributions enough that text-channel tests falsely accuse honest providers of substituting the claimed model. We observe that recent agentic post-training internalizes tool-use directly into the weights, opening a new audit channel that the serving stack still exposes and that is largely invariant to deployment context. We introduce Agentic Provenance (AgentProv), the first action-based identity audit for agentic LLM APIs: AgentProv fingerprints a deployed model through its categorical tool-call distribution and decides identity via an MMD permutation test. AgentProv catches every substituted model (100% on 630 evaluated checkpoint pairs), while holding the false-positive rate under system-prompt injection at 7% (vs. 67% for MET and 53% for RUT). On third-party API endpoints, AgentProv's disagreements with MET are consistent with an independent token-count side-channel that detects provider-injected system prompts.

</details>


### [120] [A Formal Analysis of Agent Payment Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00060)
*Ke Jiang,Mohan Yu,Yuan Chang,Mohit Kumar Jangid,Jianyu Niu,Cong Wang,Yinqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对四种代表性AI代理支付协议（x402、MPP、ACP、AP2）在Tamarin中进行了系统形式化分析，通过86个验证案例识别出40个此前未记录的正式一致性发现，并将其整合为18条共享安全原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理支付协议正在成为自主商业的关键交易层，但与传统支付流程不同，它们将用户意图、委托授权、凭证使用、结算和履行分散在多个参与者和阶段中，导致安全依赖关系无法由单一消息或参与者强制执行。然而这些保证在快速演进的规范、模式和参考实现中大多是隐式的，缺乏系统性的形式化分析。

Method: 作者在Tamarin中形式化了四种代表性代理支付协议（x402、MPP、ACP、AP2），采用统一的代理支付生命周期抽象，构建基于源代码的模型以捕获各协议的角色、状态、信任假设和生命周期转换。通过基于源代码的验证问题和反例轨迹来暴露缺失的绑定、状态约束和跨阶段对应关系，并针对每个保留的违规构建最小增强参考模型以重新验证预期属性。

Result: 在86个验证案例中，复现了46个已知或校准案例，并识别出40个此前未记录的正式一致性发现。对每个保留的违规隔离缺失的协议关系并重新验证；此外评估了新x402发现在三个实现中的表现，并通过实现PoC、SDK/模式级证据和源代码对齐的可执行轨迹验证了覆盖五条安全原则的十个代表性发现。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，委托授权必须与其所产生的经济和服务的效应在各参与者、状态和协议阶段之间保持一致性，这构成了代理支付协议安全性的核心要求。

Abstract: Agent payment protocols are emerging as a key transaction layer for autonomous commerce, enabling AI agents to purchase goods and services and execute payments on users' behalf. Unlike conventional payment flows, they distribute user intent, delegated authority, credential use, settlement, and fulfillment across multiple actors and stages, creating security dependencies that no single message or participant can enforce. Yet these guarantees remain largely implicit across evolving specifications, schemas, and reference implementations, with little systematic formal analysis.
  We formalize four representative agent payment protocols: x402, MPP, ACP, and AP2 in Tamarin. Using a common abstraction of the agent payment lifecycle, we construct source-grounded models that capture each protocol's roles, state, trust assumptions, and lifecycle transitions. Rather than assuming a complete property taxonomy, we use source-backed verification questions and counterexample traces to expose missing bindings, state constraints, and cross-stage correspondences, consolidating them into 18 shared security principles. Across 86 verification cases, our analysis reproduces 46 known or calibration cases and identifies 40 previously undocumented formal-consistency findings. For each retained violation, we isolate the missing protocol relation, construct a minimally strengthened reference model, and reverify the intended property. We further evaluate the new x402 findings across three implementations and validate ten representative findings through implementation PoCs, SDK/schema-level witnesses, and source-aligned executable traces spanning five security principles. Our results show that delegated authorization must remain consistent with its resulting economic and service effects across actors, states, and protocol stages.

</details>


### [121] [Explainable Artificial Intelligence for Industrial Cybersecurity: A Review of Methods, Operational Integration, and Research Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00171)
*Amr S. Mohamed,Charlotte Fritz,Ahmad Mohammad Saber,Yiqun Ma,Ratinder Kaur,Mohammed Al-Darwbi,Daniela Friedrich,Deepa Kundur*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对工业网络安全中可解释人工智能（XAI）技术进行了全面综述，重点关注工业安全运营中心（SOC）环境和运营安全工作流，涵盖AI在SOC中的作用、XAI技术家族、工业系统特有的运营/监管/安全需求、关键挑战及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业基础设施数字化转型及IT/OT融合扩大了网络攻击面，虽然AI/ML技术被越来越多地部署于工业网络安全（特别是SOC）中以改善威胁检测和自动响应，但其不透明的决策过程给运营信任、监管合规和事件响应带来挑战，因此需要XAI来提升AI驱动安全系统的透明性和可解释性。

Method: 本文采用系统综述方法：首先考察AI在工业SOC工作流中的角色及工业环境中的运营数据类型，分析AI威胁检测的优缺点；然后分类审查主要XAI方法家族（特征归因、替代模型、基于规则的解释和可视化技术），分析其在工业用例中的适用性；最后讨论工业系统与传统IT环境的差异化要求及关键挑战。

Result: 论文识别出工业网络安全中应用XAI的核心挑战，包括：标注数据集有限、模型可靠性问题、可解释性与性能之间的权衡，以及XAI工具整合到SOC工作流的困难。同时明确了工业系统在运营、监管和安全方面区别于传统IT环境的独特要求。

Conclusion: 论文指出开放式研究方向，强调需要开发可信赖、运营上可行且领域特定的XAI驱动网络安全解决方案，以支持工业环境中AI安全工具的落地应用。

Abstract: The increasing digitalization of industrial infrastructure and the convergence of information technology (IT) and operational technology (OT) have expanded the cyberattack surface of industrial systems. To address the growing complexity of cyber threats, artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) techniques are increasingly deployed within industrial cybersecurity operations, particularly in Security Operations Centers (SOCs). While these approaches improve anomaly detection, threat analysis, and automated response, their opaque decision-making presents challenges for operational trust, regulatory compliance, and incident response. EXplainable Artificial Intelligence (XAI) has emerged as a promising paradigm to improve the transparency and interpretability of AI-driven cybersecurity systems and decisions. This paper provides a comprehensive review of XAI techniques in industrial cybersecurity, focusing on industrial SOC environments and operational security workflows. We examine the role of AI in industrial SOC workflows, the types of operational data leveraged in industrial environments, and the benefits and limitations of AI-based threat detection. We then review major families of XAI approaches, including feature attribution methods, surrogate models, rule-based explanations, and visualization techniques, and analyze their applicability to industrial use cases. We further discuss the operational, regulatory, and safety requirements that distinguish industrial systems from traditional IT environments. Key challenges are examined, including limited labeled datasets, model reliability, explainability-performance tradeoffs, and the integration of XAI tools into SOC workflows. Finally, we identify open research directions and opportunities for developing trustworthy, operationally viable, and domain-specific XAI-enabled cybersecurity solutions for industrial environments.

</details>


### [122] [DUPIN: Attack Learning Is Still Needed! Demonstrating Few-Shot after Unsupervised Pretraining Is A Nimble Forensics Learner](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00259)
*Chanwoo Bae,Hailun Ding,Shiqing Ma,Xiangyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为DUPIN的新型学习型攻击取证方法，通过在大规模审计事件（来源图）上进行无监督预训练，再借助少量标注的攻击样本进行少样本微调，最终在25个APT攻击活动、四个不同数据源上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的攻击取证方法依赖大量标注数据，而现实中标注攻击样本稀缺且获取成本高昂。作者希望利用海量未标注的审计日志，通过预训练加大幅降低对标注样本的依赖，提升APT攻击检测的泛化能力。

Method: DUPIN包含两个阶段：1) 无监督预训练——在高达38-52天、总计7.3TB的海量审计日志（以来源图形式表示）上进行预训练；2) 少样本学习阶段——利用少量标注的攻击示例进行微调，以增强检测能力。

Result: 在25个APT攻击活动、四个不同数据源上进行了评估，并与多种基线方法进行了对比，验证了方法的可扩展性和有效性。

Conclusion: DUPIN证明了在大规模未标注审计数据上进行无监督预训练，结合少样本微调，能够有效提升APT攻击检测性能，同时降低对大量人工标注的依赖，是一种可扩展的攻击取证方案。

Abstract: We propose a novel approach to learning-based attack forensics called DUPIN. DUPIN performs unsupervised pre-training on an enormous amount of audit events in the form of provenance graphs. It then proceeds to a few-shot learning stage, leveraging a small number of labeled attack examples to fine-tune its detection capabilities. We pretrain DUPIN on up to 38 - 52 days of audit logs (7.3TB total) and evaluate it against various baselines on 25 APT campaigns across four different data sources, facilitating the scalable evaluation.

</details>


### [123] [Delegation Without Trust: An Empirical Gap Analysis of Identity, Authorization, and Runtime Governance in Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00267)
*Panduranga Sai Varma Dantuluri,Jyotirmoy Sundi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出自主LLM代理必须在"不可信模型假设"下进行安全评估，即即使代理被完全提示注入，系统仍不能超出明确委托的权限。作者建立了面向多代理委托的威胁模型和八项安全需求，证明现有四种主流框架（LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen和MCP）均不满足要求，并实现了一个能够防御所有威胁的授权代理，其性能开销微乎其微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主LLM代理正代表用户持有凭证、调用工具并派生子代理，但驱动代理的语言模型可被对手劫持。这使得"谁有权在谁的授权下做什么"这一分布式系统经典问题变得紧迫且基本未解决。现有安全设计大多将授权逻辑内置于模型内部，缺乏对提示注入攻击的系统性防御。

Method: 首先定义不可信模型假设下的安全标准，建立涵盖四类对手（混淆代理、令牌窃取与重放、提示注入权限提升、被攻陷子代理）的威胁模型并推导八项安全需求；然后系统性审计现有框架；最后实现并对抗性评估一个授权代理（authorization broker），该代理将授权决策从模型内部抽离到外部执行。

Result: 默认代理运行时（宽泛载体凭证+模型内授权）无法通过全部四种威胁测试；在LangGraph、CrewAI、AutoGen和MCP中，三个无内置隔离，一个仅部分覆盖。所实现的授权代理可拦截全部四种威胁，抵御11种直接攻击设计，拒绝20万伪造令牌中的全部，将受攻陷子代理限制在其委托任务内（平均1.5个可达动作对比载体委托下的8,100个，基于2,000个随机场景），且每次决策仅约2.6微秒开销。该机制已在VotalAI的LLM Shield中生产落地。

Conclusion: 代理系统安全必须以不可信模型为前提进行设计，授权必须从模型内部解耦到外部代理层强制执行。授权代理方案在安全性、对抗鲁棒性和性能开销上均验证了可行性，表明这一思路可从研究走向生产实践。

Abstract: Autonomous LLM agents increasingly act on a user's behalf: they hold credentials, call tools and services, and spawn sub-agents that act further on their behalf. This turns a long-standing distributed-systems question -- who is authorized to do what, on whose authority -- into an urgent and largely unsolved problem, because the component driving each agent is a language model an adversary can hijack. We argue that agent security must be evaluated under an untrusted-model assumption: a correct system is one in which a fully prompt-injected agent still cannot exceed the authority explicitly delegated to it. Against this standard we make three contributions. First, we give a threat model for multi-agent delegation centered on four adversaries -- confused deputy, token theft and replay, prompt-injection privilege escalation, and compromised sub-agents -- and derive eight security requirements a governed agent system must meet. Second, we show the gap is real: a default agent runtime modeling common practice (broad bearer credentials, authorization gated inside the model) fails all four threats, and across four widely used frameworks -- LangGraph, CrewAI, AutoGen, and the Model Context Protocol (MCP) authorization model -- three provide no built-in confinement and one only partial; no existing standard alone covers the requirement set. Third, we implement and adversarially evaluate an authorization broker that closes the gap. It blocks all four threats; it resists 11 direct attacks on its design and accepts 0 of 200,000 forged tokens; it confines a compromised sub-agent to its delegated task (a mean of 1.5 reachable actions versus all 8,100 under bearer delegation, across 2,000 randomized scenarios); and it enforces at microsecond cost (about 2.6 microseconds per decision), negligible against model inference. These principles are also realized in production in VotalAI's LLM Shield.

</details>


### [124] [Workload Identification with Physical Side Channels for AI Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00309)
*Simone Gargiulo,Gabriel Kulp*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种通过物理功率信号（而非可欺骗的NVML遥测）来识别NVIDIA H200 GPU上运行的AI工作负载类别的方法。研究覆盖17种开源LLM和25种非AI工作负载，能以97%的准确率区分训练、推理和非AI计算。同时测试了四种对抗性规避策略，并提出了一种加固检测器，能对其中三种实现≥99%的检出率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI治理需要国际政策层面的算力验证机制，而确定前沿实验室是否遵守协议需要监管机构辨别其算力用途。现有的芯片内NVML遥测可被伪造或重放，因此需要一种独立于操作者配合的物理验证通道。

Method: 在NVIDIA H200上以约10MHz采样率记录930条五秒功率迹线，覆盖17种开源LLM家族和25种非AI负载。从功率信号提取频谱特征进行分类，区分训练、推理和非AI计算。随后将GPU操作者视为对抗性主体，测试四种伪装训练的规避策略，生成680条额外对抗迹线，并训练加固分类器进行检测。

Result: 对未见过的模型家族，区分训练/推理/非AI的准确率为97%，宏平均F1为0.955。AI负载频谱能量主要低于约20kHz，训练的显存受限优化器更新过程特别可识别。加固分类器对三种规避策略（保留该策略不参与训练）检出率≥99%，而对稀释的低秩适应（LoRA）检出率为48%-88%，增加额外救援规则后可提升至≥98%。

Conclusion: 外部观察者原则上能通过GPU物理功率信号独立验证AI工作负载类别，无需操作者配合且不易被欺骗。虽然对抗性评估尚不全面，但为AI算力治理的国际政策提供了首个可操作的物理验证基础，并为开发和测试更强规避机制提供了数据集。

Abstract: AI compute verification is one of the first tangible and tractable points for international policy aimed at AI governance. Determining whether frontier labs, or any operator, comply with agreements requires the regulating authority to discern how their compute is used. The elementary building block of AI compute is the GPU, and any activity it executes leaves a physical trace. Here, we show that an external observer can identify the class of the workload running on an NVIDIA H200 from its power draw. Unlike on-chip NVML telemetry, which can be spoofed or replayed, such a physical channel can in principle be observed independently of operator cooperation. We recorded $930$ five-second traces at $\sim 10$ MHz, covering seventeen open LLM families and twenty-five non-AI workloads. Over this corpus we separate training from inference and from non-AI computation with an accuracy of $97\%$ and a macro-averaged F1 score of $0.955$, evaluated on model families unseen during training. AI workload spectral content predominantly lies below $\sim 20$kHz and training is particularly recognizable through the memory-bound optimizer update. The GPU operator is then treated as adversarial and able to reshape the physical computation itself. Four evasion strategies are tested to disguise training as inference, producing an additional 680 adversarial traces. A detector hardened against evasion strategies, with the tested strategy held out, catches training $\geq 99\%$ of the time for three of the four strategies. The fourth, diluted low-rank adaptation (LoRA), is detected $48$--$88\%$ of the time with a hardened classifier, rising to $\geq 98\%$ with an additional rescue rule. While these attacks are not a comprehensive evaluation against adversarial behaviour, they offer initial insights beyond genuine activities and a dataset for developing and testing stronger evasion mechanisms.

</details>


### [125] [OreProof: Verifiable Provenance with Limited Disclosure for Critical-Minerals Supply Chains Using Zero-Knowledge Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00340)
*Oleksandr Hrabar,Hossein Arshadi Soufiani,Henry M. Kim,Chien-Chih Chen,Ali Vazirizadeh,Hjalmar Turesson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍OreProof平台，一种解决关键矿产供应链中可验证性与披露意愿之间结构性矛盾的原型可追溯系统。通过将零知识证明、混合链上/链下数据模型与UNTP对齐的可验证凭证相结合，该系统在透明基线下可将可直接推断的机密属性从四类中的三类降至零，同时提升批处理锚定的吞吐量，并提炼出四项初始设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键矿产供应链存在结构性张力：监管方和购买方要求可验证的来源与溯源信息，但上游参与者不愿披露供应商身份、品位/产量及价格等敏感数据。传统验证方式似乎要求公开这些数据，因此需要一种能够在确保可验证性的同时保护披露者隐私的解决方案。

Method: 设计科学研究方法，构建了OreProof黄金溯源平台原型。核心技术包括：混合链上/链下数据模型、用于选择性披露的Groth16零知识证明、Merkle批量锚定管道、以及基于UNTP标准的可验证凭证，部署于公共zkEVM测试网络。

Result: 在定义的攻击者模型下，相对于透明基线的三到四类可直接推断的机密属性降至零；批量锚定大幅提升了系统吞吐量。

Conclusion: 论文贡献包括OreProof原型以及四条初始设计原则：1) 对已承诺数据进行证明而非直接暴露；2) 仅凭证可验证来源，对混合商品标记未知输入；3) 从源头即颁发符合标准的凭证；4) 按供应链角色划分披露权限。

Abstract: Critical-minerals supply chains face a structural tension: regulators and buyers demand verifiable provenance, yet upstream actors are hesitant to disclose supplier identities, assay grades/yields, and prices that verification appears to require. We report a design science account of OreProof, a prototypical traceability platform addressing this verifiability-disclosure trade-off. Instantiated for gold, OreProof combines a hybrid on-chain/off-chain data model, Groth16 zero-knowledge proofs for selective disclosure, a Merkle-batched anchoring pipeline, and UNTP-aligned verifiable credentials on a public zkEVM testnet. Against a transparent baseline, directly inferable confidential attributes fell from three of four categories to none under a defined attacker model, while batched anchoring substantially improved throughput. Our contributions are the artifact prototype as well as four nascent design principles: prove over committed data rather than exposing it; credential only verifiable origin and flag unknown inputs for blended commodities; emit standards-aligned credentials from the outset; and partition disclosure by supply-chain role.

</details>


### [126] [NeuroPriv: Adversarial Representation Learning for Privacy in Wearable EEG Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00390)
*Sarmistha Sarna Gomasta,Bhawana Chhaglani,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了可穿戴脑电图（EEG）系统在传输压缩特征时存在严重的神经隐私泄露风险——常用的EEG特征不仅能完成预期的认知任务，还能被用来推断用户身份、性别和年龄；研究进一步证明通过隐私感知的表示学习可以在保持任务性能的同时显著降低这些敏感属性的推理精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴EEG系统在认知监测和数字健康应用中越来越普及，但现有系统普遍假设"传输压缩特征而非原始EEG数据"即可提供足够的隐私保护，该假设是否成立缺乏系统性验证，因此需要实证检验压缩EEG特征的隐私泄露风险。

Method: 以EEGMAT为案例研究，对其压缩EEG特征进行多任务评估：(1) 认知状态分类（设计用途）；(2) 性别、年龄和主体身份的敏感属性推断（隐私风险测试）。随后引入隐私感知的表示学习方法，对比其在保持认知分类性能的同时对敏感属性推理的抑制效果。

Result: 压缩EEG特征在认知状态分类上达到0.788的平衡准确率，但同时实现了0.858的性别推断、0.789的年龄推断和0.692的身份推断准确率。采用隐私感知表示学习后，任务性能保持在0.781，而性别、年龄和身份推断准确率分别降至0.563、0.467和0.206。

Conclusion: 可穿戴EEG的压缩特征本身并不构成充分的隐私保护屏障，研究中存在显著的神经隐私泄露。隐私感知的表示学习能够在几乎不损失任务性能的前提下大幅削弱敏感属性推断，因此可穿戴神经健康系统应采用目的受限的表示（purpose-limited representations）并建立显式的隐私审计机制。

Abstract: Wearable EEG systems may expose sensitive information beyond their intended health function, creating substantial risks to neuroprivacy. In this work, we show that commonly used EEG features can reveal participant identity and demographic attributes in addition to supporting the intended cognitive task. Wearable EEG is increasingly being explored for cognitive monitoring, neurological assessment, and longitudinal digital-health applications, yet many systems assume that transmitting compact spectral or spatial features instead of raw EEG provides sufficient privacy protection. Using EEGMAT as a motivating case study, we find that compact EEG features achieve a balanced accuracy of 0.788 for cognitive-state classification while enabling gender, age, and subject-identity inference with balanced accuracies of 0.858, 0.789, and 0.692, respectively. We further show that privacy-aware representation learning preserves task performance at 0.781 while reducing these inference accuracies to 0.563, 0.467, and 0.206. These findings motivate purpose-limited representations and explicit privacy auditing in wearable neurohealth systems.

</details>


### [127] [Federated Trust for Embodied Robot Capability Marketplaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00404)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"联邦信任"（federated trust）作为机器人能力市场（robot skill marketplace）中软件包安全验证的架构模型，摒弃传统的中心化PKI/证书颁发机构方案，改为每个部署的桥（bridge）维护本地信任目录，使用Ed25519分离签名和SSH风格信任文件实现安装时本地验证，以适配异构监管环境、隔离部署和小规模运维团队等机器人车队的现实约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型驱动的机器人舰队日益依赖"机器人技能应用商店"（能力市场）进行软件部署。默认的云原生答案是中心化PKI（单一证书颁发机构、单一透明度日志、单一信任根），但机器人舰队面临异构监管制度、气隙部署、极少的运维人员以及物理世界中错误信任发布者的严重后果，中心化信任模型并不匹配这些特征。

Method: 每个部署的桥（bridge）维护自己的本地信任目录，记录可接受的签名者；签名者使用内含公钥的分离Ed25519签名信封标识自己；安装时验证为本地集合成员检查而非到证书颁发机构的网络往返。密码学原语采用标准方案（Ed25519分离签名和SSH风格信任文件），核心贡献在于架构上的组合方式。实现包含五子命令CLI、注册表服务器、每桥安装门控和80个测试。另实现了最小版本引脚（minimum-version pin）扩展以防御降级攻击。

Result: 多部署评估显示同一注册表流在不同信任目录的桥上产生不同的安装判定（该设计的关键属性）。在5000次对抗试验中，严格模式门控拒绝100%的恶意发布者、篡改、伪造和撤销签名攻击，在最小版本引脚扩展下拒绝96.6%的降级尝试。与Sigstore-Cosign和python-TUF的同硬件对比显示，联邦信任的每次验证成本介于两者之间，每个发布者的存储占用低于两者。

Conclusion: 中心化PKI不适合机器人舰队的安全验证场景，联邦信任模型（本地信任目录+标准密码学原语组合）为具身机器人部署提供了更合适的安全架构，在保持标准工具链的同时实现了按部署灵活分化的信任决策，并具备可验证的安全性和可比较的运行时性能。

Abstract: Robot capability marketplaces, the "app store for robot skills," are emerging as the deployment vector for LLM-driven robot fleets. The default cloud-native answer to "is this package safe to install?" is centralised PKI: one certificate authority, one transparency log, one root of trust. We argue this is the wrong model for embodied robot fleets, where operators face heterogeneous regulatory regimes, air-gapped deployments, tiny operator headcounts, and physical-world consequences for trusting the wrong publisher. We present federated trust: each deployed bridge maintains its own local trust directory of acceptable signers; signers identify themselves with a public key embedded in a detached Ed25519 signature envelope; install-time verification is a local set-membership check rather than a network round trip to a certificate authority. The cryptographic primitives are deliberately standard (Ed25519 detached signatures and SSH-style trust files); the contribution is the architectural commitment that this composition fits embodied robot fleets specifically. We implement the model in a runtime governance layer with a five-subcommand CLI, a registry server, a per-bridge install gate, and 80 tests. A multi-deployment evaluation shows the same registry stream producing divergent install verdicts on bridges with different trust directories, the load-bearing design property. Across 5000 adversarial trials, the strict-mode gate rejects 100% of rogue-publisher, tampered, forged, and revoked-signer attacks, and 96.6% of downgrade attempts under a minimum-version pin extension. A same-hardware comparison against Sigstore-Cosign and python-TUF locates federated trust's per-verify cost between the two and its per-publisher storage footprint below both.

</details>


### [128] [Don't Trust the Code, Check Its Effects: Runtime Refinement for Regenerated Systems Code Under an Adversarial Generator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00430)
*Jinhao Hu,Ashvin Goel,Laurent Bindschaedler*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对大型语言模型生成系统代码的"不可宽容"场景（效果不可逆、生成器可能对抗），提出由固定可信中介者独占所有效果执行权的参考监视器架构，生成代码仅做规划，并给出六个可中介性条件来界定此类中介干预的可行边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作利用LLM从规范生成系统代码，通过重新执行来验证生成代码的可信度，这适用于效果可恢复的"宽容"场景。但在系统代码效果不可逆且生成器可能具有对抗性的"不宽容"场景中，事后重执行无法检查效果，证明在其假设不成立时会静默失败，因此需要新的安全机制。

Method: 提出"拒绝给生成代码执行权"的核心立场：生成代码仅负责规划，由一个固定的可信中介者独占所有效果（写入和硬件命令）的所有权，只有当规范本应产生该效果时才予以执行。将该机制实例化为针对重新生成设备驱动的参考监视器，并定义了效果词汇表上的六种可中介性条件：可读性（legibility）、规范输入可观察性（spec-input observability）、可关联性（correlatability）、完备性（completeness）、结果可枚举性（outcome enumerability）和显式持久性（explicit durability）。

Result: 提出并形式化了"可中介性包络"（mediability envelope）概念，给出了决定此类安全中介是否可行的六个条件，并将这一框架具体实例化为重新生成的设备驱动的参考监视器架构。

Conclusion: 由于安全保证内建在固定可信的中介者而非可再生的代码中，该保障能够在代码重新生成后依然成立。六条件框架为判断何时可以实现这种安全中介提供了系统性依据。

Abstract: Recent work uses large language models to generate systems code from specifications, treating the specification as the durable artifact and the implementation as disposable. Regenerating the implementation specializes it to each workload and device. However, that work lives in a forgiving setting: a component's externally visible effects, its writes and device commands, are recoverable, and the generator is honest, so trust is discharged by re-execution. We target the unforgiving setting: systems code whose effects are irreversible, produced by a generator that may be adversarial. There, re-execution cannot check an effect after the fact, and a proof fails silently when its assumptions do. We take the position that the only safe way to operate here is to deny the generated code the authority to act. The generated code only plans, while a fixed trusted mediator owns every effect and performs one only when the specification would have produced it. Because the guarantee lives in the mediator, not the code, it survives regeneration. We instantiate this as a reference monitor for regenerated device drivers, and characterize the mediability envelope, six conditions on the effect vocabulary: legibility, spec-input observability, correlatability, completeness, outcome enumerability, and explicit durability. They decide when such mediation is possible.

</details>


### [129] [Capability-Gated Language Models: Security Composes, Utility Does Not](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00445)
*Patrikas Vanagas,Augustas Mačijauskas,Laurynas Lopata*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出"能力门控部署"（capability-gated deployment）：在单一模型权重内部实现按主体的访问控制，配置形成格结构（meet累积限制、join合并能力）。通过稀疏秩门控和嵌套因子分解机制实例化，并进行了安全性与实用性的实证分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言模型安全机制（安全微调、过滤、遗忘）其变体都存在于模型权重之外——过滤器可重新配置、安全层级可倍增、产物可重新发布；但在单一权重集内，所有请求面对的都是相同的模型配置。这缺乏按主体的细粒度访问控制能力。

Method: 定义能力门控部署：在单一权重集内实现按主体的访问控制，配置构成格（lattice）——meet运算是累积主体的限制，join运算是合并联盟的能力。实现上采用稀疏秩门控叠加在已有的嵌套因子分解机制上，通过单次归因引导配置搜索，所有结果从预先注册的留出测试集一次性读取。安全属性可组合，在单调诱导假设下可证明meet操作的安全性。

Result: 在两个模型谱系中，留出测试集上的meet操作加深了抑制效果，经过多重校正后仍存活的效应得到增强。但实用性不具组合性：单看无害的配置组合后可能导致保留率下降和流畅度受损，且不存在组合性上界。

Conclusion: 安全性可以组合（meet操作在假设下可证明），但实用性无法组合——说明能力门控部署在安全上有理论保障，在实际效用方面存在根本限制，需要谨慎设计配置的组合方式。

Abstract: Deployed language model safeguards (safety fine-tuning, filtering, unlearning) vary by principal only outside the model weights: filters are reconfigured, tiers are multiplied, and artefacts are reissued; inside one set of weights every request meets the same model configuration. This motivates us to define capability-gated deployment: per-principal access control inside one set of weights, whose configurations form a lattice - meets accumulate a principal's restrictions and joins pool a coalition's reach. We instantiate it by sparse rank gating over an existing nested-factorisation mechanism, guide profile search with one-pass attribution, and read every result once from a pre-registered held-out split. Security composes: provably at meets under a monotone-elicitation assumption we falsify pointwise. In two lineages the median held-out meet deepens suppression; the one effect surviving correction strengthens it. Utility does not: individually harmless profiles can compose to retention and fluency damage, and no compositional bound exists.

</details>


### [130] [Does Reasoning Mitigate Backdoor Attacks? A Neuro-Symbolic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00464)
*Marco Antonio Corallo,Andrea Agiollo,Mauro Conti,Alberto Giaretta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地评估了神经符号（NeSy）AI模型在后门攻击下的对抗鲁棒性，通过对比DeepProbLog与基线神经网络在八种后门设置和四种推理任务中的表现，发现NeSy模型虽在平均意义上比纯神经网络更鲁棒，但其鲁棒性高度依赖于推理过程的严格性及其与攻击目标的兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经符号AI被视为实现可信AI的新范式，其被普遍认为是"天然鲁棒"的（robust-by-design），但其神经-符号集成过程引入了额外的复杂度，可能成为攻击入口。此前缺乏对NeSy模型在对抗环境下（尤其是后门攻击）的系统性研究。

Method: 以最流行的NeSy框架DeepProbLog为研究对象，将其与基线神经网络进行对比，在总共八种后门攻击设置和四种推理任务上进行系统化的实证评估。

Result: 实验结果表明，NeSy模型平均而言确实比纯神经网络对手更具鲁棒性，但其鲁棒程度在很大程度上取决于推理过程执行的严格程度，以及推理过程与所选对抗目标的兼容性。

Conclusion: NeSy模型的对抗鲁棒性并非一成不变，需要针对具体推理机制和攻击场景进行深入分析与验证；研究代码已开源供复现与进一步研究。

Abstract: Neuro-Symbolic (NeSy) AI has recently emerged as a novel paradigm to enable trustworthy AI, aiming at integrating sub-symbolic neural perception with grounded symbolic reasoning. The neuro-symbolic integration process that characterizes these models has been proven beneficial to achieve more transparent, explainable and efficient AI systems. Meanwhile, their properties under adversarial settings have been overlooked being frequently deemed robust-by-design. However, the neural-symbolic integration process they leverage constitutes an additional layer of complexity that may provide an attack entry-point. Therefore, in this paper, we claim that an in-depth investigation of the adversarial robustness of NeSy models is necessary and provide the first systematic evaluation of backdoor attacks against NeSy. To this end, we compare the most popular NeSy framework, namely DeepProbLog, against baseline neural networks across a total of eight backdoor settings and four reasoning tasks. Our experimental results show that while NeSy models are indeed more robust than their neural counterpart on average, their robustness vastly depend on the strictness of the reasoning process being enforced and its compatibility with the chosen adversarial target. The source code to reproduce our experiments is made available at https://github.com/marcoantoniocorallo/NeSy-Backdoor.

</details>


### [131] [GlitchLab: A Hardware-in-the-Loop Optimizer for Physical Fault Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00502)
*Tanvir Hossain,Abhinav Mahadevan,Jasper Van Woudenberg,Rajesh Velegalati,Arindam Bhattacharyya*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出GlitchLab，一个在线硬件在环故障注入平台，并结合两种策略（基于Q学习的RL-Q和基于模型的自适应搜索SOBAS）来高效发现和复现硬件故障，在AES、密码和程序控制流攻击中均显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理故障注入可导致密钥恢复、认证绕过等安全失败，但有效故障难以发现：参数交互多、搜索空间大、成功设置稀疏且依赖目标、硬件尝试反馈有限。在固定测试时间内，高效搜索对评估故障敏感性至关重要。

Method: GlitchLab将延迟作为时序门控、电压和脉冲宽度作为严重性控制、硬件结果作为结构化反馈。实现两种策略：RL-Q（基于Q学习的强化学习）用于故障发现，SOBAS（基于结构化结果的自适应搜索）用于故障复现。

Result: 两种策略在所有AES、密码和控制流任务中均成功找到目标故障。在AES和控制流任务中，所需的尝试次数减少2-85倍，时间减少26-1,237倍；在密码任务中，基线在5,000次尝试内失败而本方法成功。SOBAS复现故障的频率提高7.3-21倍，RL-Q识别的AES设置多出30%。

Conclusion: 基于结构化反馈的自适应搜索策略能够显著提升物理故障注入的发现效率和复现能力，为硬件故障敏感性评估提供了高效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Physical fault injection can turn brief hardware disturbances into security failures such as key recovery, authentication bypass, and unintended control flow. Finding effective faults is difficult because many interacting parameters create a large search space, successful settings are sparse and target-dependent, and each hardware attempt provides limited feedback. Under fixed testing time, efficient search is therefore critical for assessing fault sensitivity.
  We present GlitchLab, an online hardware-in-the-loop platform that treats delay as a timing gate, voltage and pulse duration as severity controls, and hardware outcomes as structured feedback. It implements RL-Q (Q-learning-based reinforcement learning), a structured bandit for discovery, and Structured-Outcome-Based Adaptive Search (SOBAS), a model-based policy for fault reproduction.
  Both policies find a target fault in every AES, password, and control-flow campaign. On AES and control flow, they require 2-85x fewer attempts and 26-1,237x less time than the baselines; on password, both succeed while the baselines fail within 5,000 attempts. After discovery, SOBAS reproduces faults 7.3-21x more often, while RL-Q identifies 30% more distinct AES settings.

</details>


### [132] [The Safeguard Worked. Is the LLM System Safer?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00519)
*Pingyu Wu,Weiming Zhang,Nenghai Yu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文指出，已部署LLM服务的安全防护评估不应仅依赖本地测试指标（如拒绝率、攻击成功率、策略违规率），而应回答"攻击者持续适应后仍能获得多少有害帮助"这一部署层面的问题。论文认为证据要求存在强烈不对称性：一个成功的攻击即可证明有害帮助仍然存在，而要证明有害帮助几乎消弭则需要超出防护自身数据的外部证据，且只有少数深度编码的声明能提供此类证据。因此，局部评分提升并不自动等同于部署安全性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全防护评估指标（拒绝率、攻击成功率、策略违规率）只能描述防护在自己被测试请求上的表现，无法回答部署场景中的关键问题：面对持续适应的攻击者，服务实际上还能提供多少有害任务帮助。作者旨在弥合本地评估与部署安全性之间的鸿沟，建立统一的可比较评估框架。

Method: 论文通过分析不同防护家族在统一部署标准下的证据含义，考察了防护报告结果与部署问题的映射关系。作者采用深度编码方法对现存声明进行分类，区分哪些声明提供了关于防护本地功能的证据，哪些提供了关于周边系统在防护执行后仍允许什么的证据，并分析了两类证据的不对称性。

Result: 研究发现：一个成功获取有害帮助的攻击足以确立"有害帮助仍然存在"，且此类攻击在编码记录中反复出现；而证明有害帮助几乎消弭（残留有限）需要关于周围系统在防护后仍允许什么的证据，此类证据仅在少数深度编码声明中得到支持或推导。还发现一个此类声明界定了其范围内残留的上限。

Conclusion: 更好的防护本地评分本身并不构成关于部署安全性的更强主张。防护研究不应止步于提升局部评分，任何改进都必须以其是否让已部署系统真正更安全来评判。

Abstract: Safeguards in deployed LLM services are evaluated by refusal, attack success, and policy violation rates. Those rates characterize how a control performed on the requests it was tested on. A deployment has to answer a different question: how much help with harmful tasks the service still gives an attacker who keeps adapting or finds another way in. We determine what each reported result implies for that question, allowing results from different safeguard families to be compared under one deployment criterion. The evidence requirements are strongly asymmetric. One attack that obtains harmful help from the deployed service suffices to establish that such help remains, and such attacks appear repeatedly in the coded record. Establishing that little remains cannot follow from the safeguard's own numbers alone; it also requires evidence about what the surrounding system still allows after the safeguard performs its local function. Such evidence is supported or derived in only a small minority of the depth-coded claims, and one such claim bounds its scoped residual. A better local score is therefore not, by itself, a stronger claim about the deployment. Safeguard research cannot stop at raising local scores; a gain has to be judged by whether it makes a deployed system any safer.

</details>


### [133] [Transferable End-to-End Optimization for Indirect Long-Term Memory Poisoning in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00523)
*Chuanchao Zang,Jianing Wang,Wenyu Chen,Xiangtao Meng,Li Wang,Xinyu Gao,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PipePoison，将间接记忆投毒视为端到端优化问题，通过跨阶段反馈和链式损失来优化各阶段的瓶颈，显著提升攻击成功率与迁移性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有间接记忆投毒攻击多采用各阶段独立的内部优化，忽视了记忆写入、检索、利用各阶段之间的耦合关系——各阶段对同一投毒内容有不同要求，且上游变换可能抹除对下游的改进，因此攻击应被视为端到端优化问题。

Method: PipePoison从本地影子系统收集细粒度阶段反馈，利用链式结构损失识别并优化端到端成功率的瓶颈阶段，并采用稳定性校准的阶段权重与配置权重提升迁移性。

Result: 在三种智能体框架和四种记忆机制下，PipePoison将攻击利用率提升19.1个百分点；在完全未见过的受害配置上比最强基线高出16个百分点，且在八种代表性防御下仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 间接记忆投毒需要跨阶段的端到端视角而非各阶段独立优化，PipePoison验证了这一思路的有效性，展示了更强的攻击效果和跨配置泛化能力。

Abstract: Long-term memory can turn untrusted external content into persistent influence over an LLM agent's future decisions, creating the threat of indirect memory poisoning. A successful attack must survive a multi-stage pipeline comprising memory writing, retrieval, and utilization. Existing attacks largely rely on intra-stage optimization, optimizing individual stages in isolation while overlooking inter-stage coupling. Specifically, these stages impose different requirements on the same poisoning content, and each stage operates on the transformed output of its predecessor. Consequently, optimizing one stage may undermine the effectiveness of other stages, while upstream transformations may erase improvements intended for downstream stages. Indirect memory poisoning should therefore be viewed as an end-to-end optimization problem. Based on this insight, we present \textsc{PipePoison}, which collects fine-grained stage feedback from local shadow systems, uses chain-structured losses to identify and optimize the stage bottlenecking end-to-end success, and applies stability-calibrated stage and configuration weights to improve transferability. Across three agent frameworks and four memory mechanisms, \textsc{PipePoison} improves attack utilization rate by 19.1 percentage points. Even on fully unseen victim configurations, it outperforms the strongest baseline by 16 percentage points and remains effective under eight representative defenses.

</details>


### [134] [SoK: When Safe Agents Fail Together: The Security of Multi Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00595)
*Rui Yang,Junjie Xu,Zhengyu Liu,Neil Fendley,Yang Hong,Ziyang Li,Yinzhi Cao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过对197篇工作的执行级分析，系统化梳理了多智能体LLM系统（MAS）的安全问题，提出了A-I-R攻击分类框架和五部分防御契约，并审计了44个评估与基准工作，指出路径闭合与恢复仍是关键挑战，倡导以交互感知视角追踪攻击端到端路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统在不同主体边界间传递信息、状态、决策和权限，会产生局部检查无法发现的失败模式，而现有的本地安全措施不足以应对这类系统性风险。缺乏执行层面的视角，容易将多智能体场景误认为是真正多智能体安全效应的证据。

Method: 作者采用执行中心的分析方法，系统化梳理了197篇工作，涵盖六种交互接口、四种敌对方位置、七种系统级风险和八种反复出现的攻击路径。在此基础上提出A-I-R框架（按敌对方位置、交互接口和系统级风险组织攻击分类），并通过五部分防御契约（路径目标、观察、干预、信任边界、恢复）组织防御方案，最后审计了44个评估与基准工作。

Result: 研究识别出路径闭合和恢复是防御中面临的关键挑战。审计发现开放问题包括：隔离交互效应、设计可比较和可诊断的指标、支持跨MAS设计的复用，以及评估开放系统运行。这些发现表明需要交互感知的MAS安全观。

Conclusion: 安全的智能体可能集体失效，多智能体系统的安全需要端到端追踪攻击路径、测试防御是否实际闭合这些路径，并使用适当的反事实方法评估系统级效果，而非仅依赖局部检查或单个智能体的安全能力。

Abstract: Safe agents can fail together. Multi-agent LLM systems (MAS) move information, state, decisions, and authority across principal boundaries, creating failures that local checks may miss. Without an execution-level view, a multi-agent setting can easily be mistaken for evidence of a genuinely multi-agent security effect. We thus systematize MAS security through an execution-centered analysis of 197 works, covering six interaction interfaces, four adversary positions, seven system-level risks, and eight recurring attack paths. We introduce an A-I-R framework that organizes attacks by adversary position, interaction interface, and resulting system-level risk, unifying otherwise fragmented attack mechanisms across MAS. We organize defenses through a five-part contract covering path target, observation, intervention, trust boundary, and recovery, and identify path closure and recovery as key challenges. We audit 44 evaluation and benchmark works and identify open challenges in isolating interaction effects, designing comparable and diagnostic metrics, supporting reuse across MAS designs, and evaluating open-system operation. Together, these findings motivate an interaction-aware view of MAS security: trace attacks end to end, test whether defenses close those paths, and evaluate system-level effects with appropriate counterfactuals.

</details>


### [135] [NeuroGraph: An AI Graph-Driven Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Explainable Threat Reasoning in Advanced Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00604)
*Padmeswari Nandiya,Ahmad Mohsin,Ahmed Ibrahim,Iqbal H. Sarker,Helge Janicke*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个图为基础的神经符号框架，结合本体感知符号查询生成、知识图谱检索和神经语言生成，采用双大语言模型架构（一个将自然语言转为Cypher查询用于图谱检索，另一个从图谱证据生成答案），用于先进制造中IT/OT环境的网络威胁情报分析，实验显示在推理准确率、减少幻觉和多跳推理方面优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先进制造中网络物理攻击面日益复杂，传统基于文本的大型语言模型和RAG方法容易产生幻觉，缺乏对互联威胁的结构化推理能力。现有图RAG方法缺少本体一致的多跳推理和跨异构网络安全数据的透明证据追踪。

Method: 提出图为基础的神经符号框架，采用双大语言模型架构：第一个模型将自然语言问题转换为可执行的Cypher查询进行符号图检索，第二个模型严格基于检索到的图谱证据生成答案，实现本体感知的符号推理与神经生成的结合。

Result: 在公开的网络威胁情报基准上，相比已有基线在推理准确率上有一致提升，减少了幻觉，增强了多跳推理能力，并提高了对对抗性扰动的鲁棒性。运行时和可解释性分析显示框架保持交互式推理性能，能暴露图为基础的推理痕迹供分析人员检查验证。

Conclusion: 图为基础的神经符号推理有望成为面向下一代Industry 5.0环境中可扩展、可解释且可靠的网络威胁情报分析方案。

Abstract: The growing complexity of cyber-physical attack surfaces in advanced manufacturing has made cyber threat intelligence analysis increasingly difficult. Although large language models and retrieval-augmented generation have improved CTI workflows, text-based approaches remain vulnerable to hallucinations and provide limited support for structured reasoning over interconnected threats. Graph-based RAG reduces some of these limitations, but existing approaches often lack ontology-consistent multi-hop reasoning and transparent evidence tracing across heterogeneous cybersecurity data. This paper proposes a graph-grounded neuro-symbolic framework that integrates ontology-aware symbolic query generation, knowledge graph retrieval, and neural language generation to support accurate and explainable threat analysis across information technology and operational technology environments. The framework adopts a dual-large language model architecture: the first model translates natural-language questions into executable Cypher queries for symbolic graph retrieval, while the second generates answers strictly from the retrieved graph evidence. Experimental evaluation using publicly available cyber threat intelligence benchmarks shows consistent improvements over the published baseline in reasoning accuracy, while also reducing hallucinations, strengthening multi-hop reasoning, and improving robustness to adversarial perturbations. Runtime and explainability analyses further demonstrate that the framework maintains interactive inference performance and exposes graph-grounded reasoning artifacts that allow analysts to inspect and verify each stage of the analysis. Overall, the results highlight the potential of graph-grounded neuro-symbolic reasoning as a scalable, interpretable, and reliable approach to cyber threat intelligence for next-generation Industry 5.0 environments.

</details>


### [136] [A Version Space Approach for Digital Circuit Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00609)
*Mitchell A. Thornton*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的版本空间视角方法，将数字电路分析中的两个看似无关的问题——概率组合等价性检查和逻辑锁定密钥计数——统一在同一框架下。通过将候选重新参数化为块和，使嵌套系数之间的依赖变为局部性，利用和积递推在多项式时间内精确计数存活函数，闭合了2002年方法遗留的指数枚举难题，并在TrustHub基准上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多数字电路问题本质相同：在隐藏对象与一组观测一致的前提下，统计仍一致的候选数量并决定下一步观测。2002年提出的方法仅解决了概率组合等价性检查中两个特例，一般情形需要枚举观测数量的指数级复杂度。作者希望开发统一的版本空间方法，同时解决两个常被视为无关的问题。

Method: 提出版本空间计算框架：将候选布尔函数作为候选集，观测为修正Haar谱系数；通过将候选重新参数化为块和（block sums），把嵌套系数间的依赖转化为局部性，采用和积（sum-product）递推在真值表大小的多项式时间内精确计数存活函数。对于逻辑锁定密钥计数，同一递推在门级因子图上运行来计算与查询一致的密钥数。

Result: 对单系数、系数对以及每个祖先闭集均导出了闭合形式；独立近似的误差等于可计算的格指数。所有公式均与穷举枚举对照验证，复现了2002年的数据表。逻辑锁定方面，在TrustHub混淆发布的七十个实例上，存活熵每次都低于宣称的密钥长度。

Conclusion: 两个应用实为同一种方法：见证者提供观测，每个观测移除候选，版本空间被精确计数。该统一框架不仅闭合了2002年工作的遗留问题，还展示了其在多种电路分析任务中的普适性。

Abstract: Many questions about a digital circuit take the same form. A hidden object is consistent with a set of observations, and one wants to know how many remain consistent and which observation to make next. The set of surviving candidates is the version space, and its size, on a logarithmic scale, measures how much the observations have settled. This paper develops the version-space view as one method and applies it to two circuit-analysis problems usually treated as unrelated. The first is probabilistic combinational equivalence checking, where the candidates are Boolean functions and the observations are modified-Haar spectral coefficients. A method proposed in 2002 posed this counting problem and solved only two special cases, leaving the general case an enumeration exponential in the number of observations. We close it. A reparameterization onto block sums turns the dependence among nested coefficients into locality, a sum--product recursion counts the surviving functions exactly in time polynomial in the truth-table size, closed forms follow for a single coefficient, a coefficient pair, and every ancestor-closed set, and the error of the independence approximation the 2002 work resorted to equals a computable lattice index. Every formula is checked against exhaustive enumeration and reproduces the 2002 tables. The second application is key counting for logic-locked netlists, where the candidates are keys and the observations are oracle responses. The same recursion, run over the gate-level factor graph, computes the number of keys still consistent with a set of queries; across seventy instances of the TrustHub obfuscation release the surviving entropy falls below the advertised key length every time. The two applications are one method: a witness supplies observations, each removes candidates, and the version space is counted exactly.

</details>


### [137] [Automating Static Code Analysis Through CI/CD Pipeline Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00676)
*Zachary Wadhams,Ann Marie Reinhold,Clemente Izurieta*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通用的自动化流程，用于聚合SAST工具的输出并将其集成到开发者熟悉的问题追踪软件中，以提升软件开发生命周期中安全漏洞的识别与修复效率，并在SonarQube与GitLab环境中成功实现验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAST工具虽能检测安全漏洞，但因误报率高、缺乏原生流水线支持等可用性问题导致采用率低，亟需一种方式让安全漏洞在开发早期被有效识别和修复。

Method: 提出一种新颖、通用且自动化的流程，将SAST工具输出进行聚合，并集成到开发者熟悉的问题追踪软件中，从而在开发生命周期中简化漏洞识别与沟通。

Result: 在基于GitLab的开发环境中使用SonarQube SAST工具成功实现了该流程。开发者对结构化实现、实时反馈和主动漏洞管理持积极态度；尽管存在学习曲线及安全编码与工作流中断之间的权衡等挑战，整体对安全意识和响应能力产生了积极影响。

Conclusion: 该流程在增强软件开发实践的安全态势方面具有潜力，能够提升开发者的安全意识和漏洞响应效率。

Abstract: In the contemporary landscape of software devel-opment, securing sensitive data is paramount to safeguarding organizational reputation, preventing financial losses, and pro-tecting individuals from identity theft. This paper addresses the pervasive challenge of identifying and rectifying security vulnerabilities early in the development process, emphasizing the role of Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools. While SAST tools play a crucial role in detecting vulnerabilities, widespread adoption has been hindered by usability issues, including high false positive rates and a lack of native pipeline support. This paper proposes a novel, generalized, and automated process for aggregating SAST tool outputs and integrating them into developers' familiar issue-tracking software. The process streamlines the identification and communication of security vulnerabilities during the development lifecycle, facilitating more efficient remediation efforts. We demonstrate the successful implementation of the proposed process with the SonarQube SAST tool in a GitLab-based development environment. Developers were positive about the structured implementation, real-time feedback, and proactive vulnerability management. However, despite some challenges such as a potential learning curve and tradeoffs between secure coding and workflow disruption, the overall positive impact on security awareness and responsiveness suggests that the proposed process holds promise in enhancing the security posture of software development practices

</details>


### [138] [PhantomCall: Evading ML Malware Detectors via Function Call Graph Perturbation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00705)
*Md Ajwad Akil,Adrian Shuai Li,Imtiaz Karim,Arun Iyengar,Ashish Kundu,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PhantomCall，一种黑盒攻击方法，通过注入可执行dummy函数扰动Windows PE恶意软件的函数调用图（FCG），实现对三类结构不同的恶意软件检测器的高成功率规避攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Windows PE恶意软件检测器的对抗攻击只关注原始字节、PE头或函数内部控制流图，尚未探索函数调用图（FCG）这一攻击面，而FCG结构是图基恶意软件检测器的重要特征。

Method: PhantomCall通过在目标调用点注入完全可执行的dummy函数，同时向CFG和FCG添加新节点和新边，并在保持程序语义不变的前提下，结合分类器引导搜索和可调注入参数进行结构扰动。

Result: 在2025年收集的Windows恶意软件语料上，对MalConv、MalGraph和SAFE+GNN三个检测器进行测试，最佳PhantomCall变体在所有配置下达到85-100%的攻击成功率，相比此前最优方法在MalGraph上最高提升14.78个百分点、在SAFE+GNN上提升95.5个百分点，平均生成规避变体的速度快2.9倍。多数规避仅需修改单一调用点，86-97%的变体在沙箱语义测试中保持原始恶意行为。

Conclusion: PhantomCall证明了FCG是有效且此前被忽视的攻击表面，在跨架构检测器上实现高效、高速且语义保持的对抗规避，揭示了图基恶意软件检测器的脆弱性。

Abstract: Prior adversarial attacks on Windows PE malware detectors target raw bytes, PE headers, or intra-function control-flow graphs, leaving the function call graph (FCG) unexplored as an attack surface. Yet the FCG structure is an important feature in graph-based malware detectors. We present Phan- tomCall, a black-box attack that perturbs the FCG of Windows PE malware by injecting fully executable dummy functions at targeted call sites, adding new nodes and edges to both the CFG and FCG while preserving program semantics. We pair this structural perturbation with classifier-guided search and tunable injection parameters, effective across three archi- tecturally distinct classifiers. Evaluated on a 2025-collected Windows malware corpus against MalConv (raw-byte CNN), MalGraph (graph-based GNN), and SAFE+GNN (pure FCG GNN trained from scratch on a 2024 corpus) at two FPR thresholds, the best PhantomCall variant achieves 85-100% attack success rate across all configurations, exceeding prior state-of-the-art by up to 14.78 percentage points on MalGraph and 95.5 percentage points on SAFE+GNN, and generating evasive variants up to 2.9x faster on average across all targets. For MalConv and MalGraph, the majority of evasions require only a single call site modification, and 86-97% of evaluated evasive variants preserve the original malicious behavior in sandbox-based semantic testing across all configurations.

</details>


### [139] [Differentially Private Paired Table-Image Multimodal Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00708)
*Kai Chen,Josephine Lamp,Somesh Jha,Tianhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了DP-TabImage，一个用于差分隐私下表格-图像配对数据联合合成的模态特化框架，通过分解联合分布并采用私有概率图模型与DP-SGD训练的扩散模型，实现了表格保真度、图像保真度和跨模态对齐之间的良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多数据集同时包含图像与多变量表格记录的配对数据，但现有差分隐私合成工作往往只单独研究表格数据或图像数据，缺乏针对两种模态数据配对联合合成的方案。核心挑战在于两种模态倾向于不同的私有学习机制，且同时需要保持它们之间的依赖关系。

Method: DP-TabImage将联合分布分解为p(x,y)=p_T(y)p_I(x|y)：使用私有概率图模型建模多变量表格分布p_T(y)，使用DP-SGD训练的表格条件扩散模型建模条件图像分布p_I(x|y)。为在裁剪和加噪梯度下促进条件学习，该方法在私有表格-图像原型上进行预训练，将私有构建的属性条件图像与从已经私有的表格模型中推导出的表格向量配对，且不增加额外的隐私成本。

Result: 在三个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，DP-TabImage在表格保真度、图像保真度和跨模态对齐之间取得了较强的平衡。分析进一步揭示：视觉预热主要改善边际图像保真度，而对齐的表格-图像预热对于改善跨模态对应关系至关重要。

Conclusion: DP-TabImage有效解决了差分隐私下异构模态配对数据合成的问题，通过模态特化设计实现了私有条件下的多模态数据合成，为隐私保护的数据生成研究提供了新思路。

Abstract: Differentially private (DP) synthesis has been extensively studied for tabular and image data separately, yet many real-world datasets contain images paired with multivariate tabular records. Synthesizing such data is particularly challenging under DP, as the two modalities favor different private learning mechanisms while their dependence must also be preserved. To address this challenge, we propose DP-TabImage, a modality-specialized framework for private paired synthesis. DP-TabImage instantiates the factorization $p(x,y)=p_T(y)p_I(x\;|\;y)$ using a private Probabilistic Graphical Model for the multivariate table distribution and a table-conditioned diffusion model trained with DP-SGD for the conditional image distribution. To facilitate conditional learning under clipped and noisy gradients, we further pretrain the model on private table-image prototypes, pairing privately constructed attribute-conditioned images with tabular vectors derived from the already private tabular model at no additional privacy cost. Experiments on three real-world datasets show that DP-TabImage achieves a strong balance among tabular fidelity, image fidelity, and cross-modal alignment. Our analysis further reveals that visual warm-up primarily improves marginal image fidelity, whereas aligned table-image warm-up is critical for improving cross-modal correspondence. Our source code is available in the GitHub repository, https://github.com/KaiChen9909/TabImage_Syn.

</details>


### [140] [SoK: Motion Data Privacy in Extended Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00711)
*Azim Ibragimov,Alina Vasina,Uliana Polshcha,Eric D. Ragan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对XR头显记录的动捕隐私进行了系统化综述，整理了134篇相关论文，涵盖攻击途径、推理风险和保护方法，并提出了一套威胁模型、攻击防御分类体系以及未来研究指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XR设备必须通过动捕为用户提供沉浸式体验，但这会在不经意间记录个人运动模式，进而泄露敏感信息。先前关于XR隐私的系统化研究较为宽泛，未将动捕隐私作为独立领域对待；而该领域文献自上次综述以来已增长近四倍，亟需专门的系统性梳理。

Method: 研究者审查了134篇关于XR头显动捕隐私的相关论文，从攻击者如何获取运动模式、可从中推断出的信息以及保护方法三个角度进行分析。基于综述结果，综合出运动模态、表示和推理风险的分类体系，构建了XR运动威胁模型，系统化了攻防方法，识别了文献空白，并为未来研究提供了评估指南。
