Result: 本文系统化整理了XR动捕隐私的文献现状，形成了运动模态/表示/推理风险的分类体系，提出了XR运动威胁模型，并明确了攻防方法谱系及现有文献中的研究空白。

Conclusion: 通过该综述澄清了XR运动隐私研究的当�前状态，并为未来动捕隐私保护机制的评估提出了可操作的建议。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) provides immersive, interactive 3D experiences. To enable these experiences, the devices must track user motion so the system can respond to actions such as grabbing, looking at, or moving an object. However, motion tracking has raised privacy concerns since it records a person's motion patterns. These motion patterns have been studied extensively across various fields (i.e., gait identification and profiling) and have been shown to reveal sensitive information. With the adoption of XR, these patterns became easier to record and obtain than ever. This creates a fundamental privacy tension: motion tracking enables core XR functionality yet requires users to compromise their privacy. Prior systematization-of-knowledge (SoK) studies on XR privacy have examined the field broadly, with motion-related research distributed across several privacy domains rather than treated as a distinct area of study. However, XR motion privacy has gained significant momentum since the prior SoK, with the literature nearly quadrupling in size and thereby warranting a dedicated systematization of this topic. This SoK examines 134 relevant papers on privacy concerns in motion patterns recorded by XR headsets, including how adversaries can obtain users' motion patterns, the inferences they can draw from them, and methods for protecting users. Based on this review, we synthesize a taxonomy of motion modalities, representations, and inference risks; develop an XR motion threat model; systematize the attack and defense approaches in the XR motion literature; identify gaps in the literature; and provide guidelines for future studies evaluating motion privacy mechanisms. Together, our SoK clarifies the state of XR motion privacy and provides recommendations for future evaluations.

</details>


### [141] [MROP: Mask-Region Optimized Purification Against Backdoor Attack in Deep JSCC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00786)
*Seongkyu Yang,Hyeonho Noh,Hyun Jong Yang,Jonggyu Jang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对深度联合信源信道编码（JSCC）图像传输系统中输入补丁后门攻击的安全漏洞问题，提出了一种无需重新训练的推理阶段防御方法MROP（掩码区域优化净化）。该方法通过在编码器输入端放置逐像素掩码，利用Gumbel-sigmoid松弛优化来定位触发器并净化图像，在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上的实验表明，该方法在显著降低攻击成功率（ASR）的同时保持了干净重建的PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度JSCC将图像通过端到端DNN直接映射为信道符号进行传输，其流水线如同黑箱，接收端无法及时发现图像被篡改后引入的安全攻击（特别是输入补丁后门攻击）。现有的补丁触发器防御大多面向分类任务设计，而JSCC的重建场景尚未得到有效解决，因此需要开发针对重建任务的防御方法。

Method: 提出掩码区域优化净化（MROP）方法。与从输入-输出梯度定位触发器的基线方法（梯度掩码防御）不同，MROP在编码器输入处放置逐像素掩码，通过Gumbel-sigmoid松弛来优化掩码以定位触发器区域，随后细化触发区域以实现更好的纯净图像重建。该方法在推理阶段运行，无需重新训练JSCC模型。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和STL-10数据集上，结合DeepJSCC和SwinJSCC模型进行实验评估。结果表明，与梯度掩码防御基线相比，所提出的MROP方法能显著降低攻击成功率（ASR），同时保持干净重建图像的峰值信噪比（PSNR）。

Conclusion: MROP方法有效填补了深度JSCC重建场景中针对输入补丁后门攻击的防御空白，为深度JSCC系统的安全性提供了可靠的推理阶段解决方案，兼顾了防御有效性和重建质量。

Abstract: Deep joint source and channel coding (JSCC) transmits a source by mapping it directly to channel symbols through an end-to-end deep neural network (DNN) and reconstructing it at the receiver. Taking image transmission as an application, this DNN pipeline behaves as a black box: the receiver cannot readily detect security attacks when the transmitted images are corrupted, thereby introducing a new security vulnerability. In this letter, we study defense against input-patch backdoor attacks on deep JSCC, in which a small trigger patch attached to the input forces the decoder to emit an attacker-chosen target image. Most existing patch-trigger defenses are designed for classification, leaving the reconstruction setting of deep JSCC unaddressed. We adapt the gradient mask defense to this reconstruction setting as a baseline and then propose mask-region optimized purification (MROP), which operates at inference and requires no retraining of the JSCC model. Unlike the baseline, which localizes the trigger from the input--output gradient, MROP instead places a per-pixel mask at the encoder input and optimizes it via a Gumbel-sigmoid relaxation to localize the trigger, then refines the trigger region to reconstruct the pure images better. In numerical results, we evaluate the proposed method on CIFAR-10 and STL-10 datasets along with the DeepJSCC and SwinJSCC models. By doing so, we show that the proposed method substantially lowers the attack success rate (ASR) while preserving the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) of clean reconstructions.

</details>


### [142] [RISA: Response Inspection and Selective Actions for Refusal Calibration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00790)
*Wenhan Chang,Tianqing Zhu,Ping Xiong,Shiyi Liao,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出RISA框架，一种推理时对齐方法，通过检查LLM的初始响应并选择性纠正拒答错误（无需更新模型参数），显著提升拒绝行为的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时对齐方法大多在不先判断初始响应是否已恰当的情况下进行干预，可能破坏正确的拒绝或有用的回答。需要一种能识别提示意图语义变化、适应不同基座模型的选择性干预方法。

Method: RISA采用两阶段策略：先用固定上下文规则对明确案例进行拒答评分；对未匹配案例，利用基于校准线性探针从模型最终层隐藏状态推导拒答分数；并根据不同基座模型分别校准探针分数、表征支持边界及动作阈值。运行时不将提示分数与初始拒答状态结合，仅在必要时通过动作策略进行干预。

Result: 实验结果表明，RISA在提升拒答可靠性的同时，大幅保留了模型效用，提供了一种实用的响应感知式拒答校准解决方案。

Conclusion: RISA通过响应检查与选择性干预，实现了无需更新基础模型参数的推理时拒答校准，在保证安全拒绝的同时减少了对正常回答的干扰，为LLM对齐提供了新的实用路径。

Abstract: Reliable refusal behavior requires Large Language Models (LLMs) to reject harmful prompts with only answering benign ones. Incorrect refusal behavior can either expose users to harmful responses or prevent users from obtaining useful answers. Training-time alignment improves refusal behavior by updating model parameters with safety data, but requires additional computation and training. In contrast, inference-time alignment aims to modify LLM behavior during inference without updating the underlying model parameters. Existing inference-time methods mainly rely on in-context safety prompting, activation steering, or decoding control. However, most of them intervene without first determining whether the initial response is already appropriate, potentially altering a correct refusal or a useful answer. Effective selective intervention therefore requires identifying prompt intent beyond sensitive keywords, covering semantic variations that fixed rules may miss, and adapting the verifier to different base models. To address these challenges, we propose Response Inspection and Selective Actions (RISA), an inference-time framework that inspects the initial response and selectively corrects refusal errors without updating the base model. RISA first uses fixed contextual rules to assign refusal scores to clear cases. For unmatched cases, it derives a refusal score from the final-layer prompt hidden state using a calibrated linear probe. To adapt to different base models, RISA separately calibrates the probe score, representation-support boundary, and action thresholds. At runtime, RISA combines the prompt score with the initial refusal status and applies an action policy to intervene only when necessary. Experimental results demonstrate that RISA improves refusal reliability while largely preserving model utility, offering a practical solution for response-aware refusal calibration in LLMs.

</details>


### [143] [Effective Interventions Against AI-Enhanced Scams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00806)
*Kyle Fredrickson*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究AI驱动的新一代诈骗模式下的有效干预策略，通过构建诈骗利润模型分析发现，报告率、报告集中化和报告准确度三个杠杆对诈骗渠道的预期受害者数量呈乘数效应，即使对高价值诈骗基础设施的中等报告率也能显著影响诈骗盈利性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2025年全球诈骗直接损失估计达4420亿美元，美国报告损失在2020至2025年间增长了近400%。AI在诈骗中的使用显著改变了诈骗的经济学特征和运营瓶颈，因此需要重新审视哪些干预措施在新模式下仍然有效。

Method: 构建了一个简单的诈骗利润模型，用以分析不同干预措施如何渐进地影响诈骗运营，重点考察报告率、报告集中化和报告准确度三个杠杆对每诈骗渠道预期受害者数量、收入及成本的影响。

Result: 三个杠杆（报告率、报告集中化、报告准确度）对每诈骗渠道的预期受害者数量呈乘数效应，在降低每渠道收入的同时增加成本。由于效应相乘，同时作用于三个维度的干预可能对诈骗商业模式盈利性产生显著影响。分析表明，即使针对高价值诈骗基础设施的中等报告率也能显著降低诈骗盈利。

Conclusion: 在AI驱动的诈骗新范式下，针对报告机制的多种干预手段组合运用可产生乘数式的打击效果，其中关注高价值诈骗基础设施的报告策略尤为有效，这为政策制定者提供了新的干预方向。

Abstract: In 2025, scams were responsible for an estimated $442 billion in direct losses globally. In the United States, reported losses increased by nearly 400% between 2020 and 2025. Though AI in scamming is a relatively new phenomenon, its use significantly changes the economics of scams as well as the bottlenecks in scam operations. In this paper I investigate what interventions will remain effective under this new AI-driven scamming regime.
  I develop a simple model of scam profits to understand how different interventions asymptotically affect scam operations. I find that three levers--reporting rate, centralization of reporting, and report accuracy--multiply in their effect on expected victims per scam channel, reducing revenue per scam channel while increasing costs. Because effects multiply, interventions affecting all three could have a significant effect on the profitability of the scam business model. My analysis suggests that even modest reporting rates against high-value scam infrastructure could have significant impacts on scam profitability.

</details>


### [144] [Using LLMs to Elicit Security Requirements for Service-Oriented Cyber Ranges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00886)
*Michail Takaronis,Athanasia Kollarou,Georgios Kavallieratos,Vasileios Gkioulos,Sokratis Katsikas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究探索利用大语言模型（LLMs）辅助面向服务的网络靶场（SOR）安全需求获取。研究者基于SEBoK指南，向GPT-5.2等五个LLM提供安全任务目标和利益相关者需求作为提示上下文，生成84条安全需求并整合为27条，映射到架构层后由5位安全专家评估。结果显示必要性（98.5%）、清晰性（87.4%）、完整性（85.2%）、可行性（78.5%）接受率高，拒绝率仅0.7%，但可测试性较低（44.4%），说明LLM可支持需求获取早期阶段但仍需人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向服务的网络靶场（SOR）涉及众多交互组件和利益相关者，各自具有不同的安全关切。这些安全关切需要转化为安全需求，而需求获取过程通常困难且耗时，尤其当训练场景针对关键基础设施时。因此需要一个更高效的方法来辅助安全需求获取。

Method: 采用SEBoK指导流程：首先识别安全任务目标和利益相关者需求，将这些连同架构指南作为提示上下文提供给五个LLM（GPT-5.2、Gemini 3.1 Pro、Grok 4.1、Sonar和Kimi K2.5）。模型共生成84条安全需求，经整合为27条综合需求并映射到SOR架构层。最后由5位网络安全专家按必要性、清晰性、完整性、可行性、可测试性五个标准评估，并设拒绝选项。

Result: 专家评估显示高接受率：必要性98.5%、清晰性87.4%、完整性85.2%、可行性78.5%，拒绝率仅0.7%。但可测试性仅为44.4%，表明需求在测试方面信息不足。5个LLM共生成84条需求，整合为27条最终需求集。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效支持安全需求获取的早期阶段，为设计者和开发者提供有价值的基线。然而，可测试性维度的不足表明仍需人工审查和调整，特别是需要补充如何测试这些需求的信息，LLM不能完全取代人类专家在需求工程中的角色。

Abstract: Cyber ranges are complex environments comprising many interacting components and stakeholders with different security concerns. The Service-Oriented Cyber Range (SOR) is no exception, particularly when it comes to training scenarios targeting critical infrastructure. Security concerns are translated into security requirements, the elicitation of which is usually difficult and time-consuming. This work examines how large language models can assist in eliciting security requirements for a service-oriented range and help produce a useful baseline for designers and developers. The approach follows a SEBoK-guided process in which security mission objectives and stakeholder needs were first identified and then provided as a prompt context along with architectural guidelines to five LLMs: GPT-5.2, Gemini 3.1 Pro, Grok 4.1, Sonar, and Kimi K2.5. The models generated 84 security requirements in total, which were consolidated into a comprehensive set of 27 requirements and then mapped to the architectural layers of the service-oriented range. The final set was evaluated by five cybersecurity experts against the criteria of necessity, clarity, completeness, feasibility, and testability, with an additional rejection option. The results showed a high acceptance rate, specifically for necessity with 98.5%, clarity with 87.4%, completeness with 85.2%, feasibility with 78.5%, and rejection with 0.7%. Testability was lower at 44.4%, indicating a slight lack of information on how these requirements could be tested. These findings show that LLMs can support early stages of the elicitation of security requirements, although human review is still needed, especially to improve or adjust certain aspects of the requirements.

</details>


### [145] [Influence of Logging Frameworks on Bind9](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00954)
*Max Schrötter,Hannes Signer,Bettina Schnor*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出FIPS，一种专为高性能日志设计的新IPC机制，通过绕过内核和减少日志消息拷贝，解决了主机入侵防御系统在高流量下日志系统成为性能瓶颈、攻击者可利用日志丢失隐藏痕迹的问题。评估表明FIPS相比文件日志能使IPS封禁恶意客户端快2.5倍，且支持每秒一百万请求负载下处理216个攻击客户端。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于主机的入侵防御系统依赖应用日志检测和阻止恶意活动，但在现代高速网络中日志子系统本身成为瓶颈——攻击者仅需产生足够流量淹没应用的日志管道即可隐藏攻击痕迹，如Fail2Ban监控BIND9在不到65Mbps的DNS流量下即可被击败。作者认为需要设计更高效的日志传输机制来消除这一安全漏洞。

Method: 作者首先证明即使将IPS架构核心组件替换为高性能方案（如iptables配合eBPF、正则匹配使用Hyperscan），日志后端仍然是瓶颈。由此提出FIPS——一种绕过内核且将日志消息拷贝降至最低的高性能IPC机制。FIPS采用每线程无锁共享内存环形缓冲区，支持多个独立消费者按各自速率读取同一日志流，同时提供原生API和syslog接口的即插即用替代方案。

Result: 基于BIND9的评估表明：FIPS相比禁用日志只引入几乎可忽略的开销；FIPS记录的日志请求量超过所有其他被评估的日志框架；相比文件日志，FIPS使IPS封禁恶意客户端快2.5倍，并能在每秒一百万请求的负载下稳定支持216个攻击源。

Conclusion: 日志机制不应成为IPS的安全短板。FIPS通过高效的无锁共享内存设计和最小化数据拷贝，在保持极低开销的同时提供高吞吐和低延迟的日志能力，不仅能防御高速DDoS流量下的日志淹没攻击，还能显著加快IPS对恶意客户端的响应速度，为主机入侵防御系统在高性能网络环境中的可靠运行提供了实际可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Host-based Intrusion Prevention Systems (IPS) rely on application logs to detect and block malicious activity. However, on modern high-speed networks the logging subsystem itself becomes a bottleneck: an attacker can hide traces simply by generating enough traffic to overwhelm the application's log pipeline, dropping crucial traces. In this work, we show that widely deployed setups such as Fail2Ban monitoring BIND9 can be defeated with less than 65 Mbps of DNS traffic. Further, we show that when replacing core components of the IPS architecture with their higher-performance equivalent, iptables with eBPF and regex matching with Hyperscan, the logging backends themselves become the bottleneck. Therefore, we present FIPS, a new IPC designed for high-performance logging that bypasses the kernel and reduces copying of the log messages to a minimum. FIPS uses per-thread lock free shared memory ring buffers, supporting multiple independent consumers reading the same log stream at their own pace. FIPS offers both a native API and a drop-in replacement for the syslog interface. Our evaluation with BIND 9 shows that FIPS introduces almost no overhead compared to disabled logging, logs more requests than any other evaluated framework, and enables the IPS to ban malicious clients $2.5\times$ faster than with file logging while sustaining $2^{16}$ attacking clients at one million requests per second.

</details>


### [146] [AKRASIA: Stealthy Backdoor Attack on Reasoning-based Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01023)
*Chou Jin Chua,Sarang Nambiar,Murali Srinivasan,Ezekiel Soremekun*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AKRASIA，一种针对基于推理的代码大语言模型的隐蔽推理时后门攻击方法，通过探询受害者模型构建代码级触发器，利用上下文学习进行后门学习，并借助模型不忠实现隐藏触发器和生成可信推理，在多个先进LLM上达到高达99.34%的攻击成功率同时有效规避自动化防御和人工审查。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有后门攻击主要针对普通LLM，而基于推理的Code LLM（具备推理能力的代码模型）引入了安全验证层等防御机制，传统后门攻击容易被检测。作者旨在探索一种能在推理层面隐藏后门且具备高隐蔽性的攻击方法，以揭示此类模型面临的安全威胁。

Method: AKRASIA攻击包含三个核心步骤：(1)通过探询受害者LLM构建代码级后门触发器；(2)利用上下文学习（ICL）进行后门学习；(3)利用模型不忠实性（model unfaithfulness）隐藏后门触发器并生成看似合理的推理过程，从而同时规避自动化防御和人类审查。

Result: 在4个后门目标、6个推理LLM、3个编码任务/数据集和3种防御方法的评估中，AKRASIA在SOTA LLM上平均攻击成功率高达99.34%，同时保持高达97.23%的平均准确率；在14/18种防御设置下保留高达98.82%的平均攻击成功率，成功规避SOTA防御；在高达80%的设置中成功躲过人类审查，隐藏后门触发器和推理步骤。

Conclusion: AKRASIA揭示了对推理型Code LLM进行后门攻击的可行性与隐蔽性，表明当前自动化防御和人工审查难以识别此种推理层面的后门威胁，凸显了开发针对推理后门防御机制的紧迫性和重要性。

Abstract: We present AKRASIA, a stealthy, inference-time backdoor attack against reasoning-based Code LLMs. AKRASIA aims to achieve a backdoor target (e.g., malicious code execution) in reasoning LLMs while evading automated defenses and human inspection. To achieve this, AKRASIA probes the victim LLM to construct a code-level backdoor trigger. It then employs in-context learning for backdoor learning, and model unfaithfulness to conceal the backdoor trigger, and generate plausible reasoning. We evaluate AKRASIA using four backdoor targets six (6) reasoning LLMs, three coding tasks/datasets and three defense methods. AKRASIA has up to 99.34% average attack success rate on SOTA LLMs and mantains up to 97.23% average accuracy. AKRASIA evades the SOTA defense, retaining up to 98.82% average ASR in most (14/18) defense settings. It evades human inspection, successfully hiding the backdoor trigger and reasoning steps in up to 80% of settings. Our findings motivate the need to defend LLMs against reasoning backdoors.

</details>


### [147] [MultiGait: A Multi-Sensor Multi-Perspective Multi-Session Biometric Inference Benchmark and its Dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01036)
*Julian Todt,Felix Morsbach,Philip Dissert,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 作者构建了MultiGait，首个多传感器、多视角、多次会话的步态数据集（199人），并通过跨8个传感器、4个视角、3个会话的身份推断基准测试，揭示了此前被认为隐私友好型的新兴智能城市传感器仍存在显著的身份识别风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型智能城市传感器（热成像、深度相机、激光雷达）的隐私风险研究受限于缺乏合适的数据集；同时存在大量未经证实的隐私声明，评估这些传感器在单独及对比情境下的隐私风险至关重要。

Method: 收集了首个多传感器、多视角、多会话的步态数据集MultiGait（含199名个体，多种行走模式和标注属性），并使用多种最先进的识别系统验证；在此基础上开展跨8种传感器、4种视角、3次录制会话的身份推断综合基准测评。

Result: 基准测试表明，通常被认为隐私友好型的传感器仍存在可观的个体身份推断风险；现有方法在跨会话场景下泛化能力差，揭示了重要的研究空白。

Conclusion: MultiGait为严格的隐私调查提供了基础，同时指出新兴传感器并非天然隐私友好，且跨会话身份推断仍需更多研究投入。

Abstract: A lack of suitable datasets has limited the research into the privacy risks of novel smart city sensors, such as thermal cameras, depth cameras, and lidar. Given the number of unsubstantiated privacy claims and their potential widespread deployment into many people's everyday life, understanding the privacy risks of these sensors -- in isolation and in like-for-like comparisons -- is crucial. With MultiGait, we collected the first multi-sensor, multi-perspective, multi-session gait-focused dataset, for the corresponding, and additional more far-reaching investigations. The dataset, validated with multiple state-of-the-art recognition systems, comprises various walking modes and annotated personal attributes for 199 individuals, to ensure the benefit for advanced studies including cross-sensor recognition and anonymization at the edge. MultiGait represents a foundation for rigorous privacy investigations, demonstrated through an extensive identity inference benchmark across eight sensors, four perspectives, and three recording sessions. Our benchmark incidentally reveals that sensors often assumed to be privacy-friendly do still entail considerable identity inference risks, while the poor cross-session generalization of existing methods underscores an important research gap.

</details>


### [148] [HiveTraceGuard-Pro: A Compact Generative Guardrail for Prompt Injection, Jailbreaks, and Adversarial Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01046)
*Nikita Oblakov,Sabrina Sadiekh,Evgeniy Kokuykin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了HiveTraceGuard-Pro，一个0.6B参数的俄英双语生成式护栏模型，基于Qwen3-0.6B进行LoRA微调。模型在19个基准组（其中16个公开）上与34个其他护栏模型对比，综合得分为0.7432，排名第三。在俄语提示注入检测（召回率0.999）和俄语鲁棒性（F1 0.88）方面表现最佳，且推理延迟最低（14.3ms）。模型权重以Apache-2.0许可证公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有护栏模型的评估报告缺乏对俄语提示注入和俄语表面混淆攻击的证据，生产级大语言模型需要有效防御试图覆盖系统指令、绕过安全策略或诱导有害内容的输入。

Method: 从Qwen3-0.6B通过LoRA微调生成0.6B参数模型，使用俄英双语训练。采用单一二元评分规则（安全/不安全）评估最终目标轮次，训练语料配对有害与良性示例（在存在对应物时），并对两种标签应用八种混淆变换。

Result: 综合指标0.7432，落后于前两名（0.7641和0.7552）；在16个公开基准组上为0.7153。在15模型比较中，俄语鲁棒性combined-F1最高（0.88），俄语提示注入召回率最高（0.999），中位延迟最低（14.3ms）。整体FPR为0.268，FNR为0.156。部分结果在独立回复序列化的遗留设置下获得。

Conclusion: HiveTraceGuard-Pro在俄语场景下表现出领先的护栏能力，是当前俄语提示注入检测和鲁棒性方面的最佳选择，且推理效率极高。但需注意数据集与评估集尚未完全公开，部分测试在遗留序列化模式下进行，提示注入测试集至少有27.1%与训练语料重叠，可能影响结果的泛化性评价。

Abstract: Production LLMs must handle inputs that attempt to override system instructions, bypass safety policies or elicit harmful responses. A common mitigation is a separate guardrail model. Existing reports, however, provide little evidence on Russian prompt injection or Russian surface obfuscation. We present HiveTraceGuard-Pro, a 0.6B generative guardrail LoRA-tuned from Qwen3-0.6B. It is trained on Russian and English and uses one binary scoring rule (safe/unsafe) for the final target turn. Its training corpus pairs harmful examples, where a counterpart exists, with benign examples from the same domain and applies eight obfuscation transforms to both labels. In one harness, we compare HiveTraceGuard-Pro with thirty-four other guards on nineteen benchmark groups, sixteen of which are public. Its aggregate key is 0.7432, behind 0.7641 and 0.7552 for the two higher-scoring guards. Over the sixteen public groups alone, its key is 0.7153 and four of the thirty-four other suite guards score higher. In a fifteen-model comparison, HiveTraceGuard-Pro has the highest clean Russian robustness combined-F1 (0.88) and Russian prompt-injection recall (0.999). Both results use Russian sets assembled by our team, and at least 27.1% of the prompt-injection set overlaps the training corpus. Its 14.3 ms median latency is the lowest among those fifteen models in that run. Across the suite, FPR is 0.268 and FNR is 0.156. All reported response results use a legacy standalone-reply serialization rather than the natural assistant-role path of the shipped chat template. We release the merged weights on Hugging Face under Apache-2.0. The corpus, evaluation sets and evaluation code remain internal.

</details>


### [149] [What Limits Robustness in Deep Image Watermarking: An Analysis of Mechanisms and Their Scaling Across Capacities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01050)
*Marta Bistroń,Zbigniew Piotrowski*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文探讨深度图像水印中鲁棒性的核心问题——是否容量本身限制了鲁棒性，还是容量只是暴露了其他限制。文章通过两部分分析得出结论：限制来自失真类别而非容量本身，并识别了三种独立于提取网络训练的机制性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图像水印中鲁棒性仍是主要开放问题，且随着负载增大，限制因素变得更加明显。本文旨在辨明：容量是否是鲁棒性的直接限制因素，还是容量增大只是使其他根本性限制变得可见。

Method: 第一部分将水印需承受的失真及抵抗策略按主导轴分类：失真按负载容量排序，策略按可微性排序。第二部分针对"负载映射到空间块网格且提取与冻结嵌入器分离训练"的方案，测量并识别三种限制机制：负载网格的失同步、编解码器引起失真的抵抗性、可用嵌入强度窗口的收窄。实验覆盖64到16384比特的负载范围，并额外提出一种可验证泛化性声明的评估协议。

Result: 实验发现：对提取阶段进行编解码器训练不仅无效甚至有害，会降低训练工作点的读取性能。三种限制均源于提取之前，因此仅靠提取端的进一步训练无法消除。限制随失真类别而非容量本身变化。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性限制是一类设计方案固有的属性，而非单一实现的特例。该结论对网格块映射类水印方案具有普遍适用性。

Abstract: Robustness remains the principal open problem in deep image watermarking, and what limits it becomes sharper as payload grows. This paper asks whether capacity is itself the limit or only makes other limits visible, and answers in two parts. The first organizes the distortions a watermark must survive and the strategies developed to resist them, ordering each by the axis that governs it: payload capacity for the distortions, differentiability for the strategies. The second identifies and measures three mechanisms that limit robustness in schemes mapping the payload onto a spatial block grid with extraction trained separately from a frozen embedder: desynchronization of the payload grid, the resistance of codec-induced distortion to training, and the narrowing of the usable embedding-strength window. Payloads from 64 to 16384 bits are measured, well beyond the range those strategies address. Training the extraction stage against a codec proves not merely ineffective but harmful, degrading the reading at the operating points used in training. The limits follow the class of distortion rather than capacity itself, and none is removed by further training on the extraction side, because all three arise before extraction. An evaluation protocol making claims of generalization verifiable is also contributed. The conclusions are properties of a class of designs rather than of one implementation.

</details>


### [150] [JENGA: Exploiting Counter-Based RowHammer Countermeasures to Break Real-Time Predictability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01077)
*Valentin Abgrall,Marcello Traiola,Ruben Salvador,Maria Méndez Real,Alessandro Palumbo,Angeliki Kritikakou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于硬件计数器的RowHammer防御措施对实时系统时序行为的影响，提出了一种名为JENGA的攻击方法，攻击任务可通过操纵RowHammer计数器机制的内部状态，使受害者实时任务的执行时间超出预期WCET最高达200%。作者还推导了一种安全的解析上界，将缓解措施引入的延迟纳入WCET分析中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全关键实时系统必须同时满足时间可预测性和安全性。DRAM平台的RowHammer漏洞催生了多种防御措施，但这些防御的评估通常基于平均性能，不足以衡量实时系统中最重要的最坏情况行为。现有研究缺乏对RowHammer防御措施在实时系统时序行为方面影响的深入分析。

Method: 以DDR5标准化的Per-Row-Activation-Counter (PRAC)为案例，首先展示其引入的时序变化；然后提出JENGA攻击，通过攻击者控制的任务操纵RowHammer计数器机制内部状态来延长受害者任务执行时间；在gem5和Ramulator 2.0仿真环境中实现JENGA，并在TACLeBench工作负载上评估；最后推导一个安全解析上界，将缓解诱导延迟纳入WCET分析。

Result: 在TACLeBench工作负载上的评估表明，JENGA攻击可使任务延迟达到其WCET的200%，证明了仅依赖硬件计数器防御的实时系统其初始时间安全假设不再成立。

Conclusion: RowHammer硬件计数器防御（如PRAC-N）会引入不容忽视的最坏情况时序开销，实时系统需要显式建模这些缓解延迟并纳入WCET分析，以确保时间安全性假设的稳健性。

Abstract: Safety-critical real-time systems must satisfy multiple dependability requirements, notably time predictability and security. In such systems, tasks must complete within bounded and known execution times, typically characterised through Worst-Case Execution Time (WCET) analysis. At the same time, DRAM-based platforms are increasingly sensitive to the RowHammer read-disturbance security vulnerability, which has motivated the development of numerous hardware and software countermeasures in both academia and industry. However, the impact of these defences is generally evaluated in terms of average-case performance, a metric that is insufficient for safetycritical real-time systems, where worst-case behaviour is the primary concern. In this paper, we study the impact of RowHammer countermeasures based on hardware counters on the timing behaviour of real-time systems. We use a Per-Row-Activation-Counter (PRAC) countermeasure as a case study, standardised for recent DDR5 memories, and show that it can introduce significant timing variations. Based on this observation, we introduce JENGA, an attack in which an attacker-controlled task manipulates the internal state of the RowHammer countermeasure mechanism to increase the execution time of a victim real-time task beyond its expected WCET. We implement JENGA in a gem5 and Ramulator 2.0 simulation environment and evaluate its impact on TACLeBench workloads. We show that such an attack can delay tasks up to 200% of their WCET, making the initial timesafety assumptions unsafe. To address this issue, we derive a safe analytical bound that accounts for mitigation-induced delays in WCET analysis for DRAM systems protected by hardware countermeasures, such as PRAC-N.

</details>


### [151] [CRSF: Collusion-Resilient Privacy-Preserving Sensor Fusion with Byzantine-Robust Participation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01096)
*Chao Yin,Haihong Tian,Zheng Yang,Haibin Zhang,Fabio Massacci,Chenglu Jin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出CRSF协议，一种抗合谋的隐私保护传感器融合协议，针对现有基于混淆电路方案中传感器-服务器合谋和拜占庭操纵问题，通过PBFT共识阶段和状态相关的标签释放机制提供隐私性、正确性和活性保障，并在Google Cloud上验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于混淆电路的隐私保护传感器融合协议虽然高效，但存在两个关键安全缺陷：一是易受传感器-服务器合谋攻击，可能泄露诚实传感器的隐私；二是易受拜占庭服务器对传感器参与资格的操纵，导致诚实传感器被错误排除以及融合结果被篡改。这些问题严重损害了协议预期的安全性保证。

Method: CRSF协议引入两大核心设计：1) 基于实用拜占庭容错（PBFT）的传感器提交共识阶段，使任何拜占庭服务器无法单方面操纵传感器参与；2) 服务器特定的、状态相关的标签释放机制，结合电路输入标签的阈值保护，确保任何可容许的传感器-服务器合谋都无法获取足够秘密材料来破坏诚实传感器隐私。

Result: 在Google Cloud上实现了CRSF并与最先进基线进行比较，测量了无故障和代表性故障执行下的在线协议总计算和通信成本。在多种容错融合电路和最多261个传感器的场景下，CRSF在稳健安全性和协议性能之间实现了高度实用的权衡。

Conclusion: CRSF在保持隐私、可显式中止的正确性和活性的同时，有效解决了传感器-服务器合谋和拜占庭操纵两大安全弱点，并通过实验验证了其在实际部署中的可行性，为隐私保护传感器融合提供了更健壮的安全解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving sensor fusion enables an untrusted server to compute an aggregate result over distributed sensor measurements without learning either individual inputs or the final output. Recent garbled-circuit-based protocols provide an efficient realization of this functionality in a sensor--server--client architecture, but remain vulnerable to sensor--server collusion and Byzantine manipulation of sensor participation. These weaknesses can compromise honest-sensor privacy, incorrectly exclude honest sensors, and corrupt the computed fusion result, thereby undermining the security guarantees expected from the protocol.
  We present CRSF, a collusion-resilient sensor-fusion protocol that addresses these weaknesses while providing privacy, correctness with explicit abort, and liveness. CRSF introduces a Practical Byzantine Fault Tolerance (PBFT)-based agreement phase for sensor submissions and uses server-specific, status-dependent label release with threshold protection of circuit-input labels. This design prevents any Byzantine server from unilaterally manipulating sensor participation and prevents any admissible sensor-server coalition from obtaining enough secret material to compromise honest-sensor privacy.
  We implement CRSF and compare its online execution time with the most relevant state-of-the-art baseline. Our Google Cloud evaluation measures the total computation and communication cost of the online protocol under fault-free and representative faulty executions. Across a range of fault-tolerant fusion circuits and up to 261 sensors, CRSF demonstrates a highly practical trade-off between robust security and protocol performance.

</details>


### [152] [Johnny Still Receives Spam SMS: Assessing the Robustness of SMS Spam Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01171)
*Muhammad Salman,Muhammad Islam,Muhammad Ikram,Mohamed Ali Kaafar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文评估了实际部署的SMS反垃圾邮件系统（包括商业应用、第三方服务和开源模型）在标准及对抗性条件下的鲁棒性，发现现有模型易受感知型和不可感知型对抗攻击影响，且仅靠对抗训练不足以抵御跨类别攻击。作者提出结合对抗训练与架构/分词多样化的多模型集成策略，采用少数投票机制可显著提升鲁棒性同时保持良好分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMS垃圾邮件检测系统在受控环境中准确率高，但面对对抗攻击和日益复杂的垃圾邮件策略时表现不佳，且已有研究多关注实验室环境下的攻击，缺乏对真实部署系统中鲁棒性的系统评估。

Method: 评估对象包括商业通讯应用、第三方反垃圾邮件服务和Hugging Face上的开源权重模型；采用感知型和最先进的不可感知型攻击，且仅包含经过真实SMS/RCS投递验证的扰动；设计显式留出评估协议检验对抗训练的迁移性；提出结合对抗训练与多模型（架构和分词多样化）集成的方案，采用少数投票策略。

Result: 现有垃圾邮件检测模型在识别对抗性操纵消息方面存在显著漏洞；对抗训练的鲁棒性在同类扰动族内迁移良好，但对结构不同的编码级攻击急剧退化；所提出的多模型少数投票集成能大幅提升对感知型和不可感知型对抗攻击的鲁棒性，同时保持竞争性的分类准确率。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了真实场景中SMS反垃圾邮件系统的鲁棒性缺陷，单一对抗训练不足以防御多样化的攻击手段，基于模型多样性的集成防御是构建更安全SMS垃圾邮件检测系统的有效方向，同时需根据部署场景权衡精确率与召回率。

Abstract: SMS spam detection systems often achieve high accuracy in controlled environments but struggle against adversarial attacks and increasingly sophisticated spam tactics in real-world deployments. In this paper, we evaluate the robustness of SMS anti-spam systems that end users actually rely on, including commercial messaging applications, third-party anti-spam services, and publicly available open-weight models hosted on Hugging Face. We evaluate these systems under both standard and adversarial conditions, considering perceptible and state-of-the-art imperceptible attacks. We include only perturbations that we verify survive real SMS or RCS delivery, rather than lab-only artifacts. Our experiments reveal significant gaps in existing spam detectors' ability to identify adversarially manipulated messages. We further demonstrate that adversarial training alone is insufficient. Using an explicit held-out evaluation protocol, we find that robustness transfers well within a perturbation family but degrades sharply against structurally distinct, encoding-level attacks. To address these weaknesses, we propose a multi-model ensemble that combines adversarial training with spam classifiers diverse in architecture and tokenization. Our results show that this ensemble, particularly when using a minority-voting strategy, substantially improves robustness against both perceptible and imperceptible adversarial attacks while maintaining competitive classification accuracy. We also characterize the resulting precision-recall trade-off and recommend operating points for false-positive-sensitive and recall-critical deployments. These findings highlight the need for comprehensive robustness evaluations and ensemble-based defenses for building more secure SMS spam detection systems in real-world settings.

</details>


### [153] [A SoK for SoCs: Reading the TI Leaves on AI for Cyber Threat Intelligence Generation and Sharing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01174)
*Saastha Vasan,Hadjer Benkraouda,Jizhou Chen,Leyan Pan,Doguhan Yeke,Shinan Liu,Noah Spahn,Stefano Ortolani,David Evans,Christopher Kruegel,Giovanni Vigna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过文献综述和实践者调查，系统研究了网络威胁情报（CTI）生命周期中"CTI生成与共享"这一未被充分研究的阶段，将其划分为智能提取、规范化与丰富化、编码化、分发四个步骤，并通过试点研究评估了大语言模型（LLM）在各步骤中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CTI对于保护关键基础设施至关重要，但从原始攻击证据转化为可共享CTI的过程仍然零散且未被充分研究。学术界对CTI生命周期的首尾阶段（数据收集和消费）研究较多，而"生成与共享"阶段缺乏系统性研究。

Method: 采用三阶段方法：（1）对学术论文进行文献综述，组织CTI生命周期；（2）对多个组织中实践CTI生成与共享的专业人员进行调查，识别四个反复出现的挑战；（3）基于实践者洞察，将CTI生成与共享阶段分为四个步骤，并使用当前大语言模型对每个步骤进行试点可行性研究。

Result: 试点研究表明LLM能够在四个步骤中辅助分析人员，但模型仅能恢复证据中包含的一小部分指标，难以将每项声明基于所提供的证据，也无法判断什么内容对共享情报的接收者保持有用，因此每个步骤仍需要专家监督。

Conclusion: 基于研究发现，本文推导出三个自动化生产可共享情报的研究方向，为CTI生成与共享的自动化研究提供了方向性指导。

Abstract: Cyber Threat Intelligence (CTI) is essential for defending mission-critical infrastructure, yet the process of transforming raw attack evidence into shareable CTI remains fragmented and understudied.
  We conduct a literature survey of academic papers, organizing the CTI lifecycle into three stages: Threat Data Collection, CTI Generation and Sharing, and CTI Consumption. The first and third stages are well represented in the literature, whereas only a small number of papers address CTI Generation and Sharing. To learn how this stage is practiced, we survey practitioners across multiple organizations who routinely generate and share CTI. They describe a largely manual process with four recurring challenges: preventing the exposure of sensitive information, extracting indicators from noisy attack data, correlating observed behavior with standardized tactics, techniques, and procedures (TTPs), and translating CTI into the formats that sharing platforms require.
  Using the insights from the practitioner survey, we divide the CTI Generation and Sharing stage into four steps: Intelligence Extraction, Normalization and Enrichment, Codification, and Distribution. We then conduct pilot studies that probe the feasibility of current Large Language Models (LLMs) for each step. The pilot studies show that LLMs can assist an analyst in each of the four steps. However, the models recover only a fraction of the indicators the evidence contains, struggle to ground every claim in the supplied evidence, and do not judge what keeps shared intelligence useful to its recipients. Each step therefore requires expert supervision. Based on these observations, we derive three research directions for automating the production of shareable intelligence.

</details>


### [154] [Reveree: Diagnosing LLM Reverse-Engineering Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01185)
*Hadjer Benkraouda,Hongyu Cai,Berkay Celik,Gang Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Reveree诊断框架，用于评估LLM逆向工程智能体的能力，通过三级评分体系（求解率、八阶段里程碑、行为画像）分析其逆向工程轨迹。作者评估了9个前沿模型和4种提示策略在88个CTF挑战上的表现，发现基础模型占优、提示策略影响次要且依赖模型、更大更新的模型并非更可靠、失败集中在理解阶段且额外资源难以弥补，并发现真实分析能力与记忆化现象并存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM逆向工程智能体评估仅以CTF挑战是否捕获flag为单一标准，无法揭示智能体在逆向工程流程中何处失败，也无法区分成功是源于对二进制的真正分析还是对公开解决方案的记忆。

Method: 提出Reveree诊断框架，从三个层级评分智能体轨迹：求解率、八阶段RE模式下的里程碑进展、行为画像分析。理解阶段由结果盲化的LLM裁判（经人类专家验证）评分，其余阶段确定性验证。在88个picoCTF和NYU-CTF挑战上评估9个模型和4种提示策略。

Result: 基础模型主导性能，提示策略是次要且依赖模型的效应；更大、更新或更贵的模型并不更可靠；失败集中在逆向工程过程的理解阶段，额外预算、持久性或推理努力只能挽救少数失败，指向能力限制而非资源限制；模型能从挑战描述中复现picoCTF的flag（记忆化），但NYU-CTF显示可测量的回忆很少，且大多数求解在表面扰动后仍成立，表明真实分析与记忆并存。

Conclusion: LLM逆向工程智能体的能力瓶颈在于理解阶段且属于能力限制而非资源限制，记忆化与真实分析在现有评估中并存，Reveree框架为社区提供了更细粒度的逆向工程智能体诊断工具。

Abstract: Reverse engineering (RE) is critical to security tasks such as malware analysis and vulnerability discovery, and large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly able to perform it autonomously. Capture-the-flag (CTF) RE challenges have become the standard proxy for measuring this capability, but evaluation rests on a single criterion: whether the agent captures the flag. This solve rate reveals neither where in the RE process an agent fails nor whether a success reflects analysis of the binary or recall of a public solution. In this paper, we propose Reveree, a diagnostic framework that scores an LLM RE agent's trajectory at three tiers: solve rate, milestone progress through an eight-stage RE schema, and a behavioral profile of its actions. Comprehension stages are scored by an outcome-blinded LLM judge validated against a human expert; all other stages are verified deterministically. Using Reveree, we evaluate nine frontier models and four prompting strategies on 88 picoCTF and NYU-CTF challenges. We find that the base model dominates performance, whereas prompting strategy is a secondary, model-dependent effect. Surprisingly, larger, newer, or costlier models are not reliably stronger. We also find that failures concentrate at the comprehension stages of the RE process, and that extra budget, persistence, or reasoning effort rescues few of them, pointing to a competence limit rather than a resource limit. Regarding memorization, while models reproduce picoCTF flags from challenge descriptions alone, NYU-CTF shows minimal measurable recall, and most solves survive surface perturbation, indicating that genuine analysis coexists with memorization. We release Reveree to the community.

</details>


### [155] [Smart Contracts Claimed Vulnerable by the CVE Database, with Labels and Source Locations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01186)
*Monika di Angelo,Gernot Salzer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CVE-Smart-Contracts，一个截至2026年7月的以太坊智能合约CVE记录精选数据集，包含491条与已部署合约相关的记录、26条项目级记录、45条无验证工件的记录及6条被反驳的记录，支持代码分析和修复技术的实证安全研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVE数据库中包含大量与智能合约相关的漏洞记录，但缺乏系统化、经过验证和标注的数据集来支持以太坊智能合约的实证安全研究，现有数据难以直接用于代码分析和修复技术的评估。

Method: 自动化流程包括：检索CVE记录、收集额外证据、验证记录与工件之间的对应关系、按三种分类体系标注标签、以及函数级漏洞定位；其中15%需要人工分析。数据集保留所有外部输入以确保可复现性，并标注明显错误的记录为"refuted"。

Result: 数据集包含491条与已部署合约相关的记录、26条指向项目（主要是库）的记录、45条无已验证工件的记录，以及6条被反驳的记录。数据集包含了漏洞工件（源代码和运行时字节码）、三种分类体系的标签以及函数级定位信息。

Conclusion: 该数据集为智能合约安全研究提供了结构化、可复现的基准资源，可支持代码分析工具评估和漏洞修复技术的实证研究，不验证原始漏洞声明的真实性但标记了明显错误的记录。

Abstract: The Common Vulnerabilities and Exposures (CVE) database catalogs vulnerability claims in hard- and software, among them those pertaining to blockchain programs a.k.a. smart contracts. We present CVE-Smart-Contracts, a curated dataset of CVE records up to July 2026 referring to Ethereum smart contracts. The dataset contains the vulnerable artifacts (source code and runtime bytecode), labels according to three taxonomies, and function-level locations. The retrieval of CVE records, collection of additional evidence, validation of the correspondence between records and artifacts, label assignment, and vulnerability localization are automated, leaving 15% to manual analysis. The dataset does not validate the original vulnerability claims, but marks a few records obviously wrong as `refuted'. For the sake of reproducibility, all external inputs are retained, so that rerunning the pipelines results in the same outputs. The dataset comprises 491 records linked to deployed contracts, 26 referring to projects (mostly libraries), 45 without validated artifacts, and six records with refuted claims. The dataset supports empirical security research, in particular the evaluation of code analysis and repair techniques.

</details>


### [156] [Identification of Compositional Risks in Data Protection Impact Assessments and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01201)
*Henrik Graßhoff,Meiko Jensen,Malte Hansen,Nils Gruschka*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的协议，用于检测和管理分布式数据处理场景中因服务组合而引发的隐私组合风险（如不同数据处理器共用同一云服务商导致的数据可关联性问题），并改善数据保护影响评估（DPIA）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分布式数据处理中，多个服务提供商合作处理个人数据时，组合层面的隐私风险（如数据处理器无意间依赖同一云服务商导致数据可关联性）超出了单个风险评估的范围，容易被数据保护影响评估（DPIA）所忽视。

Method: 首先进行问题定义和需求获取，然后阐述协议如何识别组合风险的候选者，并利用该信息改进涉及多个数据处理器的服务组合的数据保护影响评估结果。

Result: 提出了一种能够识别候选组合隐私风险并改进多数据处理器服务组合DPIA评估结果的协议（Abstract中未提供具体的实验或评估结果）。

Conclusion: 组合层面的隐私风险在分布式数据处理场景中不可忽视，本文提出的协议为检测和管理这类风险提供了系统性方法，有助于完善数据保护影响评估。

Abstract: When personal data is processed in a distributed manner by cooperating service providers, privacy risks may emerge solely from the choice of data processors included in the composition. For instance, different data processors may unknowingly rely on the same cloud provider, allowing for unintended linkability of personal data at that very provider. As such compositional risks to privacy are beyond the scope of each individual risk assessment, they are likely to be overseen when performing a data protection impact assessment. In this paper, we propose a novel protocol to detect and manage such compositional risks to privacy. Following an initial problem definition and requirements elicitation, we elaborate how our protocol identifies candidates for compositional risks and how this information may be used to improve the results of a data protection impact assessment over service compositions including multiple data processors.

</details>


### [157] [Who Judges the Judges? A Chinese Safety QA Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Responses and Safety Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01210)
*Rui Yang,Shuang Huang,Junhua Liu,Ziqi Zhao,Qingzhong Yan,Yuhang Sun,Cong Liu,Guoping Hu,Rui Mei,Jing Shao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了C-SafeQA，一个基于策略的回复级中文安全评测基准。包含538个基础查询和8,877个对抗查询，由四个LLM部署生成37,660条查询-回复记录，标注为安全、不安全或有争议。实验发现对抗查询下不安全回复率显著升高（11.68%-30.05% vs 0.93%-3.35%），且没有任何安全评测器在所有指标上占优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准通常评估用户查询的风险等级，而非评估回复是否违反安全策略。在中文有害内容评测中，语言变异和对抗性变换可能掩盖风险意图，因此需要区分查询风险与回复安全性，构建一个专注回复级别安全的评测资源。

Method: C-SafeQA包含538个基础查询和8,877个对抗查询，由四个全量模型回答生成37,660条标注记录。参考标签通过多模型一致性裁决和安全专家的分层子集盲审生成。支持目标模型安全评测，以及使用共享参考标签对七个自动安全评测器进行审计。

Result: 基础查询的不安全回复率为0.93%-3.35%，对抗查询为11.68%-30.05%。在对抗子集上，评测器在不安全回复召回率和风险查询条件下的安全回复误报率之间存在显著权衡，没有评测器在所有指标上占优。藏头诗（acrostic）变换降低了所有七个评测器的召回率，暴露了机制特定的评测器弱点。

Conclusion: C-SafeQA揭示了回复级与查询级安全评估的关键差异，对抗变换显著提升不安全回复率且现有安全评测器存在机制性缺陷。数据集及验证代码已公开支持复现，但基准构建、回复生成和私有裁决环节保持不公开。

Abstract: Safety benchmarks for large language models often assess the risk of a user query, although the outcome of question answering depends on whether the response violates a policy. This distinction is critical in Chinese harmful-content evaluation, where linguistic variation and adversarial transformations can obscure risky intent. We introduce C-SafeQA, a policy-grounded benchmark for response-level Chinese safety evaluation. It comprises 538 base queries and 8,877 adversarial queries answered by four full-model LLM deployments, yielding 37,660 query-response records labeled safe, unsafe, or disputed. Reference labels are generated through agreement-aware multi-model adjudication and blind audits of stratified subsets by three safety experts. C-SafeQA supports both evaluation of target-model safety and auditing of seven automated safety judges against shared reference labels. Unsafe-response rates range from 0.93% to 3.35% on base queries and from 11.68% to 30.05% on adversarial queries. On the adversarial subset, judges show substantial trade-offs between unsafe-response recall and risk-query-conditioned safe-response false positive rate, and no judge dominates all metrics. Both acrostic transformations reduce unsafe recall for all seven judges, revealing mechanism-specific evaluator weaknesses. Dataset records, metadata, verification code, and judge scripts are publicly released to support recomputation, while benchmark construction, target-response generation, and private adjudication remain outside the release boundary.

</details>


### [158] [What's in Your Agent's Context? Context Privilege Escalation Attacks against AI Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01222)
*Zichuan Li,Jian Cui,Ashley Chen,Xiaojing Liao,Luyi Xing*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地分析了真实世界AI代理框架（harness）的上下文组装设计，揭示了其中存在的安全漏洞，并提出了两类新型攻击：消息角色上下文权限提升（M-CPE）和跨范围上下文权限提升（X-CPE）。作者对包括Claude Code和Codex在内的12个真实代理框架进行了安全分析，发现这些攻击可导致代理完全沦陷、远程代码执行、拒绝服务及工具/技能调用被操纵等严重后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中的AI代理框架通常依赖厂商专有或不透明的上下文组装设计，导致其上下文的来源和底层逻辑缺乏透明性，由此产生的安全风险尚未被充分研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，首次对真实代理框架的上下文组装设计进行系统性分析。

Method: 作者对真实世界的AI代理框架进行了系统性的上下文组装设计分析，研究框架如何从多源收集和组装上下文，识别出设计中存在的实用攻击向量，并归纳出M-CPE和X-CPE两类攻击类别。随后对12个真实代理框架（含Claude Code和Codex）进行了系统化的CPE攻击安全分析。

Result: 研究发现了两类新型攻击类别：M-CPE（低权限上下文内容被嵌入更高权限消息角色）和X-CPE（攻击者控制的内容超出其引入的上下文范围而持续存在）。对12个真实代理框架的分析表明，这些漏洞可导致代理完全沦陷、远程代码执行、拒绝服务以及工具或技能调用被操纵等严重后果。

Conclusion: 真实世界AI代理框架的上下文组装设计存在严重的安全隐患，厂商专有的不透明设计掩盖了这些风险。该研究揭示了上下文组装中系统性安全问题的存在，为构建更安全的AI代理框架提供了重要的分析基础和改进方向。

Abstract: Real-world, high-profile AI agent harnesses often rely on vendor-proprietary or opaque designs for context assembly, leaving the sources and underlying logic of assembled context poorly understood and the resulting security risks largely unexplored. In this paper, we present the first systematic analysis of context assembly designs in real-world AI agent harnesses. We study and uncover how an agent harness is designed to collect and assemble context from diverse sources, and identify a set of practical attack vectors arising from these designs. Our analysis brings to light two novel categories of attacks in the context assembly of real-world harnesses: (1) MessageRole Context Privilege Escalation (M-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content originating from a low-privileged context is incorporated into a higher-privileged message role. (2) Cross-Scope Context Privilege Escalation (X-CPE), which occurs when attacker-controlled content persists beyond the context in which it was introduced. We performed a systemic security analysis of the CPE attacks against 12 real-world agent harnesses, including Claude Code and Codex. The resulting consequences include full agent compromise, remote code execution, denial of service, and manipulated tool or skill invocations, etc.

</details>


### [159] [Position Matters: Feature Inversion Attacks in ViT Split Inference with Token Reduction and Shuffling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01232)
*Stefano Leggio,Giulio Rossolini,Alessandro Biondi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了ViT分割推断系统中token缩减与token打乱对隐私保护的有效性，发现token打乱仅提供表面隐私，并提出了一种名为SARA的空间对齐重建攻击方法，能够预测token位置并重构图像。同时提出了一种轻量级边端防御方法，通过移除位置嵌入和知识蒸馏来抵御SARA攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ViT越来越多地用于分割推断系统，其中token缩减可降低通信开销，token打乱被用于保护隐私，但其隐私收益尚不明确——尤其是在面对特征反转攻击时，需要系统性评估这些机制的实际隐私保护效果。

Method: 提出SARA攻击管线：(1)预测token位置，(2)恢复空间布局，(3)使用特征空间掩码自编码器重建缺失嵌入，(4)重构输入图像。防御方面提出轻量级边端方案，移除位置嵌入并通过知识蒸馏渐进调整边端transformer块。

Result: 实验表明token打乱仅提供表面隐私，SARA几乎能完全重建原始token组织。token缩减提供更强保护，但在保留token具有足够语义和位置信息时仍存在显著信息泄露。提出的防御方法大幅降低SARA攻击性能，同时保持下游任务精度，且无需修改云端模型。

Conclusion: token打乱并不能作为有效的隐私保护手段，需要更实质性的防御策略。本文提出的边端轻量级防御（去除位置编码+知识蒸馏）能够在保持模型性能的同时有效抵御特征逆转攻击。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) are increasingly used in split-inference systems, where edge devices transmit intermediate token representations to a remote cloud. In this setting, token reduction lowers computation and communication costs, while token shuffling disrupts the spatial organization of the transmitted tokens, potentially limiting information leakage. However, their privacy benefits remain unclear against feature inversion attacks, which attempt to reconstruct the input from the transmitted embeddings. In this work, we show that, despite disrupting the spatial structure required by conventional reconstruction attacks, transmitted token embeddings retain substantial positional information. Based on this observation, we introduce the Spatially Aligned Reconstruction Attack (SARA), a unified pipeline that predicts token positions, restores their spatial layout, reconstructs missing embeddings using a feature-space masked autoencoder, and recovers the input image. Our results demonstrate that token shuffling provides only apparent privacy, as SARA largely reconstructs the original token organization. Token reduction offers stronger protection, but significant leakage persists when the retained tokens preserve sufficient semantic and positional information. Finally, we introduce a lightweight edge-side defense that removes positional embeddings and progressively adapts the edge-side transformer blocks through knowledge distillation. It substantially reduces attack performance against SARA, while preserving downstream task accuracy and requiring no changes to the cloud-side model.

</details>


### [160] [MutMem-V2: Cryptographically Authorized Mutation in Persistent Agent Memory Portable Verification and Reproducible Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01235)
*Walid Saidi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MutMem V2是MutMem V1的后续版本，在不引入第二内存引擎的前提下，完善了持久代理内存的可移植验证契约和可复现安装路径。它规定了规范的字节定义、域分离的承诺机制、召回证据与排序等完整协议，并通过独立的Node和Python实现验证了所有72个结构性和加密性终端的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MutMem V1虽然实现了保留性密码学授权突变，但缺乏完整的可移植验证契约和干净的安装复现路径，存在发布空白需要填补。

Method: V2版本定义了精确的规范字节、域分离的对象和捆绑承诺、强制召回证据成员资格和排序、外部信任锚、身份纪元、撤销、授权、请求收据、有序披露以及三种突变终端类型。发布了18个版本化对象模式、39个召回向量、15个突变向量和37个封闭的召回失败原因，并实现了独立的Node和Python实现。

Result: 独立Node和Python实现在全部72个结构性和加密终端上结果一致；生产一致性语料库在28个必需类别中的42/42案例上一致；干净的Node v26.8.1安装可达到首次启动、重启和调度器就绪状态；120单元Canary实验仅支持显式标记遍历。

Conclusion: MutMem V2支持关于可移植完整性、授权、可追溯性、一致性和可复现性的声明，但不声称建立语义真理性、普遍鲁棒性或独立复制。所有公开表格均从自哈希聚合重新生成，独立验证者可重构统计数据和声明边界。

Abstract: MutMem V1 introduced retention-preserving, cryptographically authorized mutation for persistent agent memory but did not provide a complete portable verification contract or clean-install reproduction path. MutMem V2 closes that publication gap without introducing a second memory engine. It specifies exact canonical bytes, domain-separated object and bundle commitments, mandatory recall-evidence membership and ordering, external trust anchors, identity epochs, revocation, authorization, request receipts, ordered disclosure, and three mutation terminal types. The released protocol contains 18 versioned object schemas, 39 recall vectors, 15 mutation vectors, and 37 closed recall failure reasons. Independent Node and Python implementations agree on verdict and primary reason for all 72 structural and cryptographic terminals; a production-conformance corpus agrees on 42/42 cases across 28 required classes. A clean Node v26.8.1 installation reaches first-boot, restart, and scheduler readiness with no experimental memories. A separately scoped 120-unit Canary experiment supports only explicit-marker traversal. Every public table regenerates from a self-hashed aggregate, and an independent verifier reconstructs the statistics and claim boundaries. Historical V1 empirical results remain historical. MutMem V2 supports claims about portable integrity, authorization, traceability, conformance, and reproducibility under stated assumptions; it does not establish semantic truth, universal robustness, or independent replication.

</details>


### [161] [Hidden Services Protocol for Mixnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01326)
*Nicolas Constantinides,Mahdi Rahimi,Stavros Nonis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了NymHS，这是第一个实用且安全的mixnet隐藏服务协议，在提供发送者匿名性的同时保护接收者（目的地）隐私。该协议基于SURB构建，解决了现有方法在对手控制单个mixnode时可能破坏发送者或接收者匿名性的两个实际攻击，并支持匿名服务发现、认证双向会话和异步SURB补充。实验表明NymHS在实际mixnet上可高效部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有mixnet虽然能保护发送者隐私，但缺乏同时保护接收者（目的地）隐私的安全实用协议。现有SURB使用方式存在两个实际攻击，当对手仅控制单个mixnode时即可危害发送者或接收者匿名性。

Method: 基于Single-Use Reply Blocks（SURBs）设计隐藏服务协议，分析并修复现有SURB使用中的两个攻击漏洞，在此基础上提出NymHS协议，支持匿名服务发现、认证双向会话和异步SURB补充，并在开源Nym代码库上实现。

Result: 通过118个网站的web浏览实验（27种配置各测3次，共9,558次页面加载）验证了实用性。将Sphinx载荷从2 KiB增至10 KiB可使平均页面加载延迟降低约5.2倍，通信开销从21.7%降至4.3%。

Conclusion: 隐藏服务可以在mixnet上高效部署，NymHS填补了mixnet缺乏实用接收者隐私保护的空白，为mixnet上的隐藏服务提供了安全且可实现的解决方案。

Abstract: Mix networks (mixnets) provide network-level privacy by routing each communication packet through a sequence of intermediaries, called mixnodes, that randomly delay and cryptographically transform packets before forwarding them, making it difficult for observers to link mixnet entries to exits. While this mechanism protects sender privacy from both external adversaries and the receiver, existing mixnets lack a secure and practical protocol that simultaneously protects receiver (destination) privacy, particularly from the sender. We close this gap.
  We introduce the first practical and secure hidden-service protocol for mixnets, providing receiver privacy alongside sender anonymity. Our design builds on Single-Use Reply Blocks (SURBs), which enable anonymous replies without revealing the receiver's address. We show, however, that existing approaches to using SURBs expose two practical attacks that can compromise sender or receiver anonymity when the opposing party controls only a single mixnode. We develop defenses against both vulnerabilities.
  Building on these defenses, we introduce NymHS, a secure and practical hidden-service protocol for mixnets that supports anonymous service discovery, authenticated bidirectional sessions, and asynchronous SURB replenishment. We implement NymHS on the open-source Nym codebase and evaluate its practicality through web-browsing experiments across 118 websites, measuring each of the 27 configurations three times (9,558 page loads). Our results demonstrate that hidden services can be deployed efficiently over mixnets. In particular, increasing the Sphinx payload from 2 KiB to 10 KiB reduces mean page-load latency by approximately 5.2x and decreases communication overhead from 21.7% to 4.3% relative to the current Nym baseline.

</details>


### [162] [When Safety Routing Breaks: Understanding Alignment Fragility under Benign Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01455)
*Yitong Guo,Xiaoyi Chen,Siyuan Zhang,Xiaofeng Wang,Haixu Tang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Fisher几何的新解释框架，认为良性微调导致LLM拒绝行为脆弱的原因不在于梯度冲突，而在于安全相关的Fisher信息呈低秩结构，且微调会平坦化安全几何但保留输出路由通路。仅需100个良性样本即可触发该通路在输出侧MLP模块中的重新锐化，导致安全对齐崩溃而通用能力轻微下降。少量安全样本即可恢复拒绝行为，表明内部安全表征仍然保留。LoRA和ASAM可抑制早期崩溃，但随微调规模增大保护作用减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将良性微调破坏LLM安全对齐归因于梯度冲突，但这一解释尚不充分。作者希望从Fisher几何角度深入理解拒绝行为为何如此脆弱，揭示安全崩溃背后的内在机理。

Method: 论文提出Fisher几何分析框架，从信息几何角度研究安全对齐在良性微调下的退化机制。通过分析安全Fisher信息的低秩特性、安全几何平坦化、输出侧MLP模块中路由通路的重新锐化，并结合LoRA和ASAM等微调方法进行验证。

Result: 研究发现：安全Fisher信息呈现低秩结构；100个良性微调样本即可在输出侧MLP模块中重新锐化输出路由通路，导致安全攻击成功率大幅上升而通用能力仅轻度下降；少量安全微调样本即可恢复拒绝行为；LoRA和ASAM通过抑制输出侧锐度缓解早期崩溃，但在更大规模微调下保护效果减弱。

Conclusion: 安全失败的本质是低秩输出路由机制的破坏，而非简单的梯度冲突。该视角为理解LLM安全对齐退化提供了新的理论框架，也为设计更具鲁棒性的安全微调策略提供了指导方向。

Abstract: Benign fine-tuning severely weakens the safety alignment of large language models (LLMs), so we study why refusal behavior is so fragile. While prior work often attributes this failure to gradient conflict, we propose a fundamentally different Fisher-geometric explanation: safety Fisher is low-rank, and alignment makes the safety geometry flatter while preserving an output-routing pathway. After 100 benign fine-tuning examples, this pathway is selectively re-sharpened in output-side MLP modules, explaining the asymmetric fragility: safety can collapse to high attack success rates, while general utility degrades mildly. The routing view also explains why few safety examples can restore refusal behavior, indicating that internal safety-relevant representations are preserved. Finally, we show that LoRA and ASAM mitigate early collapse by suppressing output-side sharpness, but their protection weakens at larger fine-tuning scales. Overall, safety failure is best understood as a disruption of a low-rank output-routing mechanism

</details>


### [163] [Defense-as-Skill: Evolving Runtime Guard Skill for Skill-Augmented Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01487)
*Xiaofang Yang,Ziqi Miao,Dianbo Sui,Jing Shao,Lijun Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Defense-as-Skill防御范式，将运行时安全防护本身实现为一个可安装、可检查、可编辑的技能。所提出的SkillSonar守卫与不受信任的任务技能并行运行，根据用户任务边界检查敏感操作，实现允许、重规划或确认决策。通过SCOPE-R数据集和蒙特卡洛树搜索驱动的守卫技能进化机制，在Claude Code和OpenClaw上显著降低攻击成功率（ID ASR从0.482降至0.104，OOD ASR从0.606降至0.115），并验证了跨模型迁移、抵御自适应攻击者等能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 技能增强型智能体将可复用技能作为持久化运行时上下文，虽然提升了任务性能，但也为恶意技能提供了操控未来行动的持久通道。这类技能可能只在具体的用户任务和工作区状态出现后才实施危险行为（如泄露机密、破坏代码、绕过审批或为数据外泄做铺垫），使得安装前的审查远远不足，亟需运行时、基于任务条件的防护机制。

Method: 提出Defense-as-Skill范式，将运行时守卫本身实现为一个可安装、可检查、可编辑的技能。具体包含：(1) SkillSonar守卫技能，与不受信任任务技能并行运行，基于用户任务边界对敏感动作进行分类路由（允许/重规划/确认），不修改底层智能体运行时；(2) SCOPE-R数据集，覆盖6个风险家族和21个子类别，包含206个攻击确认的恶意实例和43个良性任务；(3) 运行时守卫技能进化机制，采用蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）从rollout反馈中对磁盘上的守卫技能进行迭代改进。

Result: 在Claude Code和OpenClaw上，进化后的守卫在保持良好安全-效用权衡的同时显著降低了攻击成功率。在GLM-5重复运行中，SkillSonar将ID ASR从0.482降至0.104，OOD ASR从0.606降至0.115。进一步分析展示了跨受害模型、保留风险家族和外部基准的泛化能力，以及对自适应攻击者的持续防护。消融实验表明，明确的安全责任分配和技能原生表示对效果提升均至关重要。

Conclusion: Defense-as-Skill将安全防护从固化在运行时中的逻辑转变为可审查、可进化的技能，为应对任务条件触发的恶意技能攻击提供了一种灵活而有效的运行时防护方案。该方法在多个实验设置中展现了显著的安全提升与良好的可用性权衡，且具备跨模型的可迁移性和对自适应攻击者的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Skill-augmented agents load reusable skills as persistent runtime context, improving task performance but also giving malicious skills a durable channel for steering future actions. Such skills may leak secrets, corrupt code, bypass approvals, or stage data for exfiltration only after a concrete user task and workspace state make the unsafe action appear useful. This makes pre-install vetting insufficient and calls for runtime, task-conditioned protection. We propose Defense-as-Skill, a defense paradigm that implements the runtime guard itself as an installable, inspectable, and editable skill. Our guard, SkillSonar, runs alongside untrusted task skills and checks sensitive actions against the user's task boundary, routing each action to an allow, replan, or confirmation decision without modifying the underlying agent runtime. To study this setting, we construct SCOPE-R, a task-conditioned dataset covering 6 risk families and 21 sub-categories, with 206 attack-confirmed malicious instances and 43 benign tasks. We then improve SkillSonar on the SCOPE-R training subset using runtime guard-skill evolution, a Monte-Carlo Tree Search procedure that evolves the on-disk guard skill from feedback on the rollouts. Across Claude Code and OpenClaw, the evolved guard substantially reduces attack success while maintaining a favorable safety-utility trade-off. On repeated GLM-5 runs, SkillSonar reduces ID ASR from 0.482 to 0.104 and OOD ASR from 0.606 to 0.115. Further analyses demonstrate transfer across victim models, held-out risk families, and external benchmarks, as well as retained protection against adaptive attackers. Ablations further show that explicit safety responsibility assignment and the skill-native representation are both important to the observed gains.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Harness Engineering: Anatomy, Architecture, and Evolution of Coding Agents -- A Source-Code Study of Eleven Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00006)
*Paul Barbaste,Tristan Darrigol,Germain Vu,Tom Wiltberger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对11个生产级编码harness（Claude Code、Codex CLI等）及首个元harness Omnigent进行了大规模源码解剖，定义了harness工程学科的七个标准子系统、29个重复设计模式，并发现该领域在2026年上半年完成了从"工具"到"平台"的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harness工程作为一个新兴学科缺乏系统的实证基础，作者旨在通过大规模源码审计建立这一学科最全面的经验性研究，以理解agent运行时（harness）的设计与演化规律。

Method: 对11个生产编码harness（总代码约400万行Python/TypeScript/Rust）进行源码级解剖，定义七个规范子系统，映射各系统的最小/最大实现，结合跨季度纵向diff与三倍数语料扩展（对比Omnigent元harness）进行系统分析。

Result: 得出13项跨系统观察和29个反复出现的设计模式；关键发现包括：无任何agent运行时引入通用agent框架、无向量嵌入检索（依赖确定性检索）；SKILL.md（9/11）超越MCP（8/11）成为主流，ACP在6个系统中引入"harness托管"新角色；纵向样本显示收敛演变为模仿、行为策略从prompt文本迁移至配置文件。

Conclusion: 编码harness在2026年上半年完成了从工具到平台的范式转变；论文以18条设计建议和90行最小可行harness脚手架收尾，为该学科提供指导性结论。

Abstract: An agent is a model plus a harness -- the runtime that couples an LLM to the world through a loop, tools, context management, safety controls, orchestration, and extension surfaces. Harness engineering, named as a discipline in early 2026, is the design and evolution of that runtime. This paper gives the young discipline its most comprehensive empirical foundation to date: a source-code anatomy of eleven production coding harnesses (Claude Code, Codex CLI, Gemini CLI, Mistral Vibe, OpenHands, Aider, Mini-SWE-Agent, Hermes, Pi, OpenCode, OpenClaw), plus Omnigent, the first meta-harness, analyzed as a contrast point. We define what a harness is, map its seven canonical subsystems with the minimal and maximal implementation of each, and dissect all eleven systems along those subsystems. The audit yields 13 cross-cutting observations and a catalog of 29 recurring design patterns. Two absences survive a threefold corpus expansion: across roughly four million lines of Python, TypeScript, and Rust, no agent runtime imports a general-purpose agentic framework, and none retrieves code with vector embeddings; the field runs on hand-rolled async loops and deterministic retrieval. SKILL.md skills lead MCP in adoption (9/11 vs. 8/11), and ACP ships in six systems with a new third role: harness hosting. Because the original eight systems were re-pinned rather than replaced, the study also contains a controlled longitudinal sample -- the same harnesses source-diffed across one quarter -- showing convergence becoming imitation and behavioral policy migrating from prompt prose to configuration. These threads converge on the paper's thesis: in the first half of 2026 the coding harness completed a turn from tool to platform. The paper closes with 18 design recommendations and a 90-line minimum-viable-harness scaffold.

</details>


### [165] [Structure-Behavior Coalescence and the Limits of Traditional Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00042)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文挑战传统系统理论中将结构与行为视为可分离分析维度的基础假设，提出"结构-行为融合"（SBC）原则，主张二者是同一系统过程中相互构成的方面，系统身份源于结构与行为的持续共同决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统系统理论假设结构与行为可分离分析，但这种分离导致在解释系统身份时面临困难，特别是在涉及变化、涌现和边界界定等问题时。作者试图解决这一理论困境。

Method: 提出"结构-行为融合"（SBC）重构原则，将结构与行为从独立实体重新概念化为单一系统过程中相互构成的两个方面，以控制论视角提供统一框架。

Result: 该重构为理解系统身份、涌现和边界形成提供了一个统一的、控制论视角下的系统性理解框架。

Conclusion: 系统身份应被视为结构组织与行为动态持续共同决定的结果，而非二者外部对应或对齐的结果，这为系统理论研究提供了新的整合视角。

Abstract: This paper examines a foundational assumption in traditional systems theory, namely that structure (the organization of components) and behavior (the evolution of system activity over time) can be treated as separable analytical dimensions. It argues that this separation contributes to persistent difficulties in explaining system identity, particularly in cases involving change, emergence, and boundary specification. To address this issue, the paper introduces Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as a reframing principle. SBC proposes that structure and behavior should not be understood as independently existing entities that are subsequently related through modeling constructs, but as mutually constitutive aspects of a single systemic process. From this perspective, system identity is understood as arising from the sustained co-determination of structural organization and behavioral dynamics, rather than from their external correspondence or alignment. This reframing provides a unified way of understanding system identity, emergence, and boundary formation within a cybernetically informed systems perspective.

</details>


### [166] [What Is a System? An Interaction-Based Account of Structure-Behavior Coalescence in General Systems Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00043)
*William S. Chao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出"结构-行为融合"(SBC)作为系统的交互式定义，主张系统是结构实体，其行为通过组成实体间的交互产生，交互同时贡献于结构的组织与行为的实现，从而统一了系统"是什么"与系统"做什么"的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统定义通常侧重元素、关系、边界、功能或交互等单一视角，缺乏对系统结构如何与系统行为相互构成的统一解释。

Method: 论文通过区分系统结构、交互和行为三个概念，同时将其视为统一系统表征中不可分割的方面，论证交互是连接结构关系与行为过程的关键纽带，并提出SBC作为一般系统理论的概念基础。

Result: SBC提供了一个保持系统"是什么"与"做什么"之间一致性的系统表征基础，并探讨了其在系统同一性、系统边界、行为涌现及复杂系统表征方面的意义。

Conclusion: SBC被呈现为一种补充现有系统理论的一般性概念基础，以交互为中心将结构与行为的融合置于系统定义的核心位置。

Abstract: The question of what constitutes a system remains fundamental to General Systems Theory. Existing definitions commonly characterize a system in terms of elements, relationships, boundaries, functions, or interactions, but these perspectives do not always provide a unified account of how system structure and system behavior constitute one another. This paper proposes Structure-Behavior Coalescence (SBC) as an interaction-based account of what a system is. From the SBC perspective, a system is not merely a collection of elements or relationships, nor is it adequately characterized by behavior considered independently of structure. Rather, a system is a structured entity whose behavior arises through interactions among its constituent entities, with those interactions simultaneously contributing to both its structural organization and behavioral realization. The paper develops this perspective by distinguishing system structure, interaction, and behavior while treating them as inseparable aspects of a unified system representation. It argues that interactions provide the essential link through which structural relationships become behavioral processes and through which behavioral processes reveal and instantiate system structure. This perspective provides a basis for representing systems in a manner that maintains consistency between what a system is and what a system does. The paper further considers the implications of SBC for General Systems Theory, particularly for system identity, system boundaries, behavioral emergence, and the representation of complex systems. SBC is presented as a general conceptual foundation that complements existing systems theories by placing the coalescence of structure and behavior through interaction at the center of the definition of a system.

</details>


### [167] [Towards Agentic Cloud Engineering: Graph and Loop Engineering with a Zero-Trust Agent Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00050)
*Sagar Srinivas Sakhinana,Venkataramana Runkana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个名为"Agentic Cloud Workflow Engineering"的智能体AI框架，将自然语言形式的云工程任务转化为经过验证的代码仓库和可部署的云基础设施，通过图工程、循环工程和智能体框架工程三个互补关注点实现工作流推进、受限执行、故障恢复和可验证完成，并在Google Cloud上进行了实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体在执行长时跨度、多步骤的云工作流时，需要明确的工作流推进机制、约束执行、故障恢复和可验证完成机制。现有方法缺乏统一的工程架构来支撑这些复杂需求。

Method: 框架通过三个互补关注点实现：(1)图工程——定义长时跨度的执行推进和基于验证的状态转移；(2)循环工程——提供有界的诊断、修复/重规划、重试和重新验证机制；(3)智能体框架工程——通过身份、授权、策略限定的能力、隔离和运行时防护实现零信任执行。工作流推进要求机器可检查的仓库、部署和运行时证据。

Result: 在Google Cloud上进行了实例化评估，实验表明执行要么以经过验证的可操作性云部署终止，要么在有界恢复机制下以可审计的终端故障终止，验证了仓库完整性、受控执行、证据门控推进、可操作性部署和有界恢复等能力。

Conclusion: 该框架为涵盖Agentic DevOps、CloudOps、SRE/AIOps、SecOps、DataOps、MLOps/LLMOps、AgentOps、RAG/GraphRAG等多种云工程领域的智能体工作流提供了统一的工程架构。

Abstract: Agentic AI is enabling cloud-based workflows in which autonomous agents reason over operational state, invoke authorized tools, modify software and infrastructure, deploy services, verify execution outcomes, and adapt across long-horizon, multistep tasks. Engineering such workflows requires explicit mechanisms for workflow progression, constrained execution, failure recovery, and verifiable completion. We present Agentic Cloud Workflow Engineering, an agentic AI framework that transforms natural-language agentic cloud-engineering tasks into validated code repositories and verified operational cloud deployments for automating cloud-based agentic workflows. The framework separates three complementary concerns: graph engineering specifies long-horizon workflow progression and verification-dependent transitions; loop engineering provides bounded diagnosis, repair or re-planning, retry, and re-verification; and agent harness engineering enforces zero-trust execution through identity, authorization, policy-scoped capabilities, isolation, and runtime safeguards. Workflow progression and completion require machine-checkable repository, deployment, and runtime evidence, with recovery constrained by explicit operational bounds and termination criteria. We instantiate the framework on Google Cloud and evaluate repository completeness, controlled execution, evidence-gated progression, operational deployment, and bounded recovery. Experimental results show that executions terminate with either a verified operational cloud deployment or an auditable terminal failure under bounded recovery. The framework provides a unified engineering architecture for cloud-based workflows spanning Agentic DevOps, Agentic CloudOps, Agentic SRE/AIOps, Agentic SecOps, Agentic DataOps, Agentic MLOps/LLMOps, AgentOps, Agentic RAG/GraphRAG, and related cloud-engineering domains.

</details>


### [168] [Can MCP Clients Decide What to Do After Failure? A Result-Only Actionability Audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00072)
*Rishabh Mehan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究MCP（Model Context Protocol）失败结果中可被确定性软件利用的信息，提出六维可操作性档案，通过对21个失败案例的分析发现：类型化字段虽能暴露失败但缺乏具体原因、目标和可执行修复信息，散文文本携带更多因果信息但增加了语义解析负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端收到isError:true时只知道出错了，但缺乏机器可读的依据来决策是修复参数、重新认证、等待、更换工具还是停止操作，因此需要研究失败结果本身能提供多少可操作信息。

Method: 提出六部分可操作性档案（six-part actionability profile），基于记录级证据，对从十个可访问采样服务器中诱导出的21个安全失败案例进行分析，并进行词法来源审计，最后构建一个fail-closed原型验证独立实验控制平面的确定性分支能力。

Result: 在21个失败案例中，类型化字段在18个案例中暴露了失败，8个案例暴露了宽泛的策略，但未暴露具体原因、目标、可执行修复或重放约束；散文文本承载更多原因和目标信息，但代价是语义解释成为恢复路径的一部分。

Conclusion: MCP失败结果通常能使失败可观察，有时能支持宽泛的响应决策，但很少使得具体的恢复或安全重放在该样本中完全自包含；研究范围刻意窄于生态系统调查或智能体基准测试。

Abstract: A client that receives isError:true knows that something went wrong. It may still have no machine-readable basis for deciding whether to fix an argument, authenticate, wait, choose another tool, or stop. This paper studies what deterministic software can learn from a completed MCP failure result alone; request arguments, schemas, discovery history, authen- tication state, transport metadata, host policy, and ap- plication state are outside that boundary. We introduce a six-part actionability profile and apply it with record- level evidence. In a small illustrative study of 21 safely induced failures from ten reachable sampled servers, typed fields expose failure in 18 cases and a broad policy in 8, yet expose no specific cause, target, executable repair, or replay constraint. Prose often carries more cause and target information, at the price of making semantic interpretation part of the recovery path. A lexical source audit finds the same text-centered pat- tern. Finally, a fail-closed prototype demonstrates how a separate experimental control plane could support deterministic branching. The result is deliberately nar- rower than an ecosystem survey or an agent benchmark: completed MCP results often make failure observable, sometimes make a broad response possible, and rarely make concrete recovery or safe replay self-contained in this sample.

</details>


### [169] [Framework and Benchmark for Code-Driven Agentic Testing in Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00081)
*Bin Hong,Zhenchao Zhang,Jiyuan He,Kai Zhang,Zhenya Huang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的端到端GUI测试范式CAT（Code-driven Agentic Testing），通过让智能体编写Playwright代码驱动浏览器来自主探索Web应用并发现Bug，并配套构建了CATJudge框架和CATTest基准（含102个AI生成的Web应用及精心标注的bug）。实验表明主流VLM在此任务上表现不佳，揭示了当前视觉语言模型能力与真实Web测试需求之间的显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端GUI测试评估依赖预定义检查清单，且局限于Web生成基准的数据和框架，导致视觉语言模型（VLM）的Bug发现能力从未被系统性测试过，存在明显的评估盲区。

Method: 提出CAT范式，智能体通过编写Playwright代码驱动浏览器、收集反馈并自主探索Web应用以发现错误。在此基础上实例化CATJudge框架，在单一环境中统一Browser-Use和Computer-Use工具；同时构建CATTest基准，包含102个通过人机协作生成的AI Web应用，并标注了涉及复杂交互和细微缺陷的Bug。

Result: 对主流VLM的实验显示，所有被评估的模型在该任务上均表现不佳，表明当前VLM的能力与真实世界AI Web开发测试需求之间存在明显差距。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了VLM在缺陷发现能力上的系统性评估空白，揭示了现有模型在自主Web测试场景下的不足，并开源了代码与数据以推动后续研究。

Abstract: End-to-end GUI testing is essential for verifying web applications, yet existing evaluations rely on predefined checklists and are confined to the data and frameworks of web generation benchmarks, leaving the bug-discovery ability of vision-language models (VLMs) systematically untested. We introduce \textbf{C}ode-driven \textbf{A}gentic \textbf{T}esting (CAT), a paradigm in which the agent writes Playwright code to drive the browser, gathers feedback, and autonomously explores web applications to uncover bugs. We instantiate CAT with CATJudge, an agentic framework that unifies Browser-Use and Computer-Use tools within a single environment and CATTest, a benchmark of 102 AI-generated web applications with carefully annotated bugs, built through close human-AI collaboration to feature complex interactions and subtle defects. Experiments with mainstream VLMs show that all evaluated models perform poorly, revealing a clear gap between current VLM capabilities and the demands of real-world testing in AI web development. We release our code and data at https://github.com/SleepyWithoutCoffee/CATJudge.

</details>


### [170] [Commit-first LLM judging inherits the judge's own errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00088)
*Idil Gozel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文审计了八个评估框架的默认裁判配置，发现24个配置中无一实现"先提交再判断"（commit-first）策略。对照实验表明，普通best-of-N搜索即可操纵这些裁判配置，而commit-first虽在某些任务上有效，但并非万能——它将可被操纵的锚点从候选答案转移到裁判自身答案上，评估质量取决于裁判自身能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM裁判可能被其评分的系统操纵。已有文献提出"先提交再判断"（commit-first）作为有效防御策略，但学术界尚不清楚：实际部署的软件是否采用了这一策略，以及采用它的代价是什么。

Method: 1) 审计八个广泛使用的评估框架的默认裁判配置（共24个）；2) 追踪这些配置的提示词谱系；3) 设计受控实验：使用无正确答案的best-of-N搜索优化代码，针对文档所述配置进行攻击；4) 在两个任务上测试commit-first策略的效果；5) 自我验证研究工具（criteria）的准确性。

Result: 24个默认配置中无一实现commit-first，有9个实现了文献认为无效的变体，且共享同一祖先提示词（可通过复制拼写错误追溯到）。在区间合并任务上，裁判接受了96个候选中的90-93个（攻击成功），所有被接受候选通过可见测试但未通过隐藏测试集；commit-first将接受数降为0。但在第二个任务上commit-first使情况更糟——裁判自身答案错误，群体收敛于错误答案。此外，研究者的15条标准中有5条与原始文献对照有误。

Conclusion: Commit-first并没有消除被操纵的锚点，只是将其从候选答案移动到裁判自身答案上——评估质量上限取决于裁判在该任务上的能力。这一前提条件可低成本预先测量，且具有任务局部性而非规模依赖性：较小的裁判可能在某个任务上优于前沿模型裁判且抵抗操纵。

Abstract: LLM judges, models that score another system's output, can be gamed by the systems they score. Recent work identifies one defence that works: the judge solves the task itself first and commits to that answer, then accepts a candidate only if the two match. We call this commit-first judging, and ask whether shipped software implements it, and what it costs.
  We audit the default judge configurations of eight widely used evaluation frameworks. Of the 24 configurations in scope, none implement it. Nine implement a variant the literature measures as ineffective, and share one ancestor prompt, traceable through a copied typographical error.
  In a controlled experiment, an ordinary best-of-N search with no access to correct answers optimises code against one of these configurations, used exactly as documented. On an interval merging task the judge accepted 90 of 96 candidates in one seed and 93 of 96 in the other; every accepted candidate passed every test the search could see and failed a held-out suite it could not. The judge identified the defective line and cited it as grounds for a perfect score. Commit-first judging removed the effect: 0 of 96 in both seeds. On a second task it made matters worse in both seeds: the judge's committed answer was wrong, and in one seed the population converged on it. This is our main finding. Commit-first judging does not remove the anchor that gets gamed, it moves it from the candidate to the judge's own answer, so evaluation is only as good as the judge is at the task. That precondition is cheap to measure in advance, and is task local rather than scale dependent: a smaller judge solved a task the frontier judge failed and resisted gaming where it did not.
  We also validate our own instruments: five of fifteen claims in our criteria were wrong against verbatim sources, and two held-out checks were unjustified by their specifications.

</details>


### [171] [Don't Let the Model Write the YAML: Deterministic, Minimal-Diff GitOps Remediation from LLM-Proposed Field Changes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00227)
*Pruthvi Davineni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM代理在GitOps工作流中自动编辑Kubernetes配置文件的策略安全性问题。实验发现，无论是统一diff还是全文件重写策略，在无人监督的自动化场景下都不安全：diff在严格模式下几乎无法应用（宽容模式虽成功率高但约1/7会静默错误应用），全文件重写则依赖模型能力且存在非确定性风险。作者提出了一种替代方案KubeAstra：将语义决策与语法编辑分离，让LLM只输出结构化字段变更意图，由确定性管线通过YAML解析器的位置标记精确定位并替换文本中的字符跨度，从而保证编辑正确、确定性、最小化且与模型无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有实践者倾向于让LLM直接生成编辑后的完整文件或diff来应用修复，但作者通过真实Kubernetes清单实验证明这些文本生成策略对于无人监督的自动化都不安全。存在diff静默误应用和全文件重写非确定性等问题，需要一个安全可靠的自动化配置编辑机制。

Method: 将语义决策（哪个资源、哪个字段、什么值）与语法编辑行为分离。具体为：LLM代理只输出结构化的字段更改意图，然后由确定性管线具备两个能力：(1) 通过(kind, name)索引清单，定位目标标量的精确字符跨度（利用YAML解析器的节点位置标记）；(2) 仅替换原始文本中的该跨度。文件不重新序列化，diff天然最小化，格式与注释都得以保留。

Result: 实验表明：统一diff在严格补丁模式下几乎无法应用，但在宽容工具（GNU patch）下96%可应用但14-20%会静默误应用且无错误信号；全文件重写方面，小模型会损坏文件，前沿模型通常正确但非确定性（某些运行会静默丢字段或编辑相邻字段），且每次编辑需O(文件大小)重新生成成本。而提出的方法保证编辑正确确定性且与模型无关，生成成本为O(1)。

Conclusion: 在无人监督的GitOps自动化配置编辑场景下，LLM直接生成diff或重写文件均不可靠。将LLM提出的意图与确定性、失败关闭（fail-closed）的应用契约相结合是更优路径。该方案适用于已知变更的忠实应用，变更本身是否正确仍交由人工PR审查。作者以Apache-2.0协议实现KubeAstra并发布了基准测试。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly diagnose incidents and propose remediations. In a GitOps workflow, applying a fix means editing a version-controlled config file, and the obvious implementation, having the model author the edited file or a diff, is what practitioners reach for first. Evaluating that choice on real Kubernetes manifests, we find no text-generation strategy is safe for unattended automation. Unified diffs are unsafe: under strict patching almost none apply, but that is an artifact, since a tolerant tool (GNU patch) applies 96%, yet silently misapplies about 1 in 7 (14-20%) with no error signal. Full-file rewrite is capability-dependent: a small model corrupts the file, while a frontier model is usually correct but non-deterministic (it silently drops a field or edits a neighbor on some runs) and must regenerate the whole file, costing O(file size) per edit. We present an alternative that separates the semantic decision (which resource, field, and value) from the syntactic act of editing the file. The agent emits only a structured field-change intent; a deterministic pipeline indexes manifests by (kind, name), locates the target scalar's exact character span via the YAML parser's node position marks, and replaces only that span in the raw text. Because the file is never re-serialized, the diff is minimal by construction, formatting and comments are preserved, and the edit is correct and deterministic independent of the model, at O(1) generation cost. The contribution is the pairing of an LLM-proposed intent with a deterministic, fail-closed application contract for GitOps. We implement it in KubeAstra (Apache-2.0) and release the benchmark. Our claim is scoped to faithful application of a known change; whether the change is right is left to human PR review.

</details>


### [172] [Empirical Software Engineering in Practice: Insights from Google](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00247)
*Roberto Verdecchia,Justus Bogner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ACM SIGSOFT SEN-ESE专栏推出的"ESE in Practice"系列，旨在通过采访各公司实证软件工程（ESE）实践者，了解ESE在工业界的实施现状。首期邀请了Google开发者智能团队的Ciera Jaspan和Collin Green进行访谈，探讨研究方法的运用、研究选题决策、研究结果的应用及面临的障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界对实证软件工程（ESE）在学术界的应用已有较充分了解，但对ESE在工业界如何实践知之甚少。为了填补这一认知空白，作者希望通过系列访谈揭示工业界ESE实践的真实面貌。

Method: 采用访谈的形式，作为SIGSOFT SEN-ESE专栏的系列文章。首期采访Google Developer Intelligence团队的Ciera Jaspan和Collin Green，访谈内容经过编辑形成忠实记录。

Result: 本文是该系列的第一期，展示了Google开发者智能团队在工业环境中实施实证软件工程的实践经验和见解。

Conclusion: 通过"ESE in Practice"系列访谈，为工业界和学术界搭建沟通桥梁，促进对实证软件工程在真实工业场景中应用的理解与交流。

Abstract: While it is fairly well known how empirical software engineering (ESE) is used in the academic world, we have limited knowledge of how ESE is practiced in industry. As part of our regular column on empirical software engineering (ACM SIGSOFT SEN-ESE), we want to dedicate a series of articles to interviewing ESE practitioners from various companies. Among other things, we want to understand how ESE processes are implemented in industry, e.g., different research methods, how practitioners decide on what to study, how research results are used within companies and beyond, and if they face recurrent impediments to using ESE methods in industrial contexts. In the first edition of "ESE in Practice", we are joined by Ciera Jaspan and Collin Green from the Developer Intelligence team at Google. This article is a faithful account of our conversation from August 13, 2026, which we edited for our column.

</details>


### [173] [Spec-Driven Development for Agentic Software Engineering: Harnessing Human-Agent Teamwork](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00252)
*Jessica Diaz,Joaquin Gayoso,Andrea Cimminio,Jorge Perez*

Main category: cs.SE
