TL;DR: 本文基于灰色文献进行概念分析，提出规范驱动开发（SDD）作为团队规模下智能体软件工程（ASE）的使能性学科，建立了其社会技术模型、技术/方法学"驾驭装具（harness）"的运作化表征，以及五种人机交互模式类型学。SDD以规范为中心重新确立被vibe coding消解的契约（问责性、可验证性、可转移性），但仅作为迈向学术-工业共识的第一步，有待经验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程正从AI辅助（如vibe coding）转向智能体软件工程（ASE），即自动驾驶代理被委派目标级任务。然而业界出现生产力悖论：个体生产力提升的同时，团队吞吐量、评审容量和稳定性却在下降，原因是团队规模的软件工程纪律被忽视。本文旨在为ASE在团队规模下的应用建立概念与方法论基础。

Method: 作者主要基于灰色文献（包括ASE愿景与路线图论文、从业者报告、演讲和工具）进行概念分析。原因在于同行评审证据和共享的学术-工业词汇尚未建立。研究以范式演进比较表征作为概念框架，提出：(i) SDD的社会技术模型，规范作为人与智能体之间的契约基底；(ii) 驾驭装具的运作化表征，区分围绕智能体的技术驾驭装具与围绕团队的方法学驾驭装具，并提供工作示例；(iii) 五类人机交互模式的类型学。

Result: 文章呈现三项成果：SDD的社会技术模型（规范充当人-代理契约基底）、驾驭装具的双层运作化表征（技术层围绕代理、方法学层围绕团队，含工作示例），以及重新定义人类角色的五种人机交互模式类型学。

Conclusion: SDD以规范为中心的形式重新确立了vibe coding所消解的契约：问责性、可验证性和可转移性。鉴于证据基础尚不成熟，本文定位为迈向学术-工业共识的第一步而非已验证理论，并勾勒了未来经验验证的研究议程。

Abstract: Context: Software engineering is moving from AI-assisted practices like vibe coding, in which assistants accelerate individual developers, towards Agentic Software Engineering (ASE), in which autonomous agents are delegated goal-level tasks. However, industry reports a productivity paradox: as individual productivity increases, team throughput, review capacity, and stability degrade because team-scale software engineering discipline is neglected. Objective: This paper aims to establish the conceptual and methodological foundations of Spec-Driven Development (SDD) as an enabling discipline for ASE at team scale and characterize the harness, i.e., the technical and methodological mechanisms through which teams govern agent behavior. Method: We conducted a conceptual analysis drawing predominantly on gray literature, including ASE vision and roadmap papers, practitioner reports, talks, and tooling, because peer-reviewed evidence and a shared academic-industrial vocabulary are not yet established. Results: Using a comparative characterization of the paradigm progression as conceptual framing, the article presents (i) a socio-technical model of SDD in which specifications act as the contract substrate between humans and agents; (ii) an operational characterization of the harness, distinguishing the technical harness around the agent from the methodological harness around the team, with worked examples; and (iii) a typology of five human--agent interaction patterns through which the human role is redefined. Conclusion: We conclude that SDD reconstitutes, in specification-centric form, the contracts that vibe coding dissolves: accountability, verifiability, and transferability. Given the immaturity of the evidence base, this work is presented as a first step toward academic-industrial consensus rather than a validated theory, and outlines a research agenda for future empirical validation.

</details>


### [174] [Exploring Quantum Software Testing Across Research and Practice: Emerging Results from a Multivocal Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00354)
*Rodolfo Gil-Pereira,Ronnie de Souza Santos,Cleyton Magalhaes,Italo Santos*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过多语言文献综述（结合学术论文和灰色文献），初步探讨了量子软件测试在学术与实践领域中的研究现状，揭示了该领域快速演进但碎片化的生态系统及若干核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子软件测试是一个新兴且快速发展的领域，但学术界与工业实践之间对其特征、技术和方法缺乏统一的系统性认识，作者希望通过综合学术与灰色文献来填补这一认知空白。

Method: 采用多语言（multivocal）文献综述方法，将同行评审的学术研究与灰色文献（包括博客、教程、论坛、技术报告、文档页面及公司网页）进行整合分析。

Result: 研究发现量子软件测试生态系统呈现快速演进但碎片化特征，涵盖经典测试方法适配、量子专用技术、统计验证方法、模拟器、调试环境和验证框架等多个维度；同时揭示了可扩展性限制、硬件噪声、概率性执行、有限可观测性及工具链不成熟等反复出现的挑战。

Conclusion: 该研究为量子软件测试领域提供了初步的特征化描述，勾勒出当前研究与实践中关于测试挑战、技术和工具讨论的全貌，为该领域未来的系统性研究奠定基础。

Abstract: This paper presents preliminary findings from a multivocal literature review investigating how quantum software testing is characterized across academic and practitioner-oriented sources. Our study integrated peer-reviewed studies with gray literature, including blogs, tutorials, forums, technical reports, documentation pages, and company webpages. Our results indicate a rapidly evolving but fragmented ecosystem involving classical adapted testing approaches, quantum-specific techniques, statistical validation methods, simulators, debugging environments, and verification frameworks. The reviewed material also revealed recurring challenges related to scalability limitations, hardware noise, probabilistic execution, limited observability, and immature tooling ecosystems. These findings provide an initial characterization of how research and practice currently discuss quantum software testing challenges, techniques, and tooling.

</details>


### [175] [Revisiting Feedback-Driven LLM Code Repair: A Replication and Exploratory Java Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00362)
*Louis Lalonde,Wassim Keddache,Thomas Perron Touchette,Leuson Da Silva,Foutse Khomh*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对FeedbackEval基准进行了部分复现，并探索性地将其扩展到Java语言，评估不同反馈类型对LLM代码修复效果的影响。Python复现验证了原文主要结论，但Java扩展发现测试反馈的优势不再显著，表明结论可能受基准构建、反馈表示和工具生态影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然LLM在自动代码修复中显示出潜力，但其可复现性和可泛化性问题尚未被充分探索，特别是跨语言场景下反馈类型对修复效果的影响是否一致尚不明确。

Method: 1) 在394个Python修复任务上使用GPT-4o和Claude 3.5 Sonnet部分复现原始FeedbackEval研究；2) 构建包含100个错误修复实例（来自50个Java任务）的探索性Java扩展数据集，评估不同反馈类型（包括普通测试反馈和JUnit测试反馈）的有效性。

Result: Python复现中测试反馈仍是最强反馈类型，但Java扩展中简单反馈与JUnit测试反馈无显著差异；更轻量的提示可以降低成本且不影响修复效果。

Conclusion: 原有基于Python的结论对基准构建、反馈表示和工具生态敏感，需要更严格的多语言基准评估以及谨慎的反馈设计来支持LLM修复系统的可靠部署。

Abstract: Since the advent of Large Language Models (LLMs), practitioners have increasingly leveraged them to support their software engineering tasks, including automated code repair, showing promising results. Yet, concerns regarding reproducibility and generalizability remain largely unexplored. To further evaluate these concerns and associated impacts, we partially reproduce and conduct an exploratory Java extension of the FeedbackEval benchmark [1], which evaluates how LLMs leverage different feedback types for Python code repair. First, we partially replicate the original study on 394 repair tasks using GPT-4o and Claude 3.5 Sonnet, reproducing and observing the main qualitative trends reported in the original work. Second, we conduct an exploratory Java extension by constructing 100 erroneous repair instances from 50 Java tasks and evaluating feedback effectiveness. Our results show that previous conclusions from Python may be sensitive to benchmark construction, feedback representation, and tooling ecosystem, motivating more controlled multilingual benchmarks. Specifically, while test feedback remains the strongest feedback type in our Python replication, the same ranking is not observed in our Java extension, as simple and JUnit-based test feedback do not differ significantly. We hypothesize that differences in feedback informativeness and tooling ecosystems, such as the verbosity of test frameworks, may partly explain such a difference. Finally, lighter prompts reduce cost without significant differences in repair effectiveness. Overall, our findings confirm key trends under a partially controlled replication and highlight the need for more rigorous multilingual evaluation and careful feedback design in LLMbased repair systems.

</details>


### [176] [Audit-First Rollback Semantics for Safety-Critical Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00406)
*Xue Qin,Simin Luan,Cong Yang,Zhijun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出审计优先回滚语义（audit-first rollback semantics），一种在故障停机崩溃下保证分布式部署运行时审计链与实时状态一致性的容错机制，并配套暂态状态机（provisional state machines）实现"活跃但未提升"状态的回滚契约与有界截止时间。实验在1,200次故障注入测试中达到100%的审计/实时状态一致性，而故障开启对照仅33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式部署运行时面临一个经典容错框架未明确指出的核心义务：组件配置运行的活动状态与记录其演变过程的审计链必须在每个终止配置上保持一致。现有工作只关注单方面问题（金丝雀控制器、配置回滚、签名证明），忽略了崩溃期间审计/活动一致性的横向问题，缺少在转换中途崩溃时审计链仍能如实反映活动状态的机制。

Method: 提出审计优先回滚语义作为容错机制，在故障停机崩溃期间保证每个已提交终止状态下的审计/活动一致性。该机制与暂态状态机配合工作：状态机流水线中"活跃但未提升"的状态带有明确回滚契约和有界截止时间。作者在两套运行时部署系统中实例化该方法，并与同一管线的故障开启变体进行比较评估，覆盖三个结构故障类别的十二个故障注入点。

Result: 在1,200次注入故障试验中，审计优先回滚实现了100%（600/600）的审计/活动状态一致性（Wilson 95% CI [0.994, 1.000]），单单元p95恢复延迟低于500ms（12/12单元通过SLO）；故障开启变体仅在33%的试验（200/600，Wilson 95% CI [0.297, 0.372]）中保持一致性。

Conclusion: 审计优先回滚语义在不同结构故障类别的多个注入点下显著优于故障开启变体，提供了强有力的审计/活动一致性保障。作者还将该构造推广到跨桥协调协议，并给出了停机故障桥队的安全性论证，将舰队规模的实证评估留作后续工作。

Abstract: Distributed deployment runtimes carry a coherence obligation that classical fault-tolerance frameworks do not name directly: the live state a component is configured to run and the audit chain that records how it got there must agree at every terminal configuration. Prior works mainly focus on individual aspects of the deploy-time fault surface (canary controllers, configuration rollback, signed attestations), leaving the cross-cutting question of audit/live coherence under fail-stop crash only loosely specified. Yet a key systems question remains unresolved: how can a deployment runtime guarantee that the audit chain answers truthfully about live state even when a transition crashes mid-flight? We present audit-first rollback semantics, a fault-tolerance mechanism that guarantees audit/live coherence at every committed terminal under fail-stop crashes during transition phases. The mechanism pairs with provisional state machines, pipelines whose "active but not yet promoted" states carry an explicit rollback contract and a bounded deadline. We instantiate both in a runtime deployment system and run a dependability evaluation against a fail-open variant of the same pipeline across twelve fault-injection points spanning three structural failure classes. Across 1,200 injected-failure trials, audit-first rollback achieves 100% audit/live-state coherence (600/600, Wilson 95% CI [0.994,1.000]) with per-cell p95 recovery latency below 500 ms (SLO PASS in 12/12 cells); the fail-open variant preserves coherence on only 33% of trials (200/600, Wilson 95% CI [0.297,0.372]). We further lift the construction to a cross-bridge coordination protocol with a safety argument for fleets of fail-stop bridges, leaving fleet-scale empirical evaluation to follow-on work.

</details>


### [177] [What Survives the Next Model? Benchmarking LLM-Based Techniques Against Single-Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00468)
*Nahian Salsabil,Joy Saha,Simantika Bhattacharjee Dristi,Nicholas Phair,Nusrat Jahan Mozumder,Matthew B. Dwyer,Sebastian Elbaum*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文分析了ICSE 2026上的35篇基于LLM的技术论文，发现其中37%-63%的复杂工具可以被新一代模型上的单个自动生成的提示词（无需迭代优化）轻松超越，表明大量工程化工作可能只是对暂时性模型缺陷的变通方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程研究社区大量投入LLM集成研究，但这种投资是否具有战略价值尚不清楚，因为新一代前沿模型的原生能力可能迅速使现有技术过时。

Method: 分析ICSE 2026的35篇基于LLM的论文，评估其复杂工具是否能在无迭代优化的情况下被新一代模型上的单个自动生成提示词所超越。

Result: 37%-63%的论文中，新版模型配合单个提示词即可原生超越先前精心设计的工具；建设性技术（如代码生成、修复）更易被单提示词取代；部分存活论文依赖为模型提供额外见解的策略。

Conclusion: 研究结果质疑了将技术设计为对暂时性模型缺陷的变通方案的性价比，强调应聚焦于能与未来模型代际协同扩展的持久性挑战。

Abstract: The software engineering research community has enthusiastically embraced the integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into complex techniques to solve a wide variety of tasks. However, the extent to which this investment is strategic remains unclear, as the native capabilities of successive frontier model generations can rapidly render existing techniques obsolete. To assess this research investment, we analyze 35 LLM-based technique papers from ICSE 2026. We evaluate whether their complex tools can be outperformed by the simplest possible alternative: a single, automatically generated prompt executed on a newer generation model, without any iterative refinement. We find that for between 37% and 63% papers, a newer model with a single prompt natively outperforms the heavily engineered tooling proposed just a year prior. We identify that constructive techniques like code generation or repair are more amenable to substitution by a single-prompt. We also identify a surviving set of papers relying on strategies that provide additional insights to the model where newer LLMs will amplify the proposed technique. Our findings raise questions about the cost-benefit proposition of techniques designed as workarounds to temporary model deficits and the need to focus on enduring challenges that scale synergistically with future model generations. Our source codes and results are made publicly available at https://github.com/less-lab-uva/What-Survives-the-Next-Model.

</details>


### [178] [Runtime-Independent Persistent Agents: Preserving Identity, Memory, and Code Across Models, Harnesses, and Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00546)
*Zhenyu Zhao,Roy Zhao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种与运行时无关的持久智能体架构，将连续性承载层（身份、记忆、软件本体）与可替换的部署绑定层（推理器、执行框架、交互界面）分离，并通过定义六个连续性不变量和一套quiesce-checkpoint-validate-bind-rehydrate-resume协议，实现智能体在更换模型、框架、主机时保持身份和记忆连续性的迁移机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统通常由生产其行为的模型和框架来界定，这种边界对单次执行有效，但对于长期运行的智能体而言过于简化——智能体可能在保留身份、记忆和可执行代码血统的同时更换模型、编排框架、交互会话和宿主服务器，现有架构无法支撑这种持久连续性。

Method: 提出一个双层架构：连续性承载基底P_t=(I_t,M_t,B_t)包含架构化身份表示、私有持久记忆和版本化软件体；可替换部署绑定由执行基底E_t=(R_t,H_t,D_t)（提供推理器、框架和宿主）及一组交互表面S_t组成。部署执行定义为A_t=P_t⋈(E_t,S_t)。定义了六个连续性不变量和五阶段协议（quiesce-checkpoint-validate-bind-rehydrate-resume），并实现Enoch系统作为验证。

Result: Enoch实现了可复用软件体+私有身份/记忆/工作流状态/延续权限的设计，基础设施依赖通过版本化提供者契约隔离。对冻结的公开提交进行干净房运行，通过833项核心测试和92项独立的提供者与库测试；部署实践已验证推理器版本替换、交互界面替换和宿主机替换均能保持连续性承载状态。

Conclusion: 实验证据支持"机械可替换性"和"授权系统连续性"，而非行为不变性或穷尽配对评估。后续关键测量问题是：经过授权延续后的智能体是否仍能回忆、组合并执行其身份。

Abstract: Agent systems are commonly described by the model and harness that currently produce their behavior. That boundary is useful for one execution but underspecifies a long-lived agent that may change models, orchestration harnesses, interaction sessions, and host servers while retaining one identity, memory, and executable code lineage. We present a runtime-independent architecture for persistent agents. A continuity-bearing substrate $P_t=(I_t,M_t,B_t)$ contains an architectural identity representation, private durable memory, and a versioned software body. A replaceable deployment binding comprises an execution substrate $E_t=(R_t,H_t,D_t)$, which supplies a reasoner, harness, and host, and a set of interaction surfaces $S_t$, such as chat, API, or user interface bindings. A deployed execution is $A_t=P_t\triangleright(E_t,S_t)$; changing either replaceable layer is migration, not agent creation, when an authorized protocol preserves attributable lineage and transfers continuation authority within a governed deployment boundary.
  We define six continuity invariants and a quiesce--checkpoint--validate--bind--rehydrate--resume protocol. Enoch realizes the design as a reusable body plus private installed identity, memory, workflow state, and continuation authority, with infrastructure dependencies behind versioned provider contracts. A clean-room run of the frozen public commit passes 833 core tests and 92 provider and library tests executed separately from the core suite; deployments have exercised reasoner-version, interaction-surface, and host-machine substitutions while retaining continuity-bearing state. This evidence supports mechanical substitutability and authorized system continuity, not behavioral invariance or exhaustive pairwise evaluation. The downstream measurement question is whether an authorized continuation still recalls, composes, and enacts its identity.

</details>


### [179] [WiseSpec: Requirements-Driven Agents for Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00568)
*Zhao Tian*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WiseSpec是一个需求驱动的智能体框架，专注于仓库级代码生成，通过自动构建结构化且信息丰富的需求、基于执行的质量评估和迭代细化来生成更正确的代码，平均比基线提升13.17%的%Resolved指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在处理复杂软件工程任务时由于任务描述不完整、模糊或缺乏关键上下文信息而难以生成正确代码，而现有方法主要改进编码智能体的工具、技能和工作流，忽视了任务需求本身的质量。

Method: 受软件需求工程启发，提出WiseSpec框架，包含三个核心环节：自动构建结构化且信息丰富的需求、通过基于执行的评估来评估需求质量、迭代细化和改进需求以更好地引导代码生成。

Result: 实验结果表明WiseSpec在所有基线方法中表现一致最优，在%Resolved指标上平均提升了13.17%。

Conclusion: 通过对任务需求本身进行结构化构建、质量评估和迭代优化，可以有效提升LLM在仓库级代码生成任务中的表现，验证了需求工程思想在代码生成领域的重要价值。

Abstract: Code generation aims to automatically generate source code from task requirements and has attracted significant attention with the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs). Despite remarkable progress, LLMs often struggle to generate correct code for complex software engineering tasks because task descriptions are frequently incomplete, ambiguous, or lack critical contextual information. Existing approaches primarily improve the capabilities of coding agents through more sophisticated tools, skills, and workflows, while largely overlooking the quality of the task requirements themselves. To address this limitation, we draw inspiration from software requirements engineering and propose WiseSpec, a novel requirements-driven agent framework for repository-level code generation. WiseSpec automatically constructs structured and information-rich requirements, assesses their quality through execution-based evaluation, and iteratively refines them to better guide code generation. Experimental results show that WiseSpec consistently outperforms all baselines, achieving an average improvement of 13.17% in %Resolved.

</details>


### [180] [Replacing Training with Memory: Listwise Selection for Text-to-SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00834)
*Yeonseok Jeong,Soyoung Yoon,Seongjun Lee,Seung-won Hwang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需微调的列表式（listwise）选择器MaP-SQL，用于Text-to-SQL系统的候选查询选择。该方法用可复用结构化记忆替代微调学习选择标准，并通过多排列聚合投票来缓解位置偏差，在保持效率的同时提升选择准确性，在BIRD-dev上比现有最优方法R^3-SQL平均高2.02个执行准确率点，且token消耗减少2.92倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Text-to-SQL系统采用"生成-执行-选择"流水线，列表式选择器效果虽好但微调成本高昂。作者旨在提出一种无需微调的高效列表式选择方法，既保持选择准确性又降低训练和推理成本。

Method: MaP-SQL采用两大推理时策略替代微调目标：(1)从训练数据提取可复用结构化记忆，编码自然语言到schema元素、SQL操作和期望输出的映射，作为显式决策标准进行列表式候选评估；(2)通过多输入排列聚合排序来缓解位置偏差，利用执行结果和逐点评分优化推理成本。

Result: 该方法在多个Text-to-SQL基准上表现出更稳定的选择效果且无需微调，比较次数更少。在BIRD-dev上，使用相同候选集时比现有最先进的基于选择器的方法R^3-SQL平均高出2.02个执行准确率点，同时token消耗减少2.92倍。

Conclusion: MaP-SQL证明了通过推理时策略（结构化记忆+排列聚合）可以替代代价高昂的微调，在提升列表式选择准确性的同时保持效率和兼容性，为无微调场景下的Text-to-SQL候选选择提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Modern Text-to-SQL systems often follow generate-execute-select pipelines, generating multiple candidate queries then selecting the best one. Listwise selection, by jointly comparing multiple candidates, has been widely adopted, but fine-tuning listwise selectors is costly. We thus propose a fine-tuning-free listwise selector. We replace two major fine-tuning objectives with inference-time strategies: (1) learning selection criteria as ordering and (2) mitigating positional bias. First, we build reusable structured memories instead of learning selection behavior as model parameters. Given a question, MaP-SQL retrieves memories distilled from training data that encode how natural language maps to schema elements, SQL operations, and expected outputs. These memories serve as explicit decision criteria for evaluating candidates in a listwise manner. Second, to mitigate ordering bias of listwise selectors, we aggregate rankings across multiple input permutations, with inference cost optimized by execution results and pointwise scoring. Our approach improves selection accuracy while maintaining efficiency and compatibility with existing large language models. Across Text-to-SQL benchmarks, it produces more stable selection without fine-tuning and fewer unnecessary comparisons than existing methods. On BIRD-dev, it outperforms the previous state-of-the-art selector-based method R^3-SQL by 2.02 execution accuracy points on average using the same candidate sets, with 2.92x fewer tokens.

</details>


### [181] [Probabilistic Model Checking of Autoregressive Neural Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00838)
*Helge Spieker,Dennis Gross,Arnaud Gotlieb*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于概率模型检验的方法，用于评估自回归神经序列模型在采样下的约束违反概率以及输入群体满足领域需求的比例，通过将模型提取为离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC），使用PRISM验证PCTL规范，并基于CEGAR循环自适应收紧区间，提供测试准确率无法揭示的部署关键信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试准确率无法回答部署神经序列模型时两个关键问题：系统在采样下对约束违反的可达概率质量有多大，以及多大比例的输入群体满足领域需求。仅依赖测试准确率会掩盖生成模型中潜在的不安全行为。

Method: 首先从被测系统的令牌级生成过程中提取离散时间马尔可夫链（DTMC）；然后使用PRISM模型检查器验证形式化的PCTL规范；接着将每个输入的判定结果聚合成覆盖曲线。一个可靠性定理保证了DTMC是欠逼近（under-approximation），使每个判定都能得到真实可达概率的认证区间。通过反例引导的抽象精化（CEGAR）循环自适应收紧区间，并用最大似然算法提取最可能伪造的反例轨迹。

Result: 在两个案例研究中验证了管道有效性。在GPT-2的CAPP模型（测试准确率100%）上，管道量化了贪心解码隐藏但采样可达到的概率质量，并识别出排序需求在群体层面成立所需的最小训练比例；在SMILES分子生成器（词汇量50倍）上，通过外部化学有效性预言器，识别了结构完整性与化学有效性之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该管道提供了一种超越测试准确率的正式验证框架，能够为序列模型的约束满足与群体覆盖提供保守且可证明的保证，对部署安全至关重要。

Abstract: Test-set accuracy is silent on two issues that matter when deploying autoregressive neural sequence models: how much probability mass the system under test (SUT) places on constraint-violating alternatives that are reachable under sampling and what fraction of the input population satisfies a domain requirement. We answer both with probabilistic model checking. The pipeline extracts a discrete-time Markov chain (DTMC) from the SUT's token-by-token generation, verifies formal PCTL specifications with the PRISM model checker, and aggregates the per-input verdicts into a coverage curve over the input space. A soundness theorem establishes the DTMC as an under-approximation, so every verdict yields a certified interval on the SUT's true reachability probability. The coverage built from those verdicts is, therefore, conservative by construction. A counterexample-guided abstraction refinement (CEGAR) loop adaptively tightens the interval, and a maximum-likelihood algorithm extracts the most probable falsifying trace. Two case studies exercise the pipeline. On a GPT-2 computer-aided process-planning (CAPP) model with 100% test accuracy, the pipeline quantifies the probability mass greedy decoding hides, but that is reachable with sampling; and identifies the smallest training fraction at which an ordering requirement holds population-wide, neither of which test accuracy can report. We then verify the SMILES molecular generator with a 50x larger vocabulary. The only change is an external chemical-validity oracle, and the pipeline identifies the gap between structural completeness and chemical validity.

</details>


### [182] [Does Fault Localization Beat a Fresh Attempt? A Placebo-Controlled Study of Test-Guided Code Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00854)
*Anik Jha*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究通过三臂实验（盲法整体重采样、频谱定位后跨嫌疑片段补全、随机代码片段等长补全）对比了代码修复中故障定位策略的效果。结果显示：仅9.0%的失败候选可被定位；在177个可定位候选上，定位式补全显著逊于盲法重采样（3:40, p=3.0×10⁻⁹）；与随机片段安慰剂相比，定位式补全虽总体占优但个体模型上未达显著（最佳Holm p=0.087），故仅报告为"提示性"结论。研究结论限定于24-32B模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直觉认为故障定位能聚焦代码模型的修复，但定位编辑的成功可能仅仅因为修改量小，或第二次模型调用本就可能成功。研究旨在区分这些不同解释，检验故障定位是否真的比盲法重采样更有效。

Method: 采用三臂对照实验设计作用于同一失败候选：（1）盲法整体代码重新采样；（2）基于频谱的故障定位后对嫌疑跨度进行补全；（3）在随机不相干代码片段上进行等长补全作为安慰剂。使用3-4个冻结的24-32B模型、3个基准和488个失败候选进行测试。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）仅9.0%的失败候选暴露了可用频谱的失败公开测试；（2）在177个可定位候选中，定位式补全以3:40的比率显著输给盲法重采样（p=3.0×10⁻⁹），在第三个模型家族中复现（-11.3分，95% CI [-16.6,-6.8]）；（3）与随机安慰剂相比定位式补全pooled占优（11:1, Holm调整p=0.019），但单个模型均未达主导分析显著性（最佳Holm p=0.087）。补全在48.9%的尝试中原样复现了移除的片段。

Conclusion: 故障定位在代码修复中的价值有限：实用性稀缺（仅9.0%可用），且在匹配尝试次数下效果劣于盲法重采样。定位与随机安慰剂的差异仅提示性而非确定性，更多预算不会帮助是因为模型倾向于逐字复现原文。所有结论仅适用于24-32B参数模型。

Abstract: Fault localization can focus a code model's repair on the statements a failing test implicates, but a targeted edit may succeed merely because it is small, and a second model call may succeed without using the failure at all. We separate these explanations with three arms applied to the same failed candidate: blind whole-solution resampling, spectrum-based localization followed by suspect-span infilling, and same-length infilling at a disjoint random code span. Across three frozen 26-32B models, three benchmarks and 488 failing candidates, plus a separately declared 24B fourth model from a third family, three results follow. First, localization is rarely available: only 9.0% of failing candidates expose a failing public test with a usable spectrum. Second, among the 177 candidates localizable from a strong suite, localized infilling loses decisively to blind resampling at a matched attempt count (3:40, p = 3.0 x 10^-9), opposite to our hypothesis; the loss replicates in a third family at -11.3 points (95% CI [-16.6, -6.8]), and widening the edit does not rescue it. Third, against the random-span placebo localized infilling leads pooled (11:1, Holm-adjusted p = .019), but that lead resolves in no individual model under the analysis our shipped plan designates primary (best Holm p = .087), so we report the location effect as suggestive rather than established. Re-pricing attempts as tokens narrows but does not overturn this: a span attempt spends 21.7 generated tokens against 371.1, yet 16 localized attempts reach 6.8% while one blind attempt already reaches 10.1%. Infilling reproduces the removed span verbatim in 48.9% of attempts, which is why more budget does not help. We restrict every localization conclusion to the 24-32B models tested.

</details>


### [183] [Fine-Tuning Large Language Models to Classify Pull Request-Issue Alignments: Going Beyond Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01087)
*Mustafa Yasir Altunhan,Hüseyin Özgür Kamalı,Eray Tüzün*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过微调大型语言模型（LLM）来改进Pull Request与Issue之间的对齐分类，并利用SHAP进行可解释性分析。微调后的模型在准确率、F1-micro、F1-macro和召回率上分别平均提升6.15%、6.15%、14.69%和6.15%，其中CodeLlama-7B表现最佳，代码差异、Issue正文和PR正文对预测影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PR与Issue的准确对齐对于软件开发的效率和质量至关重要，但现有对齐不准确会降低可追溯性、阻碍缺陷定位并降低可维护性。因此需要自动化且更准确的PR-Issue对齐分类方法。

Method: 首先扩展现有数据集并通过数据增强解决类别不平衡问题；然后通过指令微调GPT-4o，并使用分类特定头微调多个开源LLM（CodeLlama-7B、CodeQwen1.5-7B、StableCode-3B、CodeGemma-7B和Deepseek-Coder-6.7B）；最后使用SHAP对表现最佳的开源LLM进行可解释性分析，研究PR-Issue字段对预测的影响。

Result: 微调后的LLM优于基线模型，准确率和F1-micro平均提升6.15%，F1-macro提升14.69%，召回率提升6.15%。CodeLlama-7B是整体表现最佳的微调LLM。可解释性分析显示代码差异、Issue正文和PR正文对预测影响最大。

Conclusion: 微调显著提升了PR-Issue对齐分类的准确性和效率。可解释性分析为理解驱动对齐决策的数据特征提供了可操作的见解，加深了对LLM在软件工件上进行推理的理解。

Abstract: Context: Accurate alignment between pull requests (PRs) and corresponding issues is crucial for efficient software development and maintaining code quality, as misalignments can reduce traceability, hinder defect localization, and decrease maintainability.
  Objective: This study aims to improve automated PR-issue alignment classification by leveraging fine-tuned large language models (LLMs) across multiple alignment categories, and conducts interpretability analysis to investigate the effects of PR-issue fields on the predictions of fine-tuned LLMs.
  Method: Our methodology consists of dataset preparation, LLM fine-tuning, and interpretability analysis. We first extended an existing dataset and applied data augmentation to address class imbalance. GPT-4o was then fine-tuned via instruction tuning, and open-source LLMs including CodeLlama-7B, CodeQwen1.5-7B, StableCode-3B, CodeGemma-7B, and Deepseek-Coder-6.7B were fine-tuned using classification-specific heads. Interpretability analysis using Shapley Additive Explanations (SHAP) was conducted to examine the influence of PR-issue fields on predictions for the best-performing open-source LLM.
  Results: Fine-tuned LLMs outperformed baseline models, achieving average improvements of 6.15% in accuracy and F1-micro, 14.69% in F1-macro, and 6.15% in recall. CodeLlama-7B emerged as the best-performing fine-tuned LLM overall, while interpretability analysis revealed that code diffs together with issue body and PR body contents exert the greatest influence on predictions.
  Conclusions: Fine-tuning substantially enhances PR-issue alignment classification, improving both accuracy and efficiency. Interpretability analysis provides actionable insights into the dataset features driving alignment decisions, deepening understanding of how LLMs reason over software artifacts.

</details>


### [184] [Reliable LLM-Generated Programs for High-Energy Physics Experiments through Graph-Grounded Software Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01095)
*Yue Sun,Tong Liu,Yipu Liao,Jingde Chen,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对物理实验软件生态的LLM代码生成接地（grounding）系统，通过知识图谱混合检索、技能选择的工作流示例和执行引导修复，在ROOT框架基准上显著提升了代码生成的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用大语言模型在粒子物理实验的多阶段分析任务中往往生成不可靠的程序，因为用户的请求很少能完整指定所需的API、依赖关系和使用约定。仅靠用户提示词无法让LLM准确理解复杂软件生态系统中的关系。

Method: 提出一个完整的接地系统，包含三个核心组件：(1) 在异构软件知识图谱上进行混合检索以组织软件间关系；(2) 基于技能选择的工作流示例检索；(3) 执行引导的代码修复机制。以开源ROOT框架作为测试平台，在两种编排方式（Claude Code和独立编排）下评估。

Result: 在275个ROOT任务基准上，接地技术将首次执行成功率从58.5%提升到76.0%（Claude Code编排）和从51.3%提升到64.0%（独立编排）。最终成功率分别从90.5%提升到96.0%和从78.9%提升到90.9%，而每次成功任务的平均生成成本仅增加1.3%和3.2%。在强编码代理下收益仍然显著。

Conclusion: 显式软件知识在已有代理搭建的情况下仍然具有重要价值。该方法捕获的是大型代码库中普遍的软件关系而非ROOT特有事实，因此可以迁移到其他实验框架和专有软件中，尤其是在文档稀疏或内部依赖复杂的场景。

Abstract: Extracting physics information from modern particle-physics experiments requires multistage analyses implemented on top of large and highly interconnected software ecosystems. General-purpose large language models (LLMs) often produce unreliable programs for such tasks because a user request alone rarely specifies the required APIs, dependencies, and usage conventions. We organize these software relations before generation and retrieve task-relevant knowledge at inference time. Using the open-source ROOT framework as a representative and reproducible testbed, we evaluate a complete grounding system that combines hybrid retrieval over a heterogeneous software knowledge graph, skill-selected workflow examples, and execution-guided repair. On a benchmark of 275 ROOT tasks, grounding improves first-attempt execution from 58.5% to 76.0% under Claude Code orchestration and from 51.3% to 64.0% under standalone orchestration. Final success increases from 90.5% to 96.0% and from 78.9% to 90.9%, respectively, while the average generation cost per successful task increases by only 1.3% and 3.2%. The gains persist under a strong coding agent, indicating that explicit software knowledge remains valuable even when agentic scaffolding is already in place. Because the method captures software relations common to large codebases rather than facts specific to ROOT or a particular model, it should transfer to other experiment frameworks and proprietary software, especially where documentation is sparse or internal dependencies are complex.

</details>


### [185] [Hints Help But Do They Teach? Evaluating Skills Transfer in Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01106)
*Will Badr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过可执行评估和机制性实验，系统检验了提示（hint）在代码生成中挽救失败程序的作用机制。结果显示，相关提示确实能挽救许多失败样本，但大多数被挽救的解决方案通过普通采样即可获得；内部干预（激活方向注入）未能在准确率上产生可检测的净收益，提示更多是引导模型走向其已具备能力的解，而非注入缺失信息或实现通用的能力迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当提示将失败的程序生成变为成功时，其本质作用是提供模型缺失的信息，还是仅引导模型走向其已能产生的解？这一核心问题直接关系到对大型语言模型能力边界与提示作用机制的理解。

Method: 在HumanEval+和MBPP+上使用可执行评估，比较相关提示、无关提示和无提示采样三种条件；对Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct和Phi-3.5-mini两个模型进行实验；采用机制性测试（激活方向稳定性分析、低秩干预、隐藏状态探针）验证提示的语义效果是否可分离。

Result: 相关提示和无关提示均能挽救失败（但相关提示更有效）；无提示的采样在更多样本上成功，且覆盖了大部分相关提示挽救的解；激活方向干预产生14次挽救和18次回归，无可检测的净准确率增益；全文本规格优于虚拟KV前缀；跨基准的隐藏状态探针AUROC为0.780-0.806。

Conclusion: 相关提示可以挽救失败生成，但多数被挽救的解已能通过普通采样到达；内部干预测试未建立任务通用的能力迁移。由于不同条件使用了不同的尝试预算，比较结果不能孤立出纯语义效应，提示的作用更接近"引导"而非"信息注入"。

Abstract: When a hint turns a failing generated program into a passing one, does it provide missing information or merely steer the model toward a solution it could already produce? We test these hypotheses on HumanEval+ and MBPP+ using executable evaluation. For Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct, adaptive relevant hints rescue 36 of 79 selected failures; an unrelated hint rescues 19, while eight unhinted samples solve 46 and recover 31 of the 36 relevant-hint rescues. Phi-3.5-mini shows the same pattern: relevant hints rescue 42 of 101 failures, an unrelated hint rescues 17, and unhinted sampling solves 57, including 36 of the 42 relevant-hint rescues. Because the hint conditions use different attempt budgets, these comparisons do not isolate a purely semantic effect. Mechanistic tests on Qwen identify a stable activation direction shared by relevant and unrelated hints. Persistently adding this direction yields 14 rescues and 18 regressions, with no detectable net accuracy gain; learned low-rank interventions have a positive but imprecise estimated effect. Full textual specifications solve 22 of 24 context-defined problems, versus 5-11 for tested virtual-KV prefixes. Post-generation hidden-state probes transfer across benchmarks, with pooled AUROC 0.806 and 0.780, but their top-one selection advantage over token confidence is statistically unresolved. Overall, relevant hints can rescue failures, but most rescued solutions are already reachable through ordinary sampling, and the internal interventions tested here do not establish task-general capability transfer.

</details>


### [186] [Athena: Vulnerability-Affected Library Identification via Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01187)
*Phong Trinh Duy,Trang Dang Yen,Hung Nguyen-Huu,Bach Le,Quyet-Thang Huynh,Dieu Hoang Vu,David Lo,Thanh Le-Cong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Athena是首个基于图的方法用于漏洞受影响的库识别，将漏洞数据库建模为知识图谱，通过知识图谱补全（KGC）进行链接预测，并结合LLM重排序模块，在VulLib数据集上相比最优基线VulLibGen提升了32%的Avg. F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超过一半的漏洞数据库条目包含缺失或错误的受影响库信息，而现有自动化方法忽略了漏洞数据库的关系结构，将识别孤立地视为文本检索问题。

Method: Athena包含三个核心模块：1) 建模模块——构建集成CVE、库、CWE弱点类型、CPE产品和软件生态系统的安全知识图谱；2) 补全模块——应用模块化KGC主干网络通过链接预测预测给定CVE缺失的受影响库；3) 重排序模块——检索KGC候选结果，并使用融合知识图谱嵌入的微调LLM重新评分，联合利用结构和文本信息。

Result: 在VulLib实验上，Athena显著优于四种最先进基线，相比最优基线VulLibGen在Avg. F1上提升32%。仅110M参数的KGC主干即超越VulLibGen在7B参数下的最佳配置，重排序模块进一步带来大幅增益。

Conclusion: 图建模方法在漏洞受影响库识别任务上比纯文本方法更有效，结合图结构信息与LLM重排序可以显著提升识别性能。

Abstract: A single vulnerability in a widely used library can cascade through millions of dependent applications, yet more than half of vulnerability database entries contain missing or incorrect affected-library information. Existing automated approaches neglect the relational structure of vulnerability databases, treating identification as an isolated text retrieval problem. In this paper, we propose Athena, the first graph-based approach for vulnerability affected library identification. Athena models vulnerability databases as a knowledge graph and reformulates the identification problem as knowledge graph completion (KGC). It comprises three key modules: a Modeling module that constructs a security knowledge graph integrating CVEs, libraries, CWE weakness types, CPE products, and software ecosystems; a Completion module that applies a modular KGC backbone to predict missing affected libraries for a given CVE via link prediction; and a Re-ranking module that retrieves KGC candidates and rescores them using a fine-tuned LLM augmented with knowledge graph embeddings, jointly leveraging structural and textual information. Our experiments on VulLib demonstrate that Athena significantly outperforms four state-of-the-art baselines, achieving a 32% improvement in Avg. F1 over the best baseline (i.e., VulLibGen). Notably, our KGC backbone with only 110M parameters already surpasses VulLibGen's best configuration at 7B parameters, demonstrating the effectiveness of graph-based modeling; the re-ranking module then provides substantial further gains, consistently outperforming the best baseline across all evaluated LLM backbones.

</details>


### [187] [Continuous Autonomous Refactoring: A Research Roadmap for AI-Driven Code Quality Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01236)
*Xin Sun,Daniel Ståhl,Kristian Sandahl,Christoph Kessler*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于大语言模型的代码重构从方法级任务扩展到系统级的持续软件维护，并围绕五个核心维度（多目标优化、质量定义与评估、异构信号的多时间尺度集成、架构与设计模式、自主重构的信任）制定了研究路线图，明确了从孤立代码改进走向系统级质量维护的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代码重构方法仅限于方法级任务，缺乏将重构作为持续软件维护组件的系统性视角。作者希望将重构从偶尔手动执行的工具，转变为AI代理持续监控、评估和改进代码库的常态化流程。

Method: 作者提出了一个包含五个维度的研究路线图：(1)多目标优化问题，(2)质量定义与评估，(3)异构信号的多时间尺度集成，(4)架构与设计模式，(5)自主重构的信任问题，并将持续交付管道集成和成本考量作为横切关注点，对每个维度分析挑战并提出开放研究问题。

Result: 该论文是一个观点/路线图类工作，提出了面向自主重构的研究议程框架，明确了五个研究维度及跨切面的挑战与开放问题，为后续研究提供了方向性指引。

Conclusion: 这些维度共同定义了从孤立代码改进推进到系统级质量维护的自主重构研究议程，为LLM驱动的持续化代码重构指明了未来的研究方向。

Abstract: Large language models have shown promising capabilities in code refactoring, but existing approaches remain limited to method-level tasks. In this paper, we envision LLM-based refactoring as a continuous component of software maintenance rather than a tool invoked only for occasional manual refactoring. Under this vision, AI agents continuously monitor, evaluate, and improve codebases against explicit and evolving notions of software quality. We present a roadmap organized around five dimensions: the multi-objective optimization problem, quality definition and evaluation, multi-timescale integration of heterogeneous signals, architecture and design pattern, and trust in autonomous refactoring. We further identify integration into continuous delivery pipelines and cost considerations as cross-cutting concerns. For each dimension, we analyze the underlying challenges and pose open research questions. These dimensions define a research agenda for advancing autonomous refactoring from isolated code improvements to system-level quality maintenance.

</details>


### [188] [What Does an Agentic Software Engineering Benchmark Measure? Profiling Task Demands and Agent Behaviour Beyond What Category Labels Reveal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01271)
*Radin Shayanfar,Keheliya Gallaba,Ahmed E. Hassan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种三轴任务特征刻画框架（Spread–Novelty–Centrality, SNC），用于刻画代码仓库级编程任务的实际工程需求。作者对五个常用基准和两个模型家族（共14,922条轨迹）进行分析，发现：类别标签无法可靠反映任务需求；智能体行为暴露了人类金标准标注无法揭示的需求差异；任务需求与成功率一致相关，但成功的行为特征因模型家族而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体软件工程基准通常用"缺陷修复"或"功能实现"等名义类别标签来概括，但相同标签下的基准经由完全不同的数据构建流程生成，标签无法反映任务实际要求的工程工作量。研究者希望提出一种更精确、可量化的任务需求刻画方法，以替代不可靠的类别标签。

Method: 提出SNC概貌（Spread–Novelty–Centrality），基于实证软件工程研究构建三轴特征：Spread（扩散度）、Novelty（新颖度）、Centrality（中心性）。将该框架应用于五个广泛使用的基准，并对两个模型家族（Claude和Qwen）在三个规模下的14,922条轨迹进行分析，比较标签、人类金标准解和智能体行为之间的差异。

Result: 三个主要发现：（1）类别标签不可靠——任意两对基准在至少两个SNC轴上都有统计显著差异，且差异可追溯到具体的数据构建决策；（2）智能体行为揭示了人类金标准解无法揭示的任务需求——在问题陈述省略提示时智能体产出比金标准更大的解，而在数据构建膨胀金标准时产出更小，任务表述方式直接影响智能体产出；（3）任务需求与成功率一致相关——所有家族和规模下，成功运行集中在低SNC区域，但成功的行为特征因家族而异：Claude通过匹配金标准解的范围获得成功（文件匹配份额从最小规模的0.17升至最大规模的0.54），Qwen在每个规模都通过超出金标准范围来成功，而编辑过少对两个家族都标志着失败。

Conclusion: 基准的类别标签不足以反映编码任务的实际工程需求，SNC三轴概貌提供了更可靠的替代刻画。智能体在任务上的实际行为是评估基准质量的重要信号，能补充人类金标准解的不足。任务需求与模型表现之间存在一致但家族特有的相关性，这对基准设计和智能体评估方法具有启示意义。

Abstract: Agentic software engineering benchmarks are typically summarized by nominal category labels such as "bug fix" or "feature implementation," yet benchmarks carrying the same label are built through very different curation pipelines. A label thus reveals little about the engineering work a benchmark demands. We introduce the Spread--Novelty--Centrality (SNC) profile, a three-axis characterization of the demands of repository-level coding tasks, grounded in empirical software engineering research. We apply the profile to five widely used benchmarks and 14,922 trajectories of two model families at three scales, and report three findings. (1) A label is an unreliable proxy for task demands, as every pair of benchmarks is statistically separated on at least two SNC axes, and the separations trace back to specific curation decisions. (2) Agent behaviour reveals demands that the human-written gold solution cannot. Agents produce larger solutions than the gold where problem statements withhold hints and smaller ones where curation inflates the gold. How a task is phrased shapes what an agent produces. (3) Task demands correlate with success uniformly, with resolved runs concentrating in the low-SNC region for every family and scale, whereas the behavioural signatures of success are family-specific. Claude succeeds by matching the scope of the gold solution, and its parity share on files rises from $0.17$ at the smallest scale to $0.54$ at the largest. Qwen succeeds by exceeding the gold scope at every scale, and editing too little marks failure for both families.

</details>


### [189] [HarnessDev: Can LLMs Create and Evolve Their Own Agent Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01437)
*Yuhao Wu,Jingyuan Zhang,Jiajun Shi,Xinping Lei,Qingshui Gu,Yuxuan Zhang,Zexuan Wang,Chen He,Chen Huang,Maojia Song,Zhiyuan Zeng,Shaowen Wang,Jinkai Liu,Yunfeng Shi,Jiaheng Liu,Shen Yan,Wenhao Huang,Ge Zhang,Wenxuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出HarnessDev基准，将智能体评估从任务输出转向可运行的基础设施（agent harness），包含Creation（从零构建执行系统）和Evolution（基于下游反馈迭代优化）两个阶段，覆盖6个创建模型、4个领域和5个下游基准共2207个实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估通常只报告在特定harness下的下游任务性能，忽视了模型自身开发harness的能力。随着智能体从研究原型转向实际部署，harness对任务表现的影响日益显著，需要一个新的评估维度来关注模型构建运行基础设施的能力。

Method: HarnessDev基准采用两阶段评估：Creation阶段让智能体从最小种子和少量用例出发构建完整执行系统；Evolution阶段基于下游执行反馈迭代修订自身创建的harness。每个构建的harness在能力（保留基准上的任务成功率）和效率（执行token成本）两个维度上被评估，并设置隐藏评估任务防止开发数据泄漏。

Result: 在代码和搜索/研究任务上，生成的harness仍明显落后于成熟的人工工程参考；在写作和机器学习实验任务上，生成harness达到或超过参考水平，但执行成本差异很大。Evolution能带来一定性能提升，但结果不稳定且对保留任务的迁移有限。实验还表明性能提升强烈依赖于执行harness的模型，跨模型迁移有限。

Conclusion: 当前模型构建agent harness的能力在部分领域已接近甚至超过人工参考，但在复杂领域仍有显著差距；Evolution改进不稳定且通用性不足，且harness性能对执行模型具有强依赖性，表明该方向仍需大量研究投入。

Abstract: As agents move from research prototypes to deployed tools, their capability increasingly depends on model-external execution infrastructure, commonly termed the agent harness. Changing this harness while holding model weights fixed can substantially alter task performance. Current agent evaluations typically report downstream performance under a chosen harness, leaving a model's ability to develop the harness itself comparatively underexplored. We introduce HarnessDev, a benchmark that shifts the unit of evaluation from task outputs to runnable infrastructure. HarnessDev covers two stages. In Creation, the agent starts from a minimal seed and a small number of cases, then builds a complete execution system. In Evolution, it starts from its own created harness and iteratively revises it using downstream execution feedback, with the goal of improving benchmark performance. We then evaluate each constructed harness on capability (task success on held-out benchmarks) and efficiency (execution-token cost). The reported Creation results cover six creator LLMs, four domains, and five downstream benchmarks totaling 2,207 unique downstream instances, with hidden evaluation tasks withheld from development. We find that generated harnesses remain substantially behind mature human-engineered references on code and on search and research, while matching or exceeding the selected references on writing and machine-learning experimentation, with large variation in execution cost. Evolution produces some performance gains, but they are unstable and transfer only partially to held-out tasks. Experiments with a fixed runtime model further show that the gains depend strongly on the model executing the harness, indicating limited transfer across models.

</details>


### [190] [The Data Problem in Software Vulnerability Analysis: Artifacts, Quality, and Consumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01503)
*Yu Nong,Yao Du,Tianxiang Xu,Haipeng Cai*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对软件漏洞分析领域的数据集进行了一等公民式的研究，通过一个数据集中心化的分类体系（包含数据是什么、质量如何、用途为何三个维度），系统审查了2016-2026年间1522篇论文中的数据集，深度编码了111篇锚定论文，揭示了漏洞数据集在真实性、标注证据、泄漏处理等方面的系统性缺陷，并构建了一条"证据阶梯"来描述不同数据类型的可信度层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习和LLM的软件漏洞分析的可信度完全取决于其训练和评估数据的质量，但数据集本身很少被作为首要研究对象进行审视。作者试图填补这一空白，对漏洞分析背后的数据质量进行系统性、可验证的评估。

Method: 提出了一个数据集中心化的分类体系，从三个维度区分数据：是什么（代码、元数据、补丁、测试/PoC、推理、轨迹）、质量如何（真实性、标签证据、规模、多样性、泄漏、可用性）以及用途。系统汇编了2016-2026年间1522篇论文（含更早的基础工作），深度编码了分层选取的111篇锚定论文，每个肯定性的分级值都附有逐字原文作为证据支撑，并按属性分别统计研究覆盖度和数据集达成度。

Result: 揭示了漏洞数据集的"证据阶梯"：可执行工件是唯一主要类型中15/24的数据集既达到真实世界分级又含有经独立核验的标签；代码样本数据集（最大类别）最不真实——41个中仅20个从真实项目或CVE中提取漏洞，仅3个保持部署单元粒度，仅2个两者兼备（且仅是粗略的组件测试），仅1个达到完整上下文真实世界的严格要求。泄漏问题在90个适用的数据集中有49个未处理；超四分之一未说明可用性；推理数据近期才出现且多为模型生成；主要轨迹语料仅限3个数据集。

Conclusion: 漏洞分析数据集存在严重的真实性与标注可信度问题，尤其集中在规模最大的代码样本数据集上。作者呼吁将数据集作为一等公民对待，并建议研究者关注泄漏治理、可用性披露以及推理和轨迹数据的建设质量。

Abstract: Learning- and LLM-based software vulnerability analysis is only as trustworthy as the data it is trained and evaluated on, yet that data is rarely examined as a first-class object. We investigate the data behind vulnerability analysis through a dataset-centric taxonomy that separates what an artifact is (code, metadata, patches, tests/PoCs, reasoning, traces), how good it is (realism, label evidence, scale, diversity, leakage, availability), and what it is used for. From a systematically assembled corpus of 1522 papers covering 2016-2026 plus foundational earlier work we deep-code a tiered set of 111 anchor papers, backing every affirmative rubric-graded value with a verbatim span, and we report, per attribute, both how much it has been studied and how well datasets achieve it. The results trace an evidence ladder: executable artifacts are the only major type where 15 of the 24 datasets are both graded real-world and carry labels that received an independent check, while code-sample datasets-the largest category in both the auto-tagged corpus and the anchor set-are the least realistic: 20 of the 41 draw their vulnerabilities from authentic projects or CVEs, but only 3 keep the sample at the unit the code is deployed in, and only 2 do both-though these are coarse component tests, and just one code-sample dataset meets the codebook's stricter full-context real-world grade. Among these, leakage goes unaddressed by 49 of the 90 datasets where it applies, more than a quarter say nothing about availability, reasoning data has arrived only recently and is mostly model-generated, and primary trace corpora remain limited to three datasets, the total after a corpus-wide screen and a full-text check of every candidate it surfaced, with further datasets releasing traces secondarily behind benchmarks and harnesses.

</details>


### [191] [Adaptive Critical Token-Aware Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01601)
*Kefeng Duan,Dewu Zheng,Yanlin Wang,Terry Yue Zhuo,Mingwei Liu,Jianxing Yu,Jiachi Chen,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出ACToR，一种自适应关键令牌感知的检索框架，用于仓库级代码生成任务。ACToR在自回归生成过程中识别关键令牌，并在必要时触发定向检索，为这些决定性位置提供仓库上下文。同时设计了位置感知加权方法优化稠密检索器。在RepoExec和CoderEval两个基准上分别取得8.4%和15.4%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有仓库级代码生成方法虽然采用检索增强生成（RAG）提供仓库上下文，但通常仅将上下文作为任务级支持，未能明确识别生成过程中需要细粒度仓库上下文的关键令牌位置。LLM生成的错误往往集中在少数决定性位置（即关键令牌），一旦这些位置生成错误，后续代码会沿错误语义路径发展，最终导致功能失败。

Method: 1) 提出ACToR框架，在生成过程中动态识别关键令牌；2) 对关键令牌的位置触发按需定向检索，提供仓库上下文；3) 设计位置感知加权方法用于稠密检索器，优先选择对生成更具信息量的上下文。

Result: 在RepoExec和CoderEval两个代表性仓库级基准上，ACToR一致优于最先进方法，相对提升分别为8.4%和15.4%。此外，系统量化了关键令牌的影响，揭示了它们在生成失败中的核心作用。

Conclusion: ACToR证明了针对关键令牌的定向检索策略的重要性，关键令牌在主要生成失败中扮演核心角色，凸显了细粒度按需检索的必要性。代码和数据已开源。

Abstract: The repository-level code generation task requires synthesizing code that satisfies task requirements while remaining consistent with the target repository context. Since real-world repositories often exceed the input length limits of LLMs, existing approaches commonly adopt retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to provide repository-specific context. Despite improving repository-context retrieval, existing methods typically provide context as task-level support, without explicitly identifying the critical tokens that require fine-grained repository context during generation. During the autoregressive generation process of LLMs, errors often concentrate at a small number of decisive positions: once such tokens are generated incorrectly, subsequent code may follow an incorrect semantic path and eventually lead to functional failure. We refer to these positions as "critical tokens". In this paper, we propose ACToR, an adaptive critical token-aware retrieval framework for repository-level code generation. ACToR identifies critical tokens during generation and triggers targeted retrieval on demand to provide repository context at these decisive positions. In addition, we design a position-aware weighting method for dense retrievers to prioritize context that is more informative for generation. We evaluate ACToR on two representative repository-level benchmarks, RepoExec and CoderEval. Experimental results show that ACToR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving relative improvements of 8.4% on RepoExec and 15.4% on CoderEval. Beyond performance gains, we systematically quantify the impact of critical tokens, revealing their central role in major generation failures and highlighting the necessity of targeted retrieval strategies. We provide the code and data at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/ACToR.

</details>


### [192] [Efficient SWE Agent Benchmarking via Trajectory-Aware Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01603)
*Kefeng Duan,Dewu Zheng,Yanlin Wang,Xiwen Wang,Ensheng Shi,Xilin Liu,Yuchi Ma,Jiachi Chen,Mingwei Liu,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出PTA-IRT框架，利用智能体执行轨迹作为特权信息（超越单纯通过/失败结果），用于校准子集选择和能力估计，以低成本高效评估软件工程智能体性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软件工程智能体评估成本高昂，且已有高效评估方法（如选择代表性子集）仅依赖结果信号（通过/失败矩阵或静态任务语义），忽略了智能体的求解过程信息，导致评估精度受限。

Method: 提出PTA-IRT（Privileged Trajectory-Aware Item Response Theory）框架，将历史执行轨迹中的过程级证据（如探索上下文、尝试编辑、解题路径）作为特权信息，融合过程与结果信号，用于校准子集选择和能力估计。

Result: 在四个SWE基准测试上，低校准预算条件下，PTA-IRT在分数恢复和排名恢复方面持续优于现有的IRT基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过利用执行轨迹作为特权信息，可以更准确地在低预算下评估软件工程智能体，代码和数据已公开。

Abstract: Evaluating software engineering agents on realistic benchmarks is costly, since each task may require multi-step code exploration, modification, and test execution. Existing efficient evaluation methods select representative subsets to estimate full-benchmark performance, but are largely result-only: they fit historical pass/fail response matrices or static task semantics, discarding how agents solve problems. We propose PTA-IRT, a Privileged Trajectory-Aware Item Response Theory framework that fuses process and outcome signals. Historical execution trajectories supply process-level evidence beyond pass/fail, such as explored context, attempted edits, and solving paths, which PTA-IRT uses as privileged information for calibration subset selection and ability estimation. Under low calibration budgets, PTA-IRT consistently outperforms prior IRT baselines on score and ranking recovery across four SWE benchmarks. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/DeepSoftwareAnalytics/PTA-IRT.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [193] [HyperWorld: Hypergraph-Structured State Serialization Improves Learned Textual World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00002)
*Yun-Jian Zhang,Chen-Wei Liang,Tian-Yi Zhang,Jian Ding,Yi-Lun Wu,Ao-Bo Li,Wei-Cong Su,Saifullah,Hong-Yu An,Mu-Jiang-Shan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究文本环境中世界模型的状态序列化方式，提出HyperWorld对照研究，对比原始观测与三种符号序列化（独立句、成对三元组、以实体为中心的超边单元），发现超边序列化在小模型（0.5B-1.5B）和分布偏移场景下效果最佳，并在下游贪心规划中取得最高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本环境中，语言模型代理需要从序列化的状态描述中学习符号动作效果，但状态序列化结构的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出HyperWorld对照研究，比较原始观测与三种基于相同真实状态的符号序列化表示：独立句子、成对三元组、以及将多个相关事实围绕实体和关系分组的实体中心超边单元。所有变体使用相同的训练目标：给定状态和动作，预测符号效果或判断动作不可行。

Result: 超边序列化在0.5B-1.5B模型和分布偏移下增益最明显；更大模型缩小了差距；成对三元组在分布内精确匹配上可达或略超超边，但超边在分布外事实F1和中小规模模型上的可行性与效果预测权衡最佳；下游贪心规划中超边世界模型成功率最高。

Conclusion: 高阶状态组织是学习符号世界模型简单而有效的归纳偏置，尤其在模型容量有限或测试环境与训练存在差异时更为重要。

Abstract: World models enable language-model agents to predict environment dynamics and plan before acting. In text environments, the model must learn symbolic action effects from serialized state descriptions, but the role of serialization structure remains underexplored. We present HyperWorld, a controlled study of state serialization for learned textual world models. We compare raw observations with three symbolic serializations of the same ground-truth state: independent sentences, pairwise triples, and entity-centered hyperedge units that group multiple related facts around entities and relations. All variants use the same training objective: given a state and an action, predict symbolic effects or judge the action infeasible. Across model scales, data budgets, and in-distribution and out-of-distribution test worlds, hyperedge serialization gives the clearest gains for 0.5B--1.5B models and under distribution shift. Larger models reduce the gap, and pairwise triples can match or slightly exceed hyperedges on in-distribution exact match, but hyperedges achieve the strongest out-of-distribution fact F1 and the best small-to-medium scale trade-off between feasibility detection and effect prediction. In downstream greedy planning, the hyperedge world model also attains the highest success rate among the tested representations. These results show that higher-order state organization is a simple but effective inductive bias for learned symbolic world models, especially when model capacity is limited or test environments differ from training.

</details>


### [194] [I-CARE: Analysis of interference-related phenomena in a controllable, diverse and representative unlearning setting for text-to-image models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00003)
*Leonardo Santiago Benitez Pereira,Marcos Escudero Viñolo,Luis Herranz Arribas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出I-CARE方法论，将"干扰"（interference）形式化为生成式机器遗忘中的一等研究对象，提供任务、指标和报告模板的正式定义，支持跨遗忘设置的系统化可复现研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式机器遗忘领域虽然进展迅速，但语义相关概念被意外遗忘（即干扰）这一问题缺乏清晰的刻画和一致的评估标准，阻碍了该领域的系统化研究。

Method: I-CARE不提出新基准或新遗忘算法，而是提供一套方法论框架：为任务、指标以及结果报告模板给出正式定义，并配套开源实现和基于Web的图形界面以促进探索。

Result: 通过使用最先进的遗忘算法和常用数据集进行可行性演示，结果表明I-CARE能够支持对多种遗忘设置下干扰模式的有意义分析，验证了该框架的实际适用性。

Conclusion: I-CARE为生成式机器遗忘中的干扰研究提供了统一且可持续的评估范式，将长期科学洞见与短期经验结果解耦，为该领域的未来研究奠定了方法基础。

Abstract: Machine unlearning studies the removal of knowledge from an AI model, making the system forget a concept it previously learned. Despite rapid progress in generative machine unlearning, the unintended degradation of semantically related concepts that should have been retained (henceforth, interference) remains poorly characterized and inconsistently evaluated. This paper introduces I-CARE, a methodology that formalizes interference as a first-class object of study in generative unlearning. Rather than proposing a new benchmark or unlearning algorithm, I-CARE provides formal definitions for tasks, metrics, and templates for reporting results, enabling the systematic and reproducible study of interference across unlearning settings. While our methodology is designed to remain valid as models and unlearning algorithms evolve, decoupling long-term scientific insight from transient empirical results, we present a feasibility demonstration with state-of-the-art algorithms and frequently used datasets. The results demonstrate that I-CARE enables meaningful analysis of interference patterns across multiple unlearning settings, establishing the practical applicability of the framework. The software implementation of the methodology is provided in an open-source framework, together with a web-based graphical interface that enables exploration of the outcomes of this study without requiring direct interaction with the codebase or specialized data analysis tools.

</details>


### [195] [Discrete-Time MDP Modeling for Multi-Item Capacitated Lot Sizing with Stochastic Demand Timing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00004)
*Léa Bayati,Mohamed Dahmoune,Melek Rodoplu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了有限时域、多品项、有产能限制的批量调度问题，其中需求量确定但需求到达时期随机。问题被建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），并提出了遗传算法（GA）来搜索可行的状态反馈策略。在330个基准实例上，GA的平均最优性差距约为3.44%，在困难实例上保持在5%阈值以下，并实现了平均6.89倍的优化加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统批量调度研究通常假设需求到达时间确定性已知，但实际生产中需求到达时期往往具有随机性。现有模型难以在需求层面准确刻画产能竞争、需求特定积压和分配相关的库存动态，需要开发能够处理随机到达时序的模型和求解方法。

Method: 将随机问题建模为离散时间马尔可夫决策过程（DTMDP），包含状态空间、可行动作、转移核和单期成本函数；通过将随机实例与确定性对应物（以最可能到达时期替代）对比隔离随机时序的计算影响；提出遗传算法（GA）在状态反馈策略空间上搜索，并利用DTMDP转移模型对每个策略进行精确评估；对无法精确求解的实例使用经验Bellman时间回归估计缺失的精确求解时间以推算GA加速比。

Result: 对比实验显示随机时序显著增加了状态数、转移数、求解时间和内存压力。在330个基准实例上，GA平均最优性差距约3.44%；在90个困难实例上，GA低于5%最优性差距阈值，平均优化加速比为6.89±1.41（95%置信水平）。

Conclusion: 研究表明随机需求到达时序会显著增加问题的计算复杂度，但所提出的遗传算法能够在可接受的次优范围内高效逼近精确解，为难以精确求解的大规模随机时序批量调度问题提供了实用求解方案。

Abstract: This paper studies a finite-horizon multi-item capacitated lot-sizing problem in which demand quantities are deterministic, while demand-arrival periods are stochastic. Each demand occurs once within a known time window and must be satisfied no later than its deadline. The proposed model makes production and allocation decisions at the demand level, allowing it to represent capacity competition, demand-specific backlog, and allocation-dependent inventory dynamics. The stochastic problem is formulated as a discrete-time Markov decision process (DTMDP), including the state space, feasible actions, transition kernel, and one-period cost function. To isolate the computational effect of stochastic timing, each stochastic instance is first compared with a deterministic counterpart in which each arrival distribution is replaced by its most likely arrival period. This comparison shows that stochastic timing substantially increases the number of states, the number of transitions, solution time, and memory pressure. A genetic algorithm (GA) is then proposed for the stochastic-timing problem. The GA searches over feasible state-feedback policies and evaluates each policy exactly under the DTMDP transition model. Computational experiments on 330 benchmark instances show that the GA remains close to the exact stochastic solution whenever the latter is available, with an average optimality gap of about $3.44\%$. On the difficult benchmark instances, comprising 90 test cases, the GA remains below the $5\%$ optimality-gap threshold and achieves an average optimization speedup of $6.89 \pm 1.41$ at the $95\%$ confidence level. For instances that cannot be solved exactly on the available hardware, an empirical Bellman-time regression is used to estimate the missing exact resolution time and extrapolate the expected GA speedup.

</details>


### [196] [Long-Horizon State Tracking in LLMs: Executing MD5 through a Deep Sequence of Dependent Tool Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00012)
*Dheeraj Mohandas Pai,Lu Xian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过让LLM逐步计算MD5哈希（196个依赖工具调用、64轮、四字状态携带），干净地测试长程任务中精确状态跟踪能力，发现gpt-oss-120b在多数完成运行中能正确完成，并提出不改变权重即可提升成功率的两项关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估缺乏能干净隔离长程任务中状态跟踪困难的基准测试；现有agentic基准混淆了其中多种因素，无法定位失败根因，因此需要一种能纯粹测试模型跨多步工具调用精确携带中间状态能力的设置。

Method: 让模型逐步计算MD5哈希（196个依赖工具调用、64轮），从零实现RFC 1321算法，每步与ground-truth轨迹对齐并逐位校验摘要。在最强设置中用第二个LLM替换所有原始工具（驱动者+工作者），并使用思维链保留和投票去消除模块算术错误。

Result: gpt-oss-120b（混合专家模型，每token仅约55亿激活参数）在温度0和短固定提示下，能在多数完成的运行中跨196次调用携带完整状态并返回正确摘要。投票和保留推理是关键成功因素。

Conclusion: 长程任务的失败可按来源分类，分为状态携带、算术错误和服务问题；即使不使用精确算术外部工具，通过保留模型自身推理和投票机制，LLM可以完成长程精确状态跟踪任务。

Abstract: Long-horizon tasks remain uncommon in large language model (LLM) evaluation, and for a reason: when each step depends on the last, per-step accuracy that looks excellent in isolation decays catastrophically, as errors cascade and the end-to-end failure probability grows sharply with length. Existing agentic benchmarks report end-to-end success but confound this state-tracking difficulty with instruction interpretation, give no control group that isolates it, and are vulnerable to shortcuts such as a hallucinated final answer, so they cannot say why a long run fails. Whether an LLM can carry exact intermediate state across many tool calls at all is itself not well established. We test this cleanly by having the model compute a cryptographic hash, MD5, step by step: a sequence of $196$ dependent tool calls over $64$ rounds while it carries four $32$-bit words $(a,b,c,d)$ in its own context from one call to the next. Interpretation is trivial and, because we implement MD5 from scratch (RFC~1321), we align every call to the ground-truth trace and check the digest to the bit, so any failure is pure bookkeeping. gpt-oss-120b, a mixture-of-experts model with only $\sim$5.5B active parameters per token, at temperature $0$ with a short fixed prompt, carries the full state across all $196$ calls and returns the correct digest on a majority of completed runs. In the strongest setting we replace every primitive tool with a second LLM, so a driver and a worker compute the whole hash from scratch with no exact-arithmetic oracle in the loop. Two ingredients decide success and neither changes the weights: keeping the model's own reasoning in its context each turn, and voting over a thinking-enabled worker to remove its modular-arithmetic slips. We localize the residual failures by origin, separating state-carrying from arithmetic and from serving.

</details>


### [197] [Incremental Risk Assessment of Progressive Elder Financial Scams via Instruction-Tuned Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00005)
*Parviz Ghafariasl,Weimin Fu,Xiaolong Guo,Shing I. Chang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于累积回合的风险评估框架，用于在多轮对话中动态检测针对老年人的金融诈骗。作者构建了包含投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景的多轮对话数据集（每段对话2-8轮），并在每个累积阶段标注风险等级、风险分数、解释理由和安全建议。四种小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1和Qwen3）在统一训练框架下进行微调，其中Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在回合感知风险估计方面表现更优，适合移动端和资源受限环境下的实时诈骗防护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对老年人的金融诈骗越来越多地通过文本和语音渠道（如邮件、短信和电话）发生，诈骗过程跨越多个对话回合，从伪装或随意接触开始，通过建立信任和制造紧迫感逐步升级，最终要求敏感信息或转账。由于风险信号在回合间逐步显现，有效检测需要模型在资源受限的部署环境中持续更新风险评估，这是一个尚未充分解决的问题。

Method: 提出累积回合制风险评估框架，逐步聚合对话回合并在每一步重新评估风险。构建多轮对话数据集，覆盖投资、慈善和技术支持三类诈骗场景，每段对话包含2-8个回合，并在每个累积阶段标注定性风险等级、连续风险分数、解释性理由和安全建议。对四种小型语言模型（Phi-4、LLaMA-3.2、DeepSeek-R1、Qwen3）在统一框架下进行微调和评估。

Result: 微调后的小模型能够捕捉与欺诈相关的语言线索和跨回合的升级模式，同时保持紧凑的架构。在所有评估模型中，Phi-4和LLaMA-3.2在与其参数规模相对应的回合感知风险估计方面表现更强。

Conclusion: 结构化累积建模能够在面向部署的环境中支持增量式诈骗风险评估，同时展示了紧凑型语言模型在隐私保护和设备端欺诈防护方面的潜力。

Abstract: Financial scams targeting older adults increasingly occur through text and voice channels such as email, SMS, and phone calls, unfolding over multiple conversational turns that begin with impersonation or casual contact, escalate through trust building and urgency, and culminate in requests for sensitive information or financial transfers. Because risk signals emerge incrementally across turns, effective detection requires models that continuously update risk estimates under resource-constrained deployment settings. We propose a cumulative turn-based risk assessment framework that incrementally aggregates conversational turns and re-estimates risk at each step, enabling dynamic scam monitoring across progressively evolving conversations. A multi-turn dialogue dataset is constructed to cover investment, charity, and tech support scam scenarios, with each dialogue containing two to eight turns and annotated at every cumulative stage with a qualitative risk level, a continuous risk score, an explanatory rationale, and a safety recommendation. Four small language models (Phi-4, LLaMA-3.2, DeepSeek-R1, and Qwen3) are fine-tuned and evaluated under a unified training framework. Fine-tuned small models capture fraud-related linguistic cues and cross-turn escalation patterns while maintaining compact architectures suitable for mobile and resource-constrained deployment settings. Among the evaluated models, Phi-4 and LLaMA-3.2 achieve stronger turn-aware risk estimation performance relative to their parameter scale. These results suggest that structured cumulative modeling can support incremental scam risk assessment in deployment-oriented settings while highlighting the potential of compact language models for privacy-aware and on-device fraud protection.

</details>


### [198] [EULER: Exploring Underused Links with Evidence-Checked Return for Multi-Agent Mathematical Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00032)
*Ren Zhenzhuo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EULER是一个多智能体系统，以"桥梁"（跨数学领域的问题转移）为搜索单位，在120个近期组合学猜想上产生了10个证明、3个反例和45个部分结果，通过桥接特有的压力测试将错误结论从9降至3，且桥接材料与目标域本地操作结合产生了+4.2的正面交互效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学社区使用不同的对象、不变量和工具，跨领域转移问题成本高昂且常被跳过。作者希望设计一个系统，将这种跨领域转移本身作为搜索的基本单元，以更有效地解决数学猜想。

Method: EULER围绕固定猜想运行三条竞争路线（直接域、相邻域、远域）；一条桥梁仅在提供源表示无法执行的操作且目标侧证据沿已验证蕴含返回原始陈述时保留预算；六项有序压力测试在昂贵搜索开始前拒绝无效桥梁。实验采用120个预冻结且经过污染筛查的近期猜想，数据来自近期在组合学顶级期刊JCTA发表论文的作者。

Result: EULER产出10个证明、3个反例及45个范围限定的部分结果。消融实验显示：桥接特有压力测试将错误结论从9降至3；桥梁材料与目标域本地操作结合产生+4.2的正交互（单独任一因素都无法实现）。

Conclusion: 域距离不能可靠预测成功，可执行的操作增益和有效的返回路径才是成功的关键预测因素。这提示数学发现系统中跨域推理的价值应基于操作能力而非语义相似度来评估。

Abstract: Mathematical communities work with different objects, invariants, and tools, so transferring a problem across them is expensive and often skipped. We present EULER, a multi-agent system that takes such a transfer--a bridge--as its unit of search. Around a fixed conjecture, EULER runs direct, adjacent-domain, and distant-domain routes in competition; a bridge keeps its budget only if it supplies an operation the source representation cannot execute and its target-side evidence returns to the original statement along a checked implication. Six ordered stress tests reject invalid bridges before expensive search begins.
  We evaluate EULER on 120 recent conjectures. The conjectures were frozen before search and screened for contamination, and are drawn from public papers by authors who had recently published in the Journal of Combinatorial Theory, Series A, a leading journal in combinatorics. EULER produced 10 proofs and 3 refutations, plus 45 scoped partial results. Two mechanisms held up under ablation: bridge-specific stress tests cut incorrect conclusions from 9 to 3, and bridge material combined with a target-native operation yielded a positive interaction of +4.2 resolved tasks that neither factor produced alone. Domain distance did not reliably predict success; executable operation gain and valid return did.

</details>


### [199] [OpenAgentFlow: Enabling System-Wide Safety Boundaries for Heterogeneous AI Agent Fleets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00015)
*Dongsheng Chen,Xiangyu Zhao,Xin Yao,Xuetao Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: OpenAgentFlow提出了一种控制面/动作面架构，将异构AI代理生成的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用等统一归一化为AgentEvent流，在动作提交边界强制执行安全策略，并通过控制面维护溯源、会话状态、审计记录和可更新的策略。在Android上的多项基准测试中达到94%以上的准确率和95%以上的攻击拦截率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型驱动的AI代理正从孤立助手演变为异构系统，多个代理在同一环境中运行，安全成为系统级的动作治理问题——需要在动作修改共享状态前决定是否提交。现有安全措施覆盖提示词、工具调用、GUI动作和代理局部行为，但存在执行碎片化、难以发现跨多步动作流程中的风险、审计性和策略演进支持不足等问题。

Method: 提出OpenAgentFlow控制面/动作面架构，在动作提交边界强制执行安全。核心方法：1) 将待执行的GUI动作、API调用、工具调用及LLM生成调用归一化为统一的AgentEvent流；2) 每个事件通过共享的执行前策略执行点(PEP)路由；3) 控制面维护provenance、会话状态、审计记录和可更新策略，新规则生效无需修改代理、提示词、模型或执行路径。

Result: 在Android平台实例化OpenAgentFlow。300例动作事件基准上达到94.0%准确率和95.3%攻击拦截率；30例动态策略套件中安装新规则后有27例匹配预期行为；100例Android模拟器套件中98例跟踪用例达到90.8%原始准确率和92.9%跟踪调整通过率。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明OpenAgentFlow为异构AI代理群提供了实用的共享强制执行边界，能够有效统一管理跨GUI、API和LLM规划场景的安全策略，同时支持策略的动态更新与审计追踪。

Abstract: AI agents powered by large language models are evolving from isolated assistants into heterogeneous systems in which multiple agents, planners, controllers, and execution backends operate over the same user or enterprise environment. In such settings, safety becomes a system-level action-governance problem: deciding whether concrete agent-generated actions should be committed before they modify shared state. Existing safeguards cover prompts, tool calls, GUI actions, and agent-local behavior, but often leave enforcement fragmented, obscure risks that emerge across multi-step action flows, and provide limited support for auditability and policy evolution.
  We present OpenAgentFlow, a control-plane/action-plane architecture that enforces safety at the action-commit boundary. It normalizes pending GUI actions, API calls, tool calls, and LLM-generated invocations into a unified AgentEvent stream, routes each event through a shared pre-execution Policy Enforcement Point, and maintains provenance, session state, audit records, and updatable policies in the control plane. This creates a shared governable action stream and allows new rules to take effect without modifying agents, prompts, models, or execution paths.
  We instantiate OpenAgentFlow on Android. On a 300-case action-event benchmark, it achieves 94.0% accuracy and a 95.3% attack block rate. On a 30-case dynamic-policy suite, it matches expected behavior in 27 cases after new rules are installed. Across 98 traced cases from a 100-case Android emulator suite, it achieves 90.8% raw accuracy and a 92.9% trace-adjusted pass rate across GUI, API, and LLM-planned cases. These results show that OpenAgentFlow provides a practical shared enforcement boundary for heterogeneous AI agent fleets.

</details>


### [200] [Control-Data Flow Separation: Stable Prompt Optimization in Multi-Agent LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00621)
*Wentao Zhang,Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Junaid Ahmad Bhatti,Utkarsh Soni,Yifan Nie,Eugene Wen,Yuntian Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出控制数据流分离原则，将多智能体LLM系统中的执行关键协议（如路由、格式、终止信号）表示为类型化的程序对象，而将任务相关内容保留为可优化的语言数据流，从而防止提示优化破坏系统执行协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统的提示词同时承担两种纠缠角色：生成任务相关内容和指定执行关键协议（如消息路由、输出格式、终止信号）。提示优化旨在改进内容生成，却可能无意中损坏协议，导致整个智能体流水线失败。

Method: 基于"执行协议通常是结构化的，而任务相关内容通常是非结构化语言"的关键观察，提出控制数据流分离：将执行关键控制表示为类型化、经验证的程序对象，任务相关语言保持为可优化的数据流用于智能体通信。

Result: 在合成推理、协作评审生成和保险评级工作流上，该框架实现了100%的最终协议有效性，同时持续提升任务性能。

Conclusion: 该设计使优化器能够改进多智能体行为，同时避免路由或格式化接口暴露在提示漂移的风险中，兼顾了提示优化的灵活性与系统执行的稳健性。

Abstract: Prompt optimization can improve multi-agent LLM systems, but the prompts being optimized often serve two entangled roles: generating task-relevant content and specifying execution-critical protocols, such as message routing, output formatting, and termination signals, on which the underlying code relies. As a result, a prompt edit intended to improve content generation can inadvertently corrupt the protocol and cause the entire agent pipeline to fail. Our key observation is that these two roles have different representations: execution protocols are typically structured, while task-relevant content is usually expressed in unstructured language. Based on this, we propose control-data flow separation, where execution-critical control is represented as typed, validated program objects, while task-relevant language remains the optimizable data flow for agent communication. This design allows optimizers to improve multi-agent behavior without exposing the routing or formatting interface to prompt drift. Across synthetic reasoning, collaborative review generation, and insurance rating workflows, our framework empirically achieves 100% eventual protocol validity while consistently improving task performance.

</details>


### [201] [SCAFFOLD: A Large-Scale Structured Dataset of Computer Science Research Figures with Diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought Reasoning Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00018)
*Ranjit Raut,Aarav Subedi,Sagun Rai,Sudan Jha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了SCAFFOLD，一个大规模计算机科学研究论文图表数据集，包含图像、标题、上下文、问答对及思维链推理，共有三个规模版本（157K/37K/12K），用于训练视觉语言模型理解学术图表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机科学论文大量依赖图表（架构图、流程图、管线示意图等），这些图表往往比文字承载更多信息。然而目前没有任何公开数据集将这类图与标题、上下文、问题和逐步推理配对，而这正是训练视觉语言模型理解图表所需的关键资源。

Method: 通过布局检测和PDF解析从arXiv计算机科学论文中提取数据，结合AI辅助问题生成步骤，构建了(image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought)元组形式的结构化数据集。数据集分为三个规模：SCAFFOLD-157K（3,058篇论文，29,887张图，157,387对）、SCAFFOLD-37K（36,797对）和SCAFFOLD-12K（12,000对）。

Result: 构建了三个规模的数据集，并利用SCAFFOLD-12K在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct模型上进行了基线实验。

Conclusion: SCAFFOLD填补了计算机科学学术图表理解数据集领域的空白，提供了包含图文配对及思维链推理的大规模资源，为训练视觉语言模型理解学术图表奠定了数据基础。

Abstract: Computer science papers rely heavily on diagrams: architecture drawings, system flowcharts, and pipeline schematics that often carry more information than the text around them. There is currently no public dataset that pairs this specific kind of figure with captions, context, questions, answers, and step-by-step reasoning, which is exactly what is needed to train a vision-language model to understand them. We present \textbf{SCAFFOLD}\footnote{https://github.com/theranjitraut/scaffold}, a large-scale structured dataset of computer science research figures with diagram QA and Chain-of-Thought reasoning traces. This dataset consists of (image, caption, context, question-answer, chain-of-thought) tuples from arXiv computer science papers prepared using layout detection and PDF parsing, with an AI-assisted question-generation step. The resulting large-sized SCAFFOLD-157K dataset spans 3,058 papers with 29,887 figures (157,387 pairs), a medium-sized SCAFFOLD-37K dataset (36,797 pairs), and a small-sized SCAFFOLD-12K dataset (12,000 pairs). We used SCAFFOLD-12K for baseline experiments on Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [202] [ChatDev 2.0: A No-Code Multi-Agent Platform for Developing Everything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00714)
*Yufan Dang,Shu Yao,Bowen Lai,Chenting Xu,Ruijie Shi,Wai-Shing Leung,Huatao Li,Chen Qian,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 介绍ChatDev 2.0：DevAll，一个无代码的LLM多智能体系统开发平台，通过声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎，在保持高表达力的同时实现易用性，无需编写代码即可构建、执行和检查异构MAS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM多智能体系统开发存在两难：基于代码的框架表达力强但工程负担重，无代码构建器虽然简化了编写过程但将智能体交互限制在作者定义的流程中，缺乏灵活性。

Method: DevAll采用声明式可执行图抽象与循环感知执行引擎相结合，支持异构智能体及动态循环交互的统一表示与执行；同时提供集成可视化界面，允许用户在零编码条件下完成MAS的编写、运行、监控与检查，包括人在回路步骤。

Result: 在三个代表性任务上，DevAll无需特定于任务的编排代码即可复现最先进的MAS，且性能具有竞争力，证明其作为通用LLM多智能体系统平台的有效性。

Conclusion: DevAll在表达力与易用性之间取得了有效平衡，可作为LLM-based MAS的通用开发平台。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) have shown strong potential for solving complex tasks, yet their development forces a tradeoff: code frameworks are expressive but engineering-intensive, while no-code builders simplify authoring but constrain agent interactions to author-defined workflows. We present ChatDev 2.0: DevAll (hereafter DevAll), a no-code platform for building, executing, and inspecting heterogeneous MAS that delivers both high expressiveness and ease of use. In terms of expressiveness, DevAll pairs a declarative executable graph abstraction with a cycle-aware execution engine, so that heterogeneous agents and dynamic and cyclic interactions can be represented and executed within a single framework. For ease of use, an integrated visual interface lets users author, run, monitor, and inspect MAS, including human-in-the-loop steps, entirely without writing code. Experiments demonstrate that DevAll reproduces state-of-the-art MAS across three representative tasks at competitive performance and without task-specific orchestration code, highlighting its effectiveness as a general-purpose platform for LLM-based MAS. DevAll is available at https://github.com/OpenBMB/ChatDev.

</details>


### [203] [UI-Venus-2 Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00028)
*Venus Team,Zhuohan Cai,Haoxing Chen,Jiaxuan Chen,Weizhi Chen,Changlong Gao,Zhangxuan Gu,Yuan Guo,Yusong Hu,Jianrong Jiang,Jianguo Li,Runze Li,Jinzhen Lin,Zhenyu Ma,Changhua Meng,Han Peng,Xinyu Qiu,Shuheng Shen,Zhongyi Shui,Weiqiang Wang,Ming Wen,Zhuoer Xu,Hang Yan,Kaiwen Yang,Ruilin Yao,Nanjun Yu,Zhengwen Zeng,Lianrui Zhang,Yunzhu Zhang,Zhe Zhao,Beitong Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: UI-Venus-2 是一个跨移动端、网页和桌面环境的通用 GUI 基础智能体，通过统一闭环推理-行动框架运行。论文在环境覆盖、任务构建和奖励验证三个维度上进行联合扩展，并集成安全感知机制，以推动从基准导向模型向可靠真实世界应用转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态 GUI 智能体虽在数字任务自动化中展现出潜力，但从基准导向模型过渡到可靠的真实世界应用仍面临三大挑战：环境覆盖有限、任务构建脆弱、奖励验证不可靠。

Method: 提出 UI-Venus-2，从三个关键维度联合扩展：(1) 环境维度，扩展至170多个多语言移动应用及原生桌面操作系统；(2) 任务维度，采用深度研究流水线进行基于功能的指令生成；(3) 验证维度，采用轨迹级和样本级评估器，结合视觉关键点与多模型投票以确保可靠强化学习信号，同时集成安全感知机制控制关键操作。

Result: 论文未提供具体量化实验结果，主要呈现其框架设计及三个维度联合扩展的策略，致力于构建具备高能力、高效率且开源的通用 GUI 基础智能体。

Conclusion: UI-Venus-2 通过环境、任务和验证三维度联合扩展以及安全机制集成，朝着更具泛化性、可验证性和自我反思能力的真实世界 GUI 智能体方向推进。

Abstract: Multimodal GUI agents have emerged as a promising paradigm for digital task automation, yet transitioning from benchmark-oriented models to dependable real-world applications remains challenging due to limited environment coverage, brittle task construction, and unreliable reward verification. In this work, we present UI-Venus-2, a general-purpose foundation GUI agent designed to operate across mobile, web, and desktop environments through a unified closed-loop reasoning-action framework. To bridge the gap toward practical deployment, we jointly scale three critical dimensions: (1) Environments, expanding coverage to more than 170 multilingual mobile apps and native desktop operating systems; (2) Tasks, employing a deep-research pipeline for function-grounded instruction generation; and (3) Verification, adopting trace-level and sample-level evaluators with visual keypoints and multi-model voting to ensure reliable RL signals for training. Furthermore, we integrate safety-aware mechanisms to ensure controlled execution of consequential actions. By offering a capable, efficient, and open-source foundation, UI-Venus-2 advances the field toward more generalizable, verifiable, and self-reflective agents for real-world applications.

</details>


### [204] [When Prediction Error Is Not Enough: Evaluating Nuisance-Function Prediction for Causal Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00071)
*Cong Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究部分线性模型中预测误差与因果估计性能之间的关系，发现预测误差不能直接反映因果估计器的质量，且联合误差度量与因果偏差关联微弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果推断中通常用预测误差来评估干扰函数估计器，但其与因果估计性能之间的关系在不同性能指标下可能不一致，需要系统研究。

Method: 在部分线性模型设定下进行蒙特卡洛模拟，比较OLS、GAM、XGBoost和DML-XGBoost四种方法，评估干扰函数预测误差、偏差、RMSE及95%置信区间覆盖率，并考察基于暴露和结局干扰函数估计误差绝对叉积的联合误差度量。

Result: XGBoost在非oracle方法中RMSE最低，DML-XGBoost的置信区间覆盖率更好；预测误差在不同方法间并不稳定地与因果偏差对应，点估计最优的方法未必覆盖率最好；联合误差度量与因果偏差仅弱相关。

Conclusion: 预测误差对评估干扰函数估计有用，但不应被视为因果估计器质量的直接度量。

Abstract: Prediction error is widely used to evaluate nuisance-function estimators in causal inference, but its relationship with causal estimator performance may differ across performance measures. We studied this question in a partially linear model using Monte Carlo simulations. We compared ordinary least squares (OLS), generalized additive models (GAMs), XGBoost, and Double Machine Learning with XGBoost (DML-XGBoost), evaluating nuisance-function prediction error, bias, RMSE, and 95\% confidence interval coverage. We also examined a simple joint-error measure based on the absolute cross-product of estimation errors from the exposure and outcome nuisance functions. Across the simulated settings, XGBoost had the lowest RMSE among the non-oracle methods, while DML-XGBoost generally provided better confidence interval coverage. Prediction error did not consistently track causal bias across methods and settings, and the method with the best point-estimation performance did not necessarily have the best confidence interval coverage. The joint-error measure was only weakly associated with causal bias and did not provide a useful standalone measure of causal performance. These results suggest that prediction error is useful for assessing nuisance-function estimation, but it should not be treated as a direct measure of the quality of the resulting causal estimator.

</details>


### [205] [MiNER: Fine-Tuned Biomedical Natural Language Processing for Malaria Disease Entity Recognition in Clinical Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.00073)
*V. S. Anoop,Devika N*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于微调BioBERT预训练生物医学语言模型的方法，用于从疟疾相关科学文献中提取生物医学信息（命名实体和关系），实验表明其性能优于其他编码和机器学习方法，并发布了人工标注数据集供研究使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 疟疾仍是全球重大健康负担，其文献量庞大且持续增长，从海量文献中提取关键生物医学信息（如分子机制、流行病学和治疗方法）具有挑战性，需要创新的信息提取方法。

Method: 首先选择和预处理大规模疟疾科学文献语料，对其进行临床意义标注；然后利用BioBERT预训练语言模型将文本编码为上下文感知表示，并通过领域特定标注和监督学习对模型进行微调，以提升生物医学命名实体提取能力。

Result: 通过与不同编码方法和机器学习算法的大量实验对比，该方法在精确率、召回率和准确率上均显著优于基线方法，且发布了人工标注的实体和关系提取数据集。

Conclusion: 微调后的BioBERT模型能有效提升疟疾领域生物医学信息提取的性能，公开的数据集有助于推动健康信息学研究者训练更先进的疟疾信息提取模型。

Abstract: Malaria remains a significant global health burden, necessitating continuous research efforts to understand its complex molecular mechanisms, epidemiology, and potential therapeutic interventions. Extracting essential biomedical information from the vast and constantly growing malaria literature is a challenging task that demands innovative approaches. Recently, pre-trained language models have revolutionized natural language processing tasks, demonstrating remarkable capabilities in various domains. This paper proposes a fine-tuned pre-trained biomedical language model for biomedical information extraction from scientific literature on malaria disease.
